# 背单词(Wordbook)Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** 交付「背单词」:选书(初中 / 高中 / 四级 / 六级 / 考研 / 托福 / SAT)、每日计划与进度、学新词(词卡 → 小测 → 进入 6 盒复习)、五种零成本题型、按句拼写(模式 A,确定性四结果判分)、段位点/阶段练习/成就联动、管理端词书页与设置,数据为提交进 git 的 KyleBing sentence 级 JSONL,服务端运行时按书懒加载索引读取;第二、三阶段在同一计划里分期列出。

**Architecture:** 服务端新增与 `rank`/`rooms` 平级的层 `app/wordbook/`(纯模块:`books`、`entry`、`textnorm`、`grading`、`quiz`、`cloze`、`plan`;数据:`index`(词典文件索引与 LRU)、`store`(数据库);编排:`service`、`explain`),路由在 `routers/wordbook.py` 与 `routers/admin_wordbook.py`,管理逻辑在 `admin/wordbook_admin.py`;学习状态复用 `vocabulary_items`(`source='wordbook:<book>'`)与 `services/vocabulary.next_review`,新表由迁移 `0012_wordbook` 建立;段位只读 `wordbook_events` 表(不导入 `wordbook`)。网页新增 `features/wordbook/`(核心页面)与其子目录 `features/wordbook/spell/`(按句拼写),入口在练习页大卡与「我的」一行,「致谢与许可」页在 `pages/`。

**Tech Stack:** FastAPI + SQLAlchemy 2 async + Alembic + pydantic v2 + httpx/respx + pytest-asyncio(Python 3.12,uv);React 18 + react-router 7 + Tailwind 3 + vitest + Testing Library + lucide-react;admin:Vite 6 + React 18 + vitest;Playwright(E2E)。不新增依赖。

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-wordbook-design.md`(视觉稿 `docs/superpowers/design/wordbook-v1/mockup.html`)。前置:`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-rank-and-rooms-design.md`、`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-admin-console-v2-design.md`、`docs/superpowers/design/redesign-v3/design-notes.md`。

## Global Constraints

- 分层由 `server/tests/test_architecture.py` 与 `web/src/architecture.test.ts` 强制,不放宽既有规则:新层 `wordbook` 只导入 `wordbook/core/models/providers/speech/services`;`rank`、`rooms`、`realtime` 不导入 `wordbook`;只有 `admin`、`routers`、`main` 可以导入 `wordbook`;网页其他目录只经 `~/features/wordbook` 的 `index.ts` 引用本 feature。
- 开关:一切新行为在 `WORDBOOK_ENABLED`(默认 `false`)之下;关闭时 `GET /api/wordbook/config` 返回 `{"enabled": false}`,其余 `/api/wordbook/*` 404 `{"error":"Wordbook is disabled"}`,网页入口不渲染。AI 讲解另在 `WORDBOOK_AI_ENABLED`(默认 `false`)之下;LLM 不可用(含 402)时返回 503 `{"error":"AI explanation unavailable"}`,其他功能不受影响。
- 数据:词典文件位于 `server/dictionaries/kylebing-english-vocabulary/{junior,senior,cet4,cet6,kaoyan,toefl,sat}.jsonl`,内容与 `SOURCE.json` 的 sha256 逐字节一致(上游提交 `c4c6c80879ff17d7025c28fb853a4991c8e6be6a`);只读打开,运行时不修改;不导入数据库。
- 默认值:每日新词 20(5–100)、每日复习上限 100(20–500)、AI 全站每日 200、每人每日 20、每人每日作答 3,000、一轮学新词 5(≤10)、一批复习 20、拼写一组 3–6(默认 5)句、一次最多 4 组。已掌握 = `box ≥ 4`。判分 credit:正确 100 / 小笔误 50 / 词形不对 50 / 错误 0,首字母提示减半;笔误上限:期望长度 ≤ 4 为 1,否则 2。
- 6 盒算法不变(`INTERVAL_DAYS = (1, 2, 4, 7, 15, 30)`);不引入 FSRS。一个用户一个词一行(应用层去重);词书行不计入 `max_vocab_items`;`GET /api/vocabulary` 默认 `scope=mine`。
- 迁移只新增 `server/alembic/versions/0012_wordbook.py`(`revision="0012"`,`down_revision="0011"`,以 F3 第 1 步 `alembic heads` 的输出为准);`tests/test_migrations.py::test_models_match_migrations` 保持绿色;SQLite batch + Postgres 安全路径与往返测试。
- 新设置登记进 `app/core/settings_registry.py`(`tests/test_settings_registry.py` 强制);按用户可覆盖的额度登记进 `app/core/quotas.py::QUOTA_KEYS`;新提示词注册进 `app/services/prompts.py::PROMPTS`。
- 日志、审计、异常信息里永不出现:用户拼写输入、AI 讲解正文、提示词正文、token、文件绝对路径;只记 id、书 id、词长、状态码。每个新增上游调用都 `metering.feature_scope("wordbook")`(路径前缀已自动设定)并有一条 `caplog` 断言。
- 错误响应 `{"error": "..."}`;网页把错误原文映射为 i18n key(`translateServerError` 的 extra 表 `WORDBOOK_SERVER_ERRORS`)。
- 网页文案只放各目录自己的 `i18n/{zh-CN,en}.json`(`features/wordbook/i18n/`、`features/wordbook/spell/i18n/`、`pages/i18n/`),中英齐全;页面测试断言中文文案(`beforeEach` 设 `localStorage["enjoy-web.settings"]={language:"zh-CN"}`,`afterEach` 删除并切回 `en`)。
- 视觉遵循 redesign-v3 一色一义:青 = 词汇/词书身份;蓝 = 主操作/当前空/你;琥珀 = 提示与「接近正确」;绿 = 正确;错误用中性 `ink-2`(不用红);红只用于危险/退出;触控 ≥ 44px;深色模式;一屏最多三层模糊;界面装饰不用 emoji。
- 每个新目录有 `CLAUDE.md`(What lives here / Public surface / Allowed dependencies / Test / Pitfalls)。
- 每个任务结束时:`cd server && uv run pytest -q <任务列出的测试> tests/test_architecture.py` 与(涉及网页)`yarn web:typecheck && cd web && yarn vitest run src/features/wordbook src/architecture.test.ts`、(涉及管理端)`cd admin && yarn vitest run src/features/wordbook` 绿色;车道合并前跑全量 `uv run pytest -q`、`yarn web:typecheck && yarn web:test && yarn web:build`。提交用 conventional 格式(`feat(server): …`、`feat(web): …`、`feat(admin): …`、`chore(server): …`、`docs: …`),一个任务一个逻辑提交,提交信息以 `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>` 结尾。
- 数字取自 spec;改数字先改 spec。

## 文件结构

```
server/
  dictionaries/CLAUDE.md
  dictionaries/kylebing-english-vocabulary/{CLAUDE.md,LICENSE,SOURCE.json,<7 个 id>.jsonl}
  scripts/wordbook_data.py                    download / verify(第二阶段加 derive)
  alembic/versions/0012_wordbook.py
  app/wordbook/
    CLAUDE.md  __init__.py
    books.py      书目录、启用列表解析、数据目录解析(纯)
    textnorm.py   分词、折叠、变形/原形、在句中定位目标(纯)
    entry.py      行解析、清洗、合并为 WordCard(纯)
    index.py      BookIndex 构建、DictionaryStore(懒加载、LRU、屏蔽句、重新加载)
    grading.py    编辑距离、逐字母对齐、四结果判分、SRS 档位(纯)
    quiz.py       题型选择、干扰项、题目构建与选择题判分(纯)
    cloze.py      拼写句组构建与按 sentence_id 复原(纯)
    plan.py       本地日、每日计划、洗牌排列、下一批新词(纯)
    store.py      选书、已学词、建行、事件、今日计数、作答上限(数据库)
    service.py    today / learn / review / answer / spell 编排
    explain.py    AI 讲解(缓存、额度、降级)
  app/routers/wordbook.py  app/routers/admin_wordbook.py  app/admin/wordbook_admin.py
  tests/fixtures/wordbook/mini.jsonl  tests/wordbook_helpers.py  tests/test_wordbook_*.py
web/src/features/wordbook/
  CLAUDE.md index.ts i18n/ wordbook-api.ts use-wordbook-config.ts wordbook-card.tsx home-page.tsx books-page.tsx
  word-card.tsx choice-question.tsx typing-question.tsx session-machine.ts learn-page.tsx review-page.tsx
  session-summary.tsx plan-ring.tsx letter-diff.tsx
  spell/ CLAUDE.md i18n/ cloze-engine.ts use-cloze-input.ts cloze-board.tsx spell-results.tsx explain-sheet.tsx spell-page.tsx prefs.ts
web/src/pages/credits-page.tsx (+ i18n)
admin/src/features/wordbook/{index.ts,api.ts,strings.ts,wordbook-page.tsx,wordbook-page.test.tsx}
NOTICE  .gitattributes(追加)  server/Dockerfile  server/Dockerfile.dockerignore
```

**合并顺序**(第一阶段):F1 → F2 → F3 → F4 →(D、S、R、A、WC、WS 并行开发,各自 rebase 到 F4)→ D1…D6 → S1…S6 → R1…R3 → A1 → A2 → WC1…WC5 → WS1…WS3 → Z1 → Z2。S 车道在 D2 合并后开始写数据层,S3/S4 在 D6 合并后 rebase 再合并;A1 在 D2 合并后合并;WS3 在 WC2 合并后 rebase。

**评审波**:每条车道合并前跑 `/code-review high`;S 车道另跑一次 `security-review`(输入校验、无状态判分、AI 提示词注入、额度与降级、日志卫生);WC/WS 合并前做视觉对照(视觉稿 vs 真实页面在浏览器窗格 375×812 浅色与深色截图);Z2 对整个分支跑 `security-review`,并按 spec §11.5 / §16 的真机清单(iOS Safari、安卓 Chrome + Gboard、搜狗输入法)验证拼写输入,未验证项在 `e2e-results.md` 标「未验证」。

## 本计划的裁定

- **P-W1** `dictionary(app)` 是 `DictionaryStore` 的唯一入口(`app.state.wordbook_dict`,惰性创建,数据目录取 `settings.resolved_wordbook_data_dir`);测试用 `tests/wordbook_helpers.enable_wordbook(app, data_dir)` 注入,不打补丁。
- **P-W2** 判分与题目全部无状态(spec R-W11);`/answers` 与 `/spell/submit` 只信任 `(book_id, key, qtype)` / `(key, sentence_id)`,答案从词卡重新算。
- **P-W3** `apply_review` 从 `app/routers/vocabulary.py` 移到 `app/services/vocabulary.py`(路由文件保留同名导入,`tests/test_vocabulary_api.py` 不改);wordbook 只调用 services 版。
- **P-W4** 网页 F4 放好最终的 API 类型与函数签名、`spell/spell-page.tsx` 桩(渲染空 `<div data-testid="wordbook-spell-page" />`);各车道替换实现,不改签名。改签名需要 Addendum。
- **P-W5** 服务端契约样例 `server/tests/fixtures/wordbook_contract.json` 由 S6 对真实应用录制(`CONTRACT_UPDATE=1` 重写);网页 `wordbook-api.test.ts` 逐键核对(WC5 打开该测试)。
- **P-W6** 时间一律注入(`now` 参数),测试不 sleep;随机一律用传入的 `random.Random`,种子由 `(user_id, key, reps)` 或请求决定。
- **P-W7** 第二、三阶段任务在第一阶段全部合并并在真机验证后才开始;它们的迁移号在开工时再跑 `alembic heads` 确认(下文写的是预期值)。
- **P-W8(写计划时的实跑)** 本计划第一阶段的服务端代码与测试(F1 清单与脚本、F2、F3、D1–D6、S1–S6、R1–R3 的服务端部分、A1)在写计划时被原样组装进一份**会话草稿目录里的** `server/` 副本(不在仓库内)并对真实词典文件实跑:新增测试全部通过;全量 `pytest` 2,742 通过,另 20 个失败均为环境原因(草稿路径过长导致房间语音临时文件 `FileNotFoundError`、副本里没有 `web/` 目录导致 `test_sentence_split` 读不到网页缩写表),在仓库内复跑这些用例通过。实跑发现并已写回本计划的修正:`test_settings_registry` 的 env-only 集合、`test_migrations` 的单 head 断言、`test_rank_achievements` 的目录与数量、`test_prompts(_api)` 的提示词列表、用户 id 非自增(测试从注册响应取 id)、拼写对齐与变形的若干边界。网页纯逻辑(`cloze-engine.ts`、`session-machine.ts`)用 Node 24 实跑通过;React 组件与页面测试未实跑。

## Foundation lane (F) · 第一阶段

### Task F1: 词典数据文件、清单、下载/校验脚本、Docker 与 NOTICE

**Files:**
- Create: `server/dictionaries/CLAUDE.md`、`server/dictionaries/kylebing-english-vocabulary/{CLAUDE.md,LICENSE,SOURCE.json}`、七个 `*.jsonl`(由脚本下载,不手工编辑)
- Create: `server/scripts/wordbook_data.py`、`NOTICE`
- Modify: `.gitattributes`、`server/Dockerfile`、`server/Dockerfile.dockerignore`、`server/CLAUDE.md`(What lives here 加 `dictionaries/`)
- Test: `server/tests/test_wordbook_data_files.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:`server/scripts/wordbook_data.py` 的 `load_manifest(directory: Path) -> dict`、`sha256_of(path: Path) -> str`、`verify(directory: Path) -> list[str]`、`download(directory: Path, manifest_dir: Path) -> None`;清单 `SOURCE.json` 的形状 `{"repo","commit","license","books":[{"id","file","upstream_path","bytes","sha256","entries","unique"}]}`;运行时目录 `/app/dictionaries/kylebing-english-vocabulary`(容器)/ `server/dictionaries/kylebing-english-vocabulary`(开发)

- [ ] **Step 1: 写清单与许可**

`server/dictionaries/CLAUDE.md`:

```markdown
# server/dictionaries/

Third-party dictionary data read at runtime by `app/wordbook/` (spec 2026-10-10-wordbook §3). Not Python; not imported.

## What lives here
- `kylebing-english-vocabulary/` – seven sentence-level JSONL word books + `SOURCE.json` manifest + upstream `LICENSE`.

## Rules
- Files are byte-identical to the pinned upstream commit; never edit them by hand. Update = change `SOURCE.json`,
  run `uv run python scripts/wordbook_data.py download`, commit alone (`chore(server): update wordbook data to <sha>`).
- `.gitattributes` marks `*.jsonl` here as `binary` (no EOL conversion: checksums must stay stable on Windows).
- Keep each file < 50 MB (GitHub soft limit); no Git LFS.
- Copied into the image by `server/Dockerfile` (`/app/dictionaries`); whitelisted in `server/Dockerfile.dockerignore`.

## Test
`uv run pytest -q tests/test_wordbook_data_files.py`
```

`kylebing-english-vocabulary/CLAUDE.md`:

```markdown
# kylebing-english-vocabulary/

KyleBing/english-vocabulary `full_line_jsonl/sentence/正序` (BSD-3-Clause, see `LICENSE` and repo-root `NOTICE`).

## What lives here
- `<id>.jsonl` for junior 初中, senior 高中, cet4 四级, cet6 六级, kaoyan 考研, toefl 托福, sat SAT: one JSON object per line
  `{"word","us","uk","translations":[{"translation","type"}],"phrases":[{"phrase","translation"}],"sentences":[{"sentence","translation"}]}`.
- `SOURCE.json` – upstream commit, upstream path, bytes, sha256, entries, unique words per book.

## Pitfalls
- Lines are not unique per word (each book concatenates 2–3 source lists); `app/wordbook/entry.py` merges them.
- Data quirks (dirty headwords, `(= …)` glosses, missing phonetics) are documented in the spec §2 and cleaned at read time.
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 写失败测试** — `server/tests/test_wordbook_data_files.py`:

```python
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

SERVER = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
ROOT = SERVER.parent
DATA = SERVER / "dictionaries" / "kylebing-english-vocabulary"


def _script():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("wordbook_data", SERVER / "scripts" / "wordbook_data.py")
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module


def test_manifest_lists_seven_books_with_ascii_files():
    manifest = json.loads((DATA / "SOURCE.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert manifest["commit"] == "c4c6c80879ff17d7025c28fb853a4991c8e6be6a"
    assert [b["id"] for b in manifest["books"]] == ["junior", "senior", "cet4", "cet6", "kaoyan", "toefl", "sat"]
    assert all(b["file"].isascii() and b["file"] == f"{b['id']}.jsonl" for b in manifest["books"])
    assert sum(b["bytes"] for b in manifest["books"]) == 45_661_272
    assert sum(b["entries"] for b in manifest["books"]) == 54_356
    assert max(b["bytes"] for b in manifest["books"]) < 50 * 1024 * 1024


def test_committed_files_match_manifest_checksums():
    assert _script().verify(DATA) == []


def test_verify_reports_missing_and_changed_files(tmp_path):
    script = _script()
    (tmp_path / "SOURCE.json").write_text(json.dumps({"repo": "x/y", "commit": "c", "books": [
        {"id": "a", "file": "a.jsonl", "bytes": 3, "sha256": "0" * 64},
        {"id": "b", "file": "b.jsonl", "bytes": 3, "sha256": script.sha256_of(DATA / "SOURCE.json")}]}), encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "b.jsonl").write_bytes(b"abc")
    problems = script.verify(tmp_path)
    assert problems[0] == "a.jsonl: missing"
    assert problems[1] == "b.jsonl: sha256 mismatch"


def test_license_notice_gitattributes_and_docker_wiring():
    assert "BSD 3-Clause License" in (DATA / "LICENSE").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "KyleBing/english-vocabulary" in (ROOT / "NOTICE").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    attrs = (ROOT / ".gitattributes").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "server/dictionaries/**/*.jsonl binary" in attrs
    assert "COPY server/dictionaries /app/dictionaries" in (SERVER / "Dockerfile").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "!server/dictionaries" in (SERVER / "Dockerfile.dockerignore").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()


def test_files_are_lf_without_bom():
    for path in DATA.glob("*.jsonl"):
        head = path.read_bytes()[:4096]
        assert not head.startswith(b"\xef\xbb\xbf") and b"\r\n" not in head, path.name
```

`server/scripts/wordbook_data.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""下载 / 校验背单词词典数据(spec 2026-10-10-wordbook §3)。只用标准库,不被 app 导入。

在 server/ 下运行:
  uv run python scripts/wordbook_data.py download [--dest DIR]   # 按 SOURCE.json 的固定提交下载并校验
  uv run python scripts/wordbook_data.py verify [--dest DIR]     # 只校验字节数与 sha256
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import sys
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

SERVER = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DEFAULT_DIR = SERVER / "dictionaries" / "kylebing-english-vocabulary"
RAW = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/{repo}/{commit}/{path}"


def load_manifest(directory: Path) -> dict:
    return json.loads((directory / "SOURCE.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def sha256_of(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1 << 20), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def verify(directory: Path) -> list[str]:
    problems: list[str] = []
    for book in load_manifest(directory)["books"]:
        path = directory / book["file"]
        if not path.is_file():
            problems.append(f"{book['file']}: missing")
        elif path.stat().st_size != book["bytes"]:
            problems.append(f"{book['file']}: size {path.stat().st_size} != {book['bytes']}")
        elif sha256_of(path) != book["sha256"]:
            problems.append(f"{book['file']}: sha256 mismatch")
    return problems


def download(directory: Path, manifest_dir: Path) -> None:
    manifest = load_manifest(manifest_dir)
    directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if not (directory / "SOURCE.json").is_file():  # --dest 指向别处时带上清单,verify 才能独立运行
        (directory / "SOURCE.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    for book in manifest["books"]:
        url = RAW.format(repo=manifest["repo"], commit=manifest["commit"], path=urllib.parse.quote(book["upstream_path"]))
        part = directory / f"{book['file']}.part"
        with urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=120) as response, part.open("wb") as out:
            while chunk := response.read(1 << 20):
                out.write(chunk)
        if sha256_of(part) != book["sha256"]:
            part.unlink()
            raise SystemExit(f"{book['file']}: sha256 mismatch after download")
        part.replace(directory / book["file"])
        print(f"{book['file']}: ok ({book['bytes']} bytes)")


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("command", choices=("download", "verify"))
    parser.add_argument("--dest", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_DIR)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.command == "download":
        download(args.dest, DEFAULT_DIR)
    problems = verify(args.dest)
    for line in problems:
        print(line, file=sys.stderr)
    return 1 if problems else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
def test_wordbook_is_a_leaf_layer(tmp_path):
    app = tmp_path / "app"
    for pkg in ("wordbook", "rank", "rooms", "admin", "routers", "services"):
        (app / pkg).mkdir(parents=True)
        (app / pkg / "__init__.py").write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    (app / "__init__.py").write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    (app / "wordbook" / "a.py").write_text("from app.rank import x\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (app / "wordbook" / "b.py").write_text("from app.routers import y\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (app / "rank" / "c.py").write_text("from app.wordbook import a\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (app / "services" / "d.py").write_text("from app.wordbook import a\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (app / "wordbook" / "ok.py").write_text("from app.services import d\nfrom app.models import User\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (app / "admin" / "ok.py").write_text("from app.wordbook import a\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (app / "routers" / "ok.py").write_text("from app.wordbook import a\n", encoding="utf-8")
    found = violations(app)
    for bad in ("app/wordbook/a.py:1", "app/wordbook/b.py:1", "app/rank/c.py:1", "app/services/d.py:1"):
        assert any(p.startswith(bad) for p in found), bad
    assert not any(p.startswith(("app/wordbook/ok.py", "app/admin/ok.py", "app/routers/ok.py")) for p in found)
```

```python
import json
from pathlib import Path

from app.wordbook.books import BOOK_IDS, BOOKS, book_by_id, default_data_dir, enabled_books

MANIFEST = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "dictionaries" / "kylebing-english-vocabulary" / "SOURCE.json"


def test_catalogue_matches_manifest():
    manifest = json.loads(MANIFEST.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert BOOK_IDS == tuple(b["id"] for b in manifest["books"])
    for spec, row in zip(BOOKS, manifest["books"]):
        assert (spec.file, spec.entries, spec.unique) == (row["file"], row["entries"], row["unique"])
    assert [b.level for b in BOOKS] == [1, 2, 3, 4, 4, 5, 6]
    assert book_by_id("cet4").title_zh == "四级" and book_by_id("nope") is None


def test_enabled_books_keeps_catalogue_order_and_drops_unknown():
    assert enabled_books("sat, junior ,bogus,,junior") == ("junior", "sat")
    assert enabled_books("") == ()


def test_default_data_dir_is_next_to_the_app_package():
    path = default_data_dir()
    assert path.name == "kylebing-english-vocabulary" and path.parent.name == "dictionaries"
    assert (path / "SOURCE.json").is_file()
```

`server/app/wordbook/books.py`:

```python
"""七本词书的目录与数据目录解析(spec §2.2、§3)。纯数据,无 I/O(default_data_dir 只算路径)。"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

SOURCE_DIR = "kylebing-english-vocabulary"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BookSpec:
    id: str
    title_zh: str
    title_en: str
    level: int  # 1 = easiest; only a proxy for difficulty (no frequency data)
    cefr: str
    file: str
    entries: int  # lines in the file
    unique: int  # distinct headwords before cleaning


BOOKS: tuple[BookSpec, ...] = (
    BookSpec("junior", "初中", "Junior high", 1, "A2", "junior.jsonl", 3223, 1987),
    BookSpec("senior", "高中", "Senior high", 2, "B1", "senior.jsonl", 6008, 3743),
    BookSpec("cet4", "四级", "CET-4", 3, "B1+", "cet4.jsonl", 7508, 4544),
    BookSpec("cet6", "六级", "CET-6", 4, "B2", "cet6.jsonl", 5651, 3991),
    BookSpec("kaoyan", "考研", "Postgraduate", 4, "B2", "kaoyan.jsonl", 9602, 5047),
    BookSpec("toefl", "托福", "TOEFL", 5, "C1", "toefl.jsonl", 13477, 10367),
    BookSpec("sat", "SAT", "SAT", 6, "C1", "sat.jsonl", 8887, 4464),
)
BOOK_IDS: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(b.id for b in BOOKS)
_BY_ID = {b.id: b for b in BOOKS}

# spec §2.6 / §2.11-2:读取时丢弃的词头(规范化键)
DENYLIST = frozenset({"undersize d", "sulfur disoxide", "aluminumal", "phosphorusp", "oxygen o", "iron fe", "rusia"})
POS_ABBREVIATIONS = frozenset({"a.", "adj.", "n.", "v.", "adv.", "vt.", "vi.", "prep."})


def book_by_id(book_id: str) -> BookSpec | None:
    return _BY_ID.get(book_id)


def enabled_books(csv: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    wanted = {part.strip() for part in csv.split(",") if part.strip()}
    return tuple(b for b in BOOK_IDS if b in wanted)


def default_data_dir() -> Path:
    """server/app/wordbook/books.py -> server/dictionaries/<SOURCE_DIR>;容器里 /app/app/... -> /app/dictionaries/..."""
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "dictionaries" / SOURCE_DIR
```

`server/tests/wordbook_helpers.py`:

```python
"""背单词测试夹具:迷你词书目录与开关注入(计划 P-W1)。"""
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

MINI = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "fixtures" / "wordbook" / "mini.jsonl"


def make_data_dir(tmp_path: Path, books: tuple[str, ...] = ("junior",)) -> Path:
    data = tmp_path / "dict"
    data.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    for book in books:
        shutil.copyfile(MINI, data / f"{book}.jsonl")
    return data


def enable_wordbook(app, data_dir: Path, **overrides) -> None:
    app.state.settings = app.state.settings.model_copy(
        update={"wordbook_enabled": True, "wordbook_data_dir": str(data_dir), **overrides})
    if hasattr(app.state, "wordbook_dict"):
        del app.state.wordbook_dict
```

`server/app/wordbook/CLAUDE.md`:

```markdown
# app/wordbook/

背单词 (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-wordbook-design.md`). Off unless `WORDBOOK_ENABLED`.

## What lives here
- `books.py` – the seven books, enabled-list parsing, data dir (pure)
- `textnorm.py` / `entry.py` – tokenizing, inflections, cleaning and merging raw lines into `WordCard` (pure)
- `index.py` – `BookIndex` + `DictionaryStore` (`dictionary(app)`): lazy per-book index, line reads, LRU, hidden sentences
- `grading.py` / `quiz.py` / `cloze.py` / `plan.py` – grading, question types and distractors, spelling sets, daily plan (pure)
- `store.py` – enrollments, learned words, events, counters (DB); `service.py` – endpoint orchestration; `explain.py` – AI "why"

## Allowed dependencies
`app.core`, `app.models`, `app.providers`, `app.speech`, `app.services`. Imported only by `app/admin`, `app/routers`,
`app/main.py` (enforced by `tests/test_architecture.py`). Rank reads `wordbook_events` through `app.models`, never this package.

## Tests
`uv run pytest -q tests/test_wordbook_*.py tests/test_rank_wordbook.py tests/test_admin_wordbook.py`
Fixture book: `tests/fixtures/wordbook/mini.jsonl`; helpers `tests/wordbook_helpers.py`.

## Pitfalls
- Dictionary files are read-only and byte-exact; never write next to them. Build indexes off the event loop (`asyncio.to_thread`).
- Grading is deterministic and stateless (P-W2); never call an LLM to grade.
- Never log user answers, explanations or prompt bodies; log book id, word length, status codes.
```

```python
WORDBOOK_TABLES = {"wordbook_enrollments", "wordbook_events", "wordbook_explanations", "wordbook_reports",
                   "wordbook_hidden_sentences"}


def test_0012_wordbook_tables_round_trip(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    cfg, db = _config(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
    command.upgrade(cfg, "0011")
    assert not WORDBOOK_TABLES & _tables(db)
    command.upgrade(cfg, "0012")
    assert WORDBOOK_TABLES <= _tables(db)
    con = sqlite3.connect(db)
    cols = {r[1] for r in con.execute("pragma table_info(wordbook_events)")}
    con.close()
    assert {"user_id", "book_id", "kind", "word", "item_id", "qtype", "outcome", "credit", "day", "created_at"} <= cols
    command.downgrade(cfg, "0011")
    assert not WORDBOOK_TABLES & _tables(db)
    command.upgrade(cfg, "head")
```

```python
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from app.core import metering
from app.core.quotas import QUOTA_KEYS
from app.core.settings_registry import SPECS
from app.models import User, WordbookEnrollment, WordbookEvent, WordbookExplanation

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 10, 9, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


async def test_enrollment_primary_key_and_event_row(app):
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as s:
        s.add(User(id=7, username="lily", password_hash="x"))
        s.add(WordbookEnrollment(user_id=7, book_id="cet4", status="active", order_mode="shuffle", seed=42, cursor=0,
                                 daily_new=20, review_cap=100, started_at=NOW, updated_at=NOW))
        s.add(WordbookEvent(user_id=7, book_id="cet4", kind="learn", word="absorb", item_id=None, qtype="meaning",
                            outcome="good", credit=100, day="2026-10-10", created_at=NOW))
        s.add(WordbookExplanation(word="absorb", body="…", model="m", prompt_version=None, created_at=NOW))
        await s.commit()
        s.add(WordbookEnrollment(user_id=7, book_id="cet4", status="paused", order_mode="book", seed=1, cursor=0,
                                 daily_new=20, review_cap=100, started_at=NOW, updated_at=NOW))
        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
            await s.commit()


def test_settings_registry_quota_and_metering(app):
    s = app.state.settings
    assert (s.wordbook_enabled, s.wordbook_ai_enabled, s.wordbook_daily_new, s.wordbook_review_cap) == (False, False, 20, 100)
    assert s.wordbook_books == "junior,senior,cet4,cet6,kaoyan,toefl,sat"
    assert s.wordbook_ai_available is False
    assert s.resolved_wordbook_data_dir.name == "kylebing-english-vocabulary"
    assert s.model_copy(update={"wordbook_enabled": True, "wordbook_ai_enabled": True}).wordbook_ai_available is True
    assert SPECS["wordbook_enabled"].group == "features" and SPECS["wordbook_ai_enabled"].danger
    assert SPECS["wordbook_data_dir"].env_only and SPECS["wordbook_books"].group == "wordbook"
    assert "wordbook_ai_daily_per_user" in QUOTA_KEYS
    assert metering.feature_for("/api/wordbook/answers") == "wordbook"


async def test_config_reports_disabled_and_other_routes_404(client, auth_headers):
    r = await client.get("/api/wordbook/config", headers=auth_headers)
    assert r.status_code == 200 and r.json() == {"enabled": False}
    assert (await client.get("/api/wordbook/config")).status_code == 401
```

```python
# ---- 背单词(设计文档 2026-10-10-wordbook §5)----

class WordbookEnrollment(Base):
    __tablename__ = "wordbook_enrollments"

    user_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), primary_key=True)
    book_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), primary_key=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8))  # active | paused
    order_mode: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8))  # shuffle | book
    seed: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    cursor: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    daily_new: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    review_cap: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer)
    started_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)


class WordbookEvent(Base):
    __tablename__ = "wordbook_events"
    __table_args__ = (
        Index("ix_wordbook_events_user_id_created_at", "user_id", "created_at"),
        Index("ix_wordbook_events_user_id_day", "user_id", "day"),
        Index("ix_wordbook_events_book_id_kind", "book_id", "kind"),
    )

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    user_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"))
    book_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(12))  # learn | review | practice | blank | spell_set
    word: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    item_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)
    qtype: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(12), nullable=True)
    outcome: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(12))
    credit: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    day: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(10))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)


class WordbookExplanation(Base):
    __tablename__ = "wordbook_explanations"

    word: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100), primary_key=True)
    body: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    model: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    prompt_version: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)


class WordbookReport(Base):
    __tablename__ = "wordbook_reports"
    __table_args__ = (Index("ix_wordbook_reports_status_created_at", "status", "created_at"),)

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    user_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True)
    book_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    word: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    sentence_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(12))  # sentence | passage (phase 2)
    reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))  # wrong | offensive | other
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(12), default="open")  # open | hidden | dismissed
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)


class WordbookHiddenSentence(Base):
    __tablename__ = "wordbook_hidden_sentences"

    sentence_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), primary_key=True)
    book_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16))
    hidden_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=utcnow)
```

```python
Revision ID: 0012
Revises: 0011
"""
import sqlalchemy as sa
from alembic import op

revision = "0012"
down_revision = "0011"
branch_labels = None
depends_on = None

TZ = sa.DateTime(timezone=True)


def upgrade() -> None:
    op.create_table(
        "wordbook_enrollments",
        sa.Column("user_id", sa.Integer, sa.ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("book_id", sa.String(16), primary_key=True),

        sa.Column("review_cap", sa.Integer, nullable=False),

    )
    op.create_table(
        "wordbook_events",
        sa.Column("id", sa.Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True),
        sa.Column("user_id", sa.Integer, sa.ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("book_id", sa.String(16), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("kind", sa.String(12), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("word", sa.String(100), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("item_id", sa.String(36), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("qtype", sa.String(12), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("outcome", sa.String(12), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("credit", sa.Integer, nullable=False),
        sa.Column("day", sa.String(10), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("created_at", TZ, nullable=False),
    )
    op.create_index("ix_wordbook_events_user_id_created_at", "wordbook_events", ["user_id", "created_at"])
    op.create_index("ix_wordbook_events_user_id_day", "wordbook_events", ["user_id", "day"])
    op.create_index("ix_wordbook_events_book_id_kind", "wordbook_events", ["book_id", "kind"])
    op.create_table(
        "wordbook_explanations",
        sa.Column("word", sa.String(100), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("body", sa.Text, nullable=False),
        sa.Column("model", sa.String(100), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("prompt_version", sa.Integer, nullable=True),
        sa.Column("created_at", TZ, nullable=False),
    )
    op.create_table(
        "wordbook_reports",
        sa.Column("id", sa.Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True),
        sa.Column("user_id", sa.Integer, sa.ForeignKey("users.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("book_id", sa.String(16), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("word", sa.String(100), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("sentence_id", sa.String(16), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("kind", sa.String(12), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("reason", sa.String(16), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("status", sa.String(12), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("created_at", TZ, nullable=False),
    )
    op.create_index("ix_wordbook_reports_status_created_at", "wordbook_reports", ["status", "created_at"])
    op.create_table(
        "wordbook_hidden_sentences",
        sa.Column("sentence_id", sa.String(16), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("book_id", sa.String(16), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("hidden_by", sa.String(64), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("created_at", TZ, nullable=False),
    )


def downgrade() -> None:
    op.drop_table("wordbook_hidden_sentences")
    op.drop_index("ix_wordbook_reports_status_created_at", table_name="wordbook_reports")
    op.drop_table("wordbook_reports")
    op.drop_table("wordbook_explanations")
    for name in ("ix_wordbook_events_book_id_kind", "ix_wordbook_events_user_id_day", "ix_wordbook_events_user_id_created_at"):
        op.drop_index(name, table_name="wordbook_events")
    op.drop_table("wordbook_events")
    op.drop_table("wordbook_enrollments")
```

属性区追加:

```python
    @property
    def resolved_wordbook_data_dir(self) -> Path:
        if self.wordbook_data_dir:
            return Path(self.wordbook_data_dir)
        return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "dictionaries" / "kylebing-english-vocabulary"

    @property
    def wordbook_ai_available(self) -> bool:
        return self.wordbook_enabled and self.wordbook_ai_enabled and bool(self.upstream_llm_base_url)

    @property
    def resolved_wordbook_ai_model(self) -> str:
        return self.wordbook_ai_model.strip() or self.default_llm_model
```

并在已有的 csv 校验处(若 `runtime_problems` 有 csv 校验钩子)给 `wordbook_books` 加校验:`field_validator("wordbook_books")` 拒绝不在 `("junior","senior","cet4","cet6","kaoyan","toefl","sat")` 的 id(core 不能导入 wordbook,常量在 config 里重复一份,`tests/test_wordbook_books.py` 补一条断言两处一致:`assert BOOK_IDS == config.WORDBOOK_BOOK_IDS`):

```python
WORDBOOK_BOOK_IDS = ("junior", "senior", "cet4", "cet6", "kaoyan", "toefl", "sat")  # = app.wordbook.books.BOOK_IDS

    @field_validator("wordbook_books")
    @classmethod
    def _wordbook_books(cls, value: str) -> str:
        unknown = {p.strip() for p in value.split(",") if p.strip()} - set(WORDBOOK_BOOK_IDS)
        if unknown:
            raise ValueError(f"unknown wordbook ids: {', '.join(sorted(unknown))}")
        return value
```

`server/app/services/vocabulary.py` 追加(从路由文件原样移过来,导入放文件顶部):

```python
from sqlalchemy import update
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.models import VocabularyItem


async def apply_review(
    session: AsyncSession, user_id: int, item_id: str, *, seen_box: int, seen_reps: int, grade: str, now: datetime,
) -> bool:
    """按读到的快照写入一次复习;乐观条件 reps == seen_reps(reps 只增不减),
    并发/连点的第二个请求在这里命中 0 行 → False(R18)。不提交。"""
    outcome = next_review(seen_box, grade, now)
    result = await session.execute(
        update(VocabularyItem)
        .where(VocabularyItem.id == item_id, VocabularyItem.user_id == user_id, VocabularyItem.reps == seen_reps)
        .values(
            box=outcome.box, due_at=outcome.due_at, reps=seen_reps + 1,
            lapses=VocabularyItem.lapses + (1 if outcome.lapsed else 0), last_reviewed_at=now,
        )
        .execution_options(synchronize_session=False)
    )
    return result.rowcount == 1
```

`server/app/routers/wordbook.py`(骨架,S 车道扩展):

```python
"""背单词接口(设计文档 2026-10-10-wordbook §7)。业务在 app/wordbook/。"""
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException

from app.core.config import Settings
from app.core.deps import current_user, get_settings
from app.models import User

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/wordbook")
DISABLED = "Wordbook is disabled"


def require_wordbook(settings: Settings = Depends(get_settings)) -> Settings:
    if not settings.wordbook_enabled:
        raise HTTPException(404, DISABLED)
    return settings


@router.get("/config")
async def wordbook_config(_: User = Depends(current_user), settings: Settings = Depends(get_settings)):
    if not settings.wordbook_enabled:
        return {"enabled": False}
    from app.wordbook.service import config_out  # S2 实现;F3 期间返回最小形状
    return config_out(settings)
```

F3 阶段 `app/wordbook/service.py` 只放:

```python
"""背单词编排(S 车道实现;F3 先放 config_out)。"""
from app.wordbook.books import BOOKS, enabled_books


def config_out(settings) -> dict:
    data_dir = settings.resolved_wordbook_data_dir
    enabled = enabled_books(settings.wordbook_books)
    return {
        "enabled": True,
        "ai_available": settings.wordbook_ai_available,
        "defaults": {"daily_new": settings.wordbook_daily_new, "review_cap": settings.wordbook_review_cap},
        "books": [{"id": b.id, "title_zh": b.title_zh, "title_en": b.title_en, "level": b.level, "words": b.unique,
                   "available": (data_dir / b.file).is_file()} for b in BOOKS if b.id in enabled],
    }
```

```ts
export type BookId = "junior" | "senior" | "cet4" | "cet6" | "kaoyan" | "toefl" | "sat";
export interface WordbookBook { id: BookId; titleZh: string; titleEn: string; level: number; words: number; available: boolean }
export type WordbookConfig =
  | { enabled: false }
  | { enabled: true; aiAvailable: boolean; defaults: { dailyNew: number; reviewCap: number }; books: WordbookBook[] };
export interface BookProgress extends Omit<WordbookBook, "available"> { learned: number; mastered: number; enrolled: boolean; active: boolean }
export type OrderMode = "shuffle" | "book";
export interface Enrollment { bookId: BookId; status: "active" | "paused"; orderMode: OrderMode; dailyNew: number; reviewCap: number; startedAt: string }
export interface DayPlan { newTarget: number; newDone: number; newLeft: number; reviewDue: number; reviewDone: number; reviewLeft: number; backlogGuard: boolean }
export interface Progress { words: number; learned: number; mastered: number; remaining: number }
export interface Today { enrollment: Enrollment | null; book: WordbookBook | null; plan: DayPlan | null; progress: Progress | null; tomorrowDue: number }
export interface Sense { pos: string; text: string }
export interface Example { id: string; en: string; zh: string }
export interface Card { key: string; word: string; us: string; uk: string; senses: Sense[]; phrases: { en: string; zh: string }[]; examples: Example[] }
export type QType = "meaning" | "word" | "cloze" | "dictation" | "spell";
export interface Question { key: string; qtype: QType; prompt: { word?: string; us?: string; meaning?: string; sentence?: string; zh?: string }; options: string[] | null; length: number | null }
export type Outcome = "correct" | "typo" | "inflection" | "wrong";
export type Grade = "good" | "hard" | "forgot";
export interface DiffOp { op: "eq" | "sub" | "ins" | "del"; a: string; b: string }
export interface ItemState { box: number; dueAt: string }
export interface AnswerInput { bookId: BookId; key: string; mode: "learn" | "review"; qtype: QType; choice?: string; typed?: string; hinted?: boolean; known?: boolean }
export interface AnswerResult { correct: boolean; outcome: Outcome | "known"; grade: Grade; expected: string; diff: DiffOp[] | null; item: ItemState | null; applied: boolean }
export type Segment = { t: "text"; v: string } | { t: "blank"; i: number; len: number };
export interface ClozeLine { key: string; sentenceId: string; segments: Segment[]; zh: string }
export interface ClozeSet { setId: string; lines: ClozeLine[] }
export interface BlankResult { i: number; outcome: Outcome; credit: number; typed: string; expected: string; diff: DiffOp[] }
export interface SpellWordResult { key: string; outcome: Outcome; credit: number; blanks: BlankResult[]; card: Card; item: ItemState | null }
export interface SpellResult { credit: number; counted: boolean; words: SpellWordResult[] }
export interface SpellAnswerLine { key: string; sentenceId: string; answers: { typed: string; hinted: boolean }[] }

export const WORDBOOK_SERVER_ERRORS: Record<string, string>;
export const localOffsetMinutes: () => number;
export function fetchWordbookConfig(api: ApiClient, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<WordbookConfig>;
export function fetchBooks(api: ApiClient, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<BookProgress[]>;
export function saveEnrollment(api: ApiClient, input: { bookId: BookId; dailyNew?: number; reviewCap?: number; orderMode?: OrderMode }): Promise<Enrollment>;
export function fetchToday(api: ApiClient, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Today>;
export function learnBatch(api: ApiClient, size?: number): Promise<{ bookId: BookId; cards: Card[]; quiz: Question[] }>;
export function reviewBatch(api: ApiClient, size?: number): Promise<{ bookId: BookId; questions: Question[]; cards: Record<string, Card> }>;
export function submitAnswer(api: ApiClient, input: AnswerInput): Promise<AnswerResult>;
export function spellSets(api: ApiClient, opts?: { count?: number; size?: number }): Promise<{ bookId: BookId; sets: ClozeSet[] }>;
export function submitSpell(api: ApiClient, input: { bookId: BookId; setId: string; lines: SpellAnswerLine[] }): Promise<SpellResult>;
export function fetchCard(api: ApiClient, bookId: BookId, key: string, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Card>;
export function explainWord(api: ApiClient, key: string): Promise<{ key: string; text: string; cached: boolean }>;
export function reportSentence(api: ApiClient, input: { bookId: BookId; key: string; sentenceId: string; reason: "wrong" | "offensive" | "other" }): Promise<{ id: number }>;
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ApiClient } from "~/api/client";
import { createFetchMock, json } from "~/test/fetch-mock";
import { fetchToday, submitAnswer, submitSpell, WORDBOOK_SERVER_ERRORS } from "./wordbook-api";

const api = (routes: Parameters<typeof createFetchMock>[0]) => {
  const mock = createFetchMock(routes);
  return { mock, api: new ApiClient({ getToken: () => "t", fetchImpl: mock.fetch }) };
};

describe("wordbook api", () => {
  it("sends the local offset and camelCases today", async () => {
    const { mock, api: client } = api({
      "GET /api/wordbook/today": () => json({ enrollment: null, book: null, plan: null, progress: null, tomorrow_due: 0 }),
    });
    expect(await fetchToday(client)).toEqual({ enrollment: null, book: null, plan: null, progress: null, tomorrowDue: 0 });
    expect(mock.calls[0].url.searchParams.get("tz_offset_minutes")).toMatch(/^-?\d+$/);
  });

  it("snake_cases answers and adds tz_offset_minutes", async () => {
    const { mock, api: client } = api({
      "POST /api/wordbook/answers": () => json({ correct: true, outcome: "correct", grade: "good", expected: "talk", diff: null, item: null, applied: false }),
    });
    await submitAnswer(client, { bookId: "cet4", key: "talk", mode: "review", qtype: "spell", typed: "talk", hinted: false });
    const body = JSON.parse(String(mock.calls[0].body));
    expect(body).toMatchObject({ book_id: "cet4", key: "talk", mode: "review", qtype: "spell", typed: "talk", hinted: false });
    expect(typeof body.tz_offset_minutes).toBe("number");
  });

  it("keeps sentence ids and blank answers in order", async () => {
    const { mock, api: client } = api({ "POST /api/wordbook/spell/submit": () => json({ credit: 100, counted: true, words: [] }) });
    await submitSpell(client, { bookId: "junior", setId: "s1", lines: [{ key: "used to", sentenceId: "abcd", answers: [{ typed: "used", hinted: false }, { typed: "to", hinted: true }] }] });
    expect(JSON.parse(String(mock.calls[0].body)).lines[0]).toEqual({ key: "used to", sentence_id: "abcd", answers: [{ typed: "used", hinted: false }, { typed: "to", hinted: true }] });
  });

  it("maps every server error to an i18n key", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(WORDBOOK_SERVER_ERRORS).sort()).toEqual([
      "AI explanation unavailable", "Book not found", "Daily answer limit reached", "Daily report limit reached",
      "No active book", "Sentence changed", "Word not found", "Wordbook data unavailable", "Wordbook is disabled",
    ]);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ApiClient } from "~/api/client";
import { createFetchMock, json } from "~/test/fetch-mock";
import { renderWithProviders } from "~/test/render";
import { useWordbookEnabled } from "./use-wordbook-config";

function Probe() {
  return <p data-testid="wb">{String(useWordbookEnabled())}</p>;
}

describe("useWordbookEnabled", () => {
  it("is false until the config says enabled, and caches per client", async () => {
    let calls = 0;
    const mock = createFetchMock({
      "GET /api/wordbook/config": () => { calls += 1; return json({ enabled: true, ai_available: false, defaults: { daily_new: 20, review_cap: 100 }, books: [] }); },
    });
    const api = new ApiClient({ getToken: () => "t", fetchImpl: mock.fetch });
    const first = renderWithProviders(<Probe />, { api });
    expect(screen.getByTestId("wb").textContent).toBe("false");
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId("wb").textContent).toBe("true"));
    first.unmount();
    renderWithProviders(<Probe />, { api });
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId("wb").textContent).toBe("true"));
    expect(calls).toBe(1);
  });

  it("treats a disabled server as off", async () => {
    const mock = createFetchMock({ "GET /api/wordbook/config": () => json({ enabled: false }) });
    renderWithProviders(<Probe />, { api: new ApiClient({ getToken: () => "t", fetchImpl: mock.fetch }) });
    await waitFor(() => expect(mock.calls).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(screen.getByTestId("wb").textContent).toBe("false");
  });
});
```

`web/src/features/wordbook/wordbook-api.ts`:上面「Produces」的全部类型原样写入,函数实现:

```ts
import type { ApiClient } from "~/api/client";

export const localOffsetMinutes = () => -new Date().getTimezoneOffset();

export const WORDBOOK_SERVER_ERRORS: Record<string, string> = {
  "Wordbook is disabled": "web.wordbook.errors.disabled",
  "Book not found": "web.wordbook.errors.bookNotFound",
  "Wordbook data unavailable": "web.wordbook.errors.dataUnavailable",
  "No active book": "web.wordbook.errors.noActiveBook",
  "Daily answer limit reached": "web.wordbook.errors.answerLimit",
  "Daily report limit reached": "web.wordbook.errors.reportLimit",
  "Sentence changed": "web.wordbook.errors.sentenceChanged",
  "AI explanation unavailable": "web.wordbook.errors.aiUnavailable",
  "Word not found": "web.wordbook.errors.wordNotFound",
};

const tz = () => ({ tz_offset_minutes: localOffsetMinutes() });

export const fetchWordbookConfig = (api: ApiClient, signal?: AbortSignal) =>
  api.request<WordbookConfig>("GET", "/api/wordbook/config", { signal });
export const fetchBooks = async (api: ApiClient, signal?: AbortSignal) =>
  (await api.request<{ books: BookProgress[] }>("GET", "/api/wordbook/books", { signal })).books;
export const saveEnrollment = (api: ApiClient, input: { bookId: BookId; dailyNew?: number; reviewCap?: number; orderMode?: OrderMode }) =>
  api.request<Enrollment>("PUT", "/api/wordbook/enrollment", { json: input });
export const fetchToday = (api: ApiClient, signal?: AbortSignal) =>
  api.request<Today>("GET", "/api/wordbook/today", { query: tz(), signal });
export const learnBatch = (api: ApiClient, size = 5) =>
  api.request<{ bookId: BookId; cards: Card[]; quiz: Question[] }>("POST", "/api/wordbook/learn/batch", { json: { size, ...tz() } });
export const reviewBatch = (api: ApiClient, size = 20) =>
  api.request<{ bookId: BookId; questions: Question[]; cards: Record<string, Card> }>("POST", "/api/wordbook/review/batch", { json: { size, ...tz() } });
export const submitAnswer = (api: ApiClient, input: AnswerInput) =>
  api.request<AnswerResult>("POST", "/api/wordbook/answers", { json: { ...input, ...tz() } });
export const spellSets = (api: ApiClient, opts: { count?: number; size?: number } = {}) =>
  api.request<{ bookId: BookId; sets: ClozeSet[] }>("POST", "/api/wordbook/spell/sets", { json: { count: opts.count ?? 2, size: opts.size ?? 5, ...tz() } });
export const submitSpell = (api: ApiClient, input: { bookId: BookId; setId: string; lines: SpellAnswerLine[] }) =>
  api.request<SpellResult>("POST", "/api/wordbook/spell/submit", { json: { ...input, ...tz() } });
export const fetchCard = (api: ApiClient, bookId: BookId, key: string, signal?: AbortSignal) =>
  api.request<Card>("GET", `/api/wordbook/cards/${encodeURIComponent(bookId)}/${encodeURIComponent(key)}`, { signal });
export const explainWord = (api: ApiClient, key: string) =>
  api.request<{ key: string; text: string; cached: boolean }>("POST", "/api/wordbook/explain", { json: { key } });
export const reportSentence = (api: ApiClient, input: { bookId: BookId; key: string; sentenceId: string; reason: "wrong" | "offensive" | "other" }) =>
  api.request<{ id: number }>("POST", "/api/wordbook/reports", { json: input });
```

注意:`decamelizeKeys` 会把 `sentenceId` 转成 `sentence_id`、`bookId` → `book_id`;答案里的 `typed` 文本是**值**不是键,不受影响。若 `api.request` 的泛型签名与此不同,按 `web/src/api/client.ts` 的实际签名调整调用写法,不改导出名。

`web/src/features/wordbook/use-wordbook-config.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { ApiClient } from "~/api/client";
import { useAuth } from "~/auth/auth-context";
import { fetchWordbookConfig, type WordbookConfig } from "./wordbook-api";

const cache = new WeakMap<ApiClient, Promise<WordbookConfig | null>>();

/** 背单词配置;拿到前为 null。每个 ApiClient 只缓存一次成功结果;失败视为关闭,下次挂载重试(同 useRoomsEnabled)。 */
export function useWordbookConfig(): WordbookConfig | null {
  const { api } = useAuth();
  const [config, setConfig] = useState<WordbookConfig | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    let pending = cache.get(api);
    if (!pending) {
      pending = fetchWordbookConfig(api).catch(() => {
        cache.delete(api);
        return null;
      });
      cache.set(api, pending);
    }
    void pending.then((value) => { if (alive) setConfig(value); });
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [api]);
  return config;
}

export function useWordbookEnabled(): boolean {
  return useWordbookConfig()?.enabled === true;
}
```

`web/src/features/wordbook/CLAUDE.md`:

```markdown
# web/src/features/wordbook/

背单词 (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-wordbook-design.md` §11): book picker, daily plan, learn, review, summary;
the spelling-in-context mode lives in `spell/`. Hidden unless `/api/wordbook/config` says enabled.

## What lives here
- `wordbook-api.ts` (types + calls, `WORDBOOK_SERVER_ERRORS`), `use-wordbook-config.ts`.
- Pages: `home-page.tsx` (/wordbook), `books-page.tsx` (/wordbook/books), `learn-page.tsx`, `review-page.tsx`; entry `wordbook-card.tsx`.
- `session-machine.ts` (learn/review queue), `word-card.tsx`, `choice-question.tsx`, `typing-question.tsx`, `letter-diff.tsx`, `plan-ring.tsx`, `session-summary.tsx`.
- `spell/` – see `spell/CLAUDE.md`. `i18n/{en,zh-CN}.json`.

## Public surface
`index.ts`: pages, `WordbookCard`, `useWordbookEnabled`, `useWordbookConfig`. Other directories import only `~/features/wordbook`.

## Allowed dependencies
`lib/`, `api/`, `audio/`, `components/`, `layout/`, `auth/`, `settings/`, `@renderer/*`, other features via `index.ts`
(TTS: `useTtsPlayer` from `~/features/chat`, called inside components only — never at module top level).

## Test
`cd web && yarn vitest run src/features/wordbook`

## Pitfalls
- Teal is the wordbook identity colour; blue only for primary actions and the active blank; wrong answers are neutral, never red.
- Grading happens on the server; never grade or reveal answers client-side beyond what the response returns.
```

`spell/CLAUDE.md`:

```markdown
# web/src/features/wordbook/spell/

按句拼写 · 模式 A (spec §6.6, §11.5): target-word blanks in dataset sentences, one hidden input, deterministic server grading.

## What lives here
- `cloze-engine.ts` (pure reducer), `use-cloze-input.ts` (hidden input: sentinel, space, backspace, IME composition),
  `cloze-board.tsx`, `spell-results.tsx`, `explain-sheet.tsx`, `spell-page.tsx`, `prefs.ts` (show letter count), `i18n/`.

## Public surface
Only `WordbookSpellPage`, re-exported by `../index.ts`.

## Allowed dependencies
Same as `../CLAUDE.md`; parent-feature modules via relative `../x` imports.

## Test
`cd web && yarn vitest run src/features/wordbook/spell`

## Pitfalls
- Android Gboard sends keyCode 229: read the `input` event's value diff, never rely on `keydown` for letters/space/backspace.
- Focus the hidden input inside the tap handler (iOS only opens the keyboard in a user gesture).
- No per-keystroke aria-live; announce once after submit.
```

## Lane dictionary (D) · 第一阶段

D 车道全是纯函数或只读文件 I/O;测试不需要数据库。迷你词书 `tests/fixtures/wordbook/mini.jsonl` 的 17 行合并后有 14 个键(`adj.` 与 `undersize d` 被丢弃,`talk`/`Talk` 合并)。

### Task D1: `textnorm.py` — 分词、折叠、变形与原形、在句中定位目标

**Files:**
- Create: `server/app/wordbook/textnorm.py`
- Test: `server/tests/test_wordbook_textnorm.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:`Token(text: str, start: int, end: int)`、`fold(text: str) -> str`、`tokenize(text: str) -> list[Token]`、`inflections(lemma: str) -> frozenset[str]`(含原形)、`lemma_candidates(form: str) -> frozenset[str]`(含自身)、`same_lemma(a: str, b: str) -> bool`、`find_form(sentence: str, key: str) -> tuple[int, int, str] | None`(原形优先,其次变形;多词键要求前面各词原形相同、最后一词可变形)、`IRREGULAR: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]]`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

```python
import pytest

from app.wordbook.textnorm import fold, find_form, inflections, lemma_candidates, same_lemma, tokenize


def test_fold_and_tokenize():
    assert fold("  Don’t ") == "don't"
    assert [t.text for t in tokenize("Sue and Bob still aren’t talking (= no) , oat-milk!")] == [
        "Sue", "and", "Bob", "still", "aren’t", "talking", "no", "oat-milk"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("lemma,forms", [
    ("talk", {"talks", "talked", "talking"}),
    ("carry", {"carries", "carried", "carrying"}),
    ("stop", {"stops", "stopped", "stopping"}),
    ("make", {"makes", "made", "making"}),
    ("go", {"goes", "went", "gone", "going"}),
    ("child", {"children"}),
    ("box", {"boxes"}),
    ("happy", {"happier", "happiest", "happily"}),
])
def test_inflections(lemma, forms):
    assert forms <= inflections(lemma) and lemma in inflections(lemma)


def test_visit_is_not_double_consonant():
    assert "visited" in inflections("visit") and "visitted" not in inflections("visit")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("form,lemma", [("talking", "talk"), ("carried", "carry"), ("stopped", "stop"),
                                        ("went", "go"), ("children", "child"), ("making", "make"), ("boxes", "box")])
def test_lemma_candidates(form, lemma):
    assert lemma in lemma_candidates(form)
    assert same_lemma(form, lemma) and same_lemma(lemma, form)


def test_same_lemma_rejects_unrelated_words():
    assert not same_lemma("talk", "walk") and not same_lemma("bed", "be")


def test_find_form_prefers_exact_then_inflected_and_handles_phrases():
    s = "I could hear Sarah and Andy talking in the next room."
    assert find_form(s, "talk") == (28, 35, "talking")
    assert find_form("After a long talk, we talked.", "talk") == (13, 17, "talk")
    assert find_form("She went home early.", "go") == (4, 8, "went")
    assert find_form("I used to live by the sea.", "used to") == (2, 9, "used to")
    assert find_form("See you on Monday morning.", "monday") == (11, 17, "Monday")
    assert find_form("Nothing here.", "talk") is None
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 跑测试确认失败** — Run: `cd server && uv run pytest -q tests/test_wordbook_textnorm.py` → FAIL(模块不存在)

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `server/app/wordbook/textnorm.py`:

```python
"""分词、折叠、规则与不规则变形、反求原形、在例句中定位目标词(spec §6.6)。纯函数。"""
import re
import unicodedata
from dataclasses import dataclass

WORD_RE = re.compile(r"[A-Za-zÀ-ÿ]+(?:['’\-][A-Za-zÀ-ÿ]+)*")
_FOLD = str.maketrans({"’": "'", "‘": "'", "ʼ": "'"})
_VOWELS = "aeiou"

# 常见不规则动词三形与不规则复数(lemma -> 变形);只用于判「词形不对」与定位例句中的目标词
_IRREGULAR_TEXT = """
be am is are was were been being|go goes went gone going|have has had having|do does did done doing
begin began begun beginning|break broke broken|bring brought|build built|buy bought|catch caught|choose chose chosen
come came|cost|cut cutting|draw drew drawn|drink drank drunk|drive drove driven|eat ate eaten|fall fell fallen
feel felt|fight fought|find found|fly flew flown flies|forget forgot forgotten forgetting|get got gotten getting
give gave given|grow grew grown|hang hung|hear heard|hide hid hidden|hit hitting|hold held|hurt|keep kept
know knew known|lay laid|lead led|leave left|lend lent|let letting|lie lay lain lying|lose lost|make made
mean meant|meet met|pay paid|put putting|read|ride rode ridden|ring rang rung|rise rose risen|run ran running
say said|see saw seen|seek sought|sell sold|send sent|set setting|shake shook shaken|shine shone|shoot shot
show showed shown|shut shutting|sing sang sung|sink sank sunk|sit sat sitting|sleep slept|speak spoke spoken
spend spent|stand stood|steal stole stolen|stick stuck|strike struck|swim swam swum swimming|take took taken
teach taught|tear tore torn|tell told|think thought|throw threw thrown|understand understood|wake woke woken
wear wore worn|win won winning|write wrote written|bear bore born borne|bend bent|bind bound|bite bit bitten
blow blew blown|burn burnt|deal dealt|dig dug digging|feed fed|forbid forbade forbidden|freeze froze frozen
light lit|overcome overcame|seek sought|shrink shrank shrunk|slide slid|spread|spring sprang sprung|swear swore sworn
sweep swept|swing swung|undergo underwent undergone|withdraw withdrew withdrawn|weep wept|wind wound
child children|man men|woman women|person people|foot feet|tooth teeth|mouse mice|goose geese|ox oxen
life lives|wife wives|knife knives|leaf leaves|half halves|wolf wolves|shelf shelves|thief thieves
crisis crises|analysis analyses|basis bases|thesis theses|phenomenon phenomena|criterion criteria
datum data|medium media|bacterium bacteria|curriculum curricula|good better best|bad worse worst|far farther further
"""
IRREGULAR: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {}
for _group in _IRREGULAR_TEXT.replace("\n", "|").split("|"):
    _words = _group.split()
    if _words:
        IRREGULAR[_words[0]] = tuple(_words[1:])
# 这些词的规则变形会撞上真实单词(be → bed / best),只用不规则表
_NO_REGULAR = frozenset({"be"})
_REVERSE: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
for _lemma, _forms in IRREGULAR.items():
    for _form in _forms:
        _REVERSE.setdefault(_form, set()).add(_lemma)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Token:
    text: str
    start: int
    end: int


def fold(text: str) -> str:
    return unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text).translate(_FOLD).strip().lower()


def tokenize(text: str) -> list[Token]:
    return [Token(m.group(0), m.start(), m.end()) for m in WORD_RE.finditer(text)]


def _one_syllable(word: str) -> bool:
    return len(re.findall(r"[aeiou]+", word)) == 1


def _double_final(word: str) -> bool:
    return (len(word) >= 3 and word[-1] not in _VOWELS + "wxy" and word[-2] in _VOWELS
            and word[-3] not in _VOWELS and _one_syllable(word))


def _regular(w: str) -> set[str]:
    out = {w}
    if re.search(r"(s|x|z|ch|sh|o)$", w):
        out.add(w + "es")
    if re.search(r"[^aeiou]y$", w):
        stem = w[:-1]
        out |= {stem + "ies", stem + "ied", stem + "ier", stem + "iest", stem + "ily", w + "ing"}
    else:
        out.add(w + "s")
        if w.endswith("e"):
            out |= {w + "d", w[:-1] + "ing", w + "r", w + "st", w[:-1] + "y" if w.endswith("le") else w + "ly"}
        else:
            out |= {w + "ed", w + "ing", w + "er", w + "est", w + "ly"}
            if _double_final(w):
                out |= {w + w[-1] + "ed", w + w[-1] + "ing", w + w[-1] + "er", w + w[-1] + "est"}
    if w.endswith("ic"):
        out.add(w + "ally")
    if w.endswith(("f", "fe")):
        out.add(re.sub(r"fe?$", "ves", w))
    return out


def inflections(lemma: str) -> frozenset[str]:
    w = fold(lemma)
    regular = {w} if w in _NO_REGULAR else _regular(w)
    return frozenset(regular | set(IRREGULAR.get(w, ())))


def lemma_candidates(form: str) -> frozenset[str]:
    f = fold(form)
    out = {f} | _REVERSE.get(f, set())
    rules = (("ies", "y"), ("ied", "y"), ("ier", "y"), ("iest", "y"), ("ily", "y"), ("ves", "f"), ("ves", "fe"),
             ("es", ""), ("s", ""), ("ed", ""), ("ed", "e"), ("d", ""), ("ing", ""), ("ing", "e"),
             ("er", ""), ("er", "e"), ("est", ""), ("est", "e"), ("ly", ""), ("ally", ""))
    for suffix, repl in rules:
        if f.endswith(suffix) and len(f) - len(suffix) >= 2:
            stem = f[: -len(suffix)]
            out.add(stem + repl)
            if repl == "" and len(stem) >= 3 and stem[-1] == stem[-2] and suffix in ("ed", "ing", "er", "est"):
                out.add(stem[:-1])  # stopped -> stop
    return frozenset(c for c in out if len(c) >= 2 and (c == f or f in inflections(c)))


def same_lemma(a: str, b: str) -> bool:
    fa, fb = fold(a), fold(b)
    if fa == fb:
        return True
    return fb in inflections(fa) or fa in inflections(fb) or bool(
        (lemma_candidates(fa) & lemma_candidates(fb)) - {""})


def find_form(sentence: str, key: str) -> tuple[int, int, str] | None:
    tokens = tokenize(sentence)
    parts = fold(key).split()
    if not parts:
        return None
    if len(parts) == 1:
        target = parts[0]
        for token in tokens:
            if fold(token.text) == target:
                return token.start, token.end, token.text
        forms = inflections(target)
        for token in tokens:
            if fold(token.text) in forms:
                return token.start, token.end, token.text
        return None
    last_forms = inflections(parts[-1])
    for i in range(len(tokens) - len(parts) + 1):
        window = tokens[i: i + len(parts)]
        if all(fold(t.text) == p for t, p in zip(window[:-1], parts[:-1])) and fold(window[-1].text) in last_forms:
            return window[0].start, window[-1].end, sentence[window[0].start: window[-1].end]
    return None
```

(`lemma_candidates` 的过滤条件保证候选本身能变回原形 `f`,避免 `bed → be` 这类误判。)

### Task D2: `entry.py` — 清洗与合并重复行为 `WordCard`

**Files:**
- Create: `server/app/wordbook/entry.py`
- Test: `server/tests/test_wordbook_entry.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:D1 的 `find_form`、`tokenize`、`fold`;`app.services.vocabulary.normalize_word`;`books.DENYLIST`、`books.POS_ABBREVIATIONS`
- Produces:`POS_BITS = {"n": 1, "v": 2, "adj": 4, "adv": 8}`、`OTHER_BIT = 16`、`Sense(pos: str, text: str)`、`Example(id: str, en: str, zh: str)`、`WordCard(key, word, us, uk, senses: tuple[Sense, ...], phrases: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...], examples: tuple[Example, ...], pos_mask: int)` 及属性 `spellable: bool`;`headword_key(word: str) -> str | None`、`pos_parts(type_str: str | None) -> list[str]`、`pos_mask_of(parts: list[str]) -> int`、`clean_meaning(text: str) -> str`、`normalize_phonetic(text: str | None) -> str`、`clean_sentence(text: str) -> str | None`、`sentence_id(en: str) -> str`、`merge_rows(rows: list[dict], *, hidden: frozenset[str] = frozenset()) -> WordCard | None`、`meaning_line(card: WordCard, max_chars: int = 24) -> str`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

```python
import json
from collections import defaultdict

from app.wordbook.entry import (clean_meaning, clean_sentence, headword_key, meaning_line, merge_rows,
                                normalize_phonetic, pos_mask_of, pos_parts, sentence_id)
from tests.wordbook_helpers import MINI


def rows_by_key():
    groups = defaultdict(list)
    for line in MINI.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        row = json.loads(line)
        key = headword_key(row["word"])
        if key:
            groups[key].append(row)
    return groups


def test_headword_rules():
    assert headword_key("  Talk ") == "talk"
    assert headword_key("adj.") is None and headword_key("undersize d") is None
    assert headword_key("a. 灵巧的， 熟练的") is None and headword_key("AluminumAl") is None
    assert headword_key("used to") == "used to" and headword_key("Mr.") == "mr."
    assert len(rows_by_key()) == 14


def test_pos_parsing():
    assert pos_parts("n & v") == ["n", "v"] and pos_parts("v / n") == ["v", "n"] and pos_parts("a&ad") == ["adj", "adv"]
    assert pos_parts(None) == ["other"] and pos_parts("vt") == ["v"]
    assert pos_mask_of(["n", "v"]) == 3 and pos_mask_of(["abbr"]) == 16


def test_text_cleaning():
    assert clean_meaning(" 能力， 本领； 才能 ") == "能力，本领；才能"
    assert normalize_phonetic("/suːˈpɜːrb/") == "suːˈpɜːrb" and normalize_phonetic("ə'bɪləti") == "əˈbɪləti"
    assert clean_sentence("Sue and Bob still aren’t talking (= are refusing to talk to each other ) .") == \
        "Sue and Bob still aren’t talking."
    assert clean_sentence("...memories of her father's complete abandonment of her.") is None
    assert clean_sentence("Too short.") is None
    assert clean_sentence("This coat was dirt cheap 非正式用语 today.") is None
    assert len(sentence_id("abc")) == 16


def test_merge_talk_duplicates():
    card = merge_rows(rows_by_key()["talk"])
    assert (card.key, card.word, card.us, card.uk) == ("talk", "talk", "tɔk", "tɔːk")
    assert [(s.pos, s.text) for s in card.senses] == [("v", "说话；谈话"), ("n", "交谈"), ("n/v", "谈话，讲话")]
    assert [e.en for e in card.examples] == [
        "I could hear Sarah and Andy talking in the next room.", "After a long talk, we decided on divorce.",
        "Sue and Bob still aren’t talking."]
    assert card.pos_mask == 3 and card.spellable
    assert meaning_line(card) == "v. 说话；谈话；n. 交谈"


def test_merge_drops_unmatched_hidden_and_marks_unspellable():
    groups = rows_by_key()
    abandon = merge_rows(groups["abandon"])
    assert [e.en for e in abandon.examples] == ["They had to abandon the car in the deep snow."]
    hidden = frozenset({abandon.examples[0].id})
    assert merge_rows(groups["abandon"], hidden=hidden).examples == ()
    careful = merge_rows(groups["careful"])
    assert careful.us == "" and careful.examples == () and careful.senses[0].text == "小心的，仔细的"
    assert merge_rows(groups["used to"]).spellable
    assert merge_rows([{"word": "Mr.", "us": "", "uk": "", "translations": [], "phrases": [], "sentences": []}]).spellable is False
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 跑测试确认失败** — Run: `cd server && uv run pytest -q tests/test_wordbook_entry.py` → FAIL

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `server/app/wordbook/entry.py`:

```python
"""行清洗与重复行合并(spec §2.11)。纯函数:输入是 json.loads 后的行,输出不可变的 WordCard。"""
import hashlib
import re
import unicodedata
from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.services.vocabulary import normalize_word
from app.wordbook.books import DENYLIST, POS_ABBREVIATIONS
from app.wordbook.textnorm import find_form, tokenize

POS_BITS = {"n": 1, "v": 2, "adj": 4, "adv": 8}
OTHER_BIT = 16
_ALIASES = {"vt": "v", "vi": "v", "a": "adj", "ad": "adv", "modal verb": "v", "modalverb": "v", "aux": "v"}
MAX_SENSES, MAX_PHRASES, MAX_EXAMPLES = 6, 10, 6
MIN_WORDS, MAX_WORDS = 4, 25
_CJK = re.compile(r"[㐀-鿿豈-﫿]")
_GLOSS = re.compile(r"\s*\(\s*=[^)]*\)")
_SPACE_BEFORE_PUNCT = re.compile(r"\s+([,.;:!?])")
_SPACE_AFTER_FULLWIDTH = re.compile(r"([，；：。、])\s+")
_SPELLABLE = re.compile(r"[a-z]+(?:[ '\-][a-z]+)*")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Sense:
    pos: str
    text: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Example:
    id: str
    en: str
    zh: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WordCard:
    key: str
    word: str
    us: str
    uk: str
    senses: tuple[Sense, ...]
    phrases: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...]
    examples: tuple[Example, ...]
    pos_mask: int

    @property
    def spellable(self) -> bool:
        """拼写/听写/挖空只出 ASCII 字母(可含空格、连字符、撇号)的词头(spec §2.11-7)。"""
        return _SPELLABLE.fullmatch(self.key) is not None


def headword_key(word: str) -> str | None:
    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", word or "").strip()
    if not text or _CJK.search(text):
        return None
    key = normalize_word(text)
    if key in POS_ABBREVIATIONS or key in DENYLIST:
        return None
    return key


def pos_parts(type_str: str | None) -> list[str]:
    raw = (type_str or "").strip().lower()
    if not raw:
        return ["other"]
    parts = [p.strip().rstrip(".") for p in re.split(r"[&/,]", raw) if p.strip().rstrip(".")]
    return [_ALIASES.get(p, p) for p in parts] or ["other"]


def pos_mask_of(parts: list[str]) -> int:
    mask = 0
    for part in parts:
        mask |= POS_BITS.get(part, OTHER_BIT)
    return mask


def clean_meaning(text: str) -> str:
    cleaned = _SPACE_AFTER_FULLWIDTH.sub(r"\1", unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text or "").strip())
    return " ".join(cleaned.split())


def normalize_phonetic(text: str | None) -> str:
    return (text or "").strip().strip("/[]").strip().replace("'", "ˈ")


def clean_sentence(text: str) -> str | None:
    t = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text or "").replace(" ", " ").replace(" ", " ")
    t = _SPACE_BEFORE_PUNCT.sub(r"\1", _GLOSS.sub("", t))
    t = " ".join(t.split())
    if not t or t.startswith(("...", "…")) or _CJK.search(t):
        return None
    if not MIN_WORDS <= len(tokenize(t)) <= MAX_WORDS:
        return None
    return t


def sentence_id(en: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha1(en.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]


def merge_rows(rows: list[dict], *, hidden: frozenset[str] = frozenset()) -> WordCard | None:
    usable = [(k, r) for r in rows if (k := headword_key(r.get("word", "")))]
    if not usable:
        return None
    key = usable[0][0]
    display = Counter(r["word"].strip() for _, r in usable).most_common(1)[0][0]
    us = next((normalize_phonetic(r.get("us")) for _, r in usable if (r.get("us") or "").strip()), "")
    uk = next((normalize_phonetic(r.get("uk")) for _, r in usable if (r.get("uk") or "").strip()), "")
    senses: list[Sense] = []
    phrases: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    examples: list[Example] = []
    seen_senses, seen_phrases, seen_examples = set(), set(), set()
    mask = 0
    for _, row in usable:
        for t in row.get("translations") or []:
            parts = pos_parts(t.get("type"))
            mask |= pos_mask_of(parts)
            text = clean_meaning(t.get("translation") or "")
            pos = "/".join(p for p in parts if p != "other")
            if text and (pos, text) not in seen_senses and len(senses) < MAX_SENSES:
                seen_senses.add((pos, text))
                senses.append(Sense(pos, text))
        for p in row.get("phrases") or []:
            en = " ".join((p.get("phrase") or "").split())
            if en and en.lower() not in seen_phrases and len(phrases) < MAX_PHRASES:
                seen_phrases.add(en.lower())
                phrases.append((en, " ".join((p.get("translation") or "").split())))
        for s in row.get("sentences") or []:
            en = clean_sentence(s.get("sentence") or "")
            if en is None or find_form(en, key) is None:
                continue
            sid = sentence_id(en)
            if sid in hidden or sid in seen_examples or len(examples) >= MAX_EXAMPLES:
                continue
            seen_examples.add(sid)
            examples.append(Example(sid, en, " ".join((s.get("translation") or "").split())))
    return WordCard(key, display, us, uk, tuple(senses), tuple(phrases), tuple(examples), mask)


def meaning_line(card: WordCard, max_chars: int = 24) -> str:
    line = "；".join(f"{s.pos}. {s.text}" if s.pos else s.text for s in card.senses[:2])
    return line if len(line) <= max_chars else line[: max_chars - 1] + "…"
```
