### Task D3: `index.py` — 书索引与 `DictionaryStore`

**Files:**
- Create: `server/app/wordbook/index.py`
- Test: `server/tests/test_wordbook_index.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:D2 的 `headword_key`、`pos_parts`、`pos_mask_of`、`clean_sentence`、`merge_rows`、`WordCard`;D1 的 `find_form`;`books.book_by_id`
- Produces:`class DataUnavailable(Exception)`(`.book_id`);`BookIndex(book_id, size, mtime_ns, keys: tuple[str, ...], ordinal: dict[str, int], offsets: list[tuple[int, ...]], pos_mask: array, has_example: array, dropped: int, built_ms: int)`,支持 `key in index`、`len(index)`;`build_index(book_id: str, path: Path) -> BookIndex`;`DictionaryStore(data_dir: Path, *, cache_size: int = 1024)`,方法 `path_for(book_id) -> Path`、`async index(book_id) -> BookIndex`、`async cards(book_id, keys: list[str]) -> dict[str, WordCard]`(保持顺序,缺的键省略)、`async card(book_id, key) -> WordCard | None`、`set_hidden(ids) -> None`、属性 `hidden: frozenset[str]`、`hidden_loaded: bool`、`reload(book_id: str | None = None) -> None`、`loaded() -> dict[str, BookIndex]`、`stale(book_id) -> bool`;`dictionary(app) -> DictionaryStore`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

```python
import asyncio

import pytest

from app.wordbook.index import DataUnavailable, DictionaryStore, build_index, dictionary
from tests.wordbook_helpers import MINI, enable_wordbook, make_data_dir


def test_build_index_dedupes_and_drops():
    idx = build_index("junior", MINI)
    assert len(idx) == 14 and idx.dropped == 2
    assert idx.keys[:3] == ("talk", "ability", "able")
    assert len(idx.offsets[idx.ordinal["talk"]]) == 2 and idx.offsets[idx.ordinal["talk"]][0] == 0
    assert idx.pos_mask[idx.ordinal["talk"]] == 3 and idx.pos_mask[idx.ordinal["able"]] == 4
    assert idx.has_example[idx.ordinal["careful"]] == 0 and idx.has_example[idx.ordinal["go"]] == 1
    assert "adj." not in idx and idx.size == MINI.stat().st_size


async def test_store_lazy_loads_reads_cards_and_caches(tmp_path):
    store = DictionaryStore(make_data_dir(tmp_path), cache_size=2)
    assert store.loaded() == {}
    cards = await store.cards("junior", ["go", "nope", "talk"])
    assert list(cards) == ["go", "talk"] and cards["talk"].examples[0].en.startswith("I could hear")
    assert set(store.loaded()) == {"junior"}
    assert (await store.card("junior", "go")) is cards["go"]  # LRU hit returns the same object
    await store.cards("junior", ["able", "child"])
    assert (await store.card("junior", "go")) is not cards["go"]  # evicted (cache_size=2)


async def test_concurrent_first_access_builds_once(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    import app.wordbook.index as mod
    real = mod.build_index
    monkeypatch.setattr(mod, "build_index", lambda b, p: calls.append(b) or real(b, p))
    store = DictionaryStore(make_data_dir(tmp_path))
    await asyncio.gather(*(store.index("junior") for _ in range(5)))
    assert calls == ["junior"]


async def test_missing_book_hidden_sentences_reload_and_stale(tmp_path):
    data = make_data_dir(tmp_path)
    store = DictionaryStore(data)
    with pytest.raises(DataUnavailable):
        await store.index("toefl")
    abandon = await store.card("junior", "abandon")
    store.set_hidden({abandon.examples[0].id})
    assert (await store.card("junior", "abandon")).examples == () and store.hidden_loaded
    assert store.stale("junior") is False
    (data / "junior.jsonl").write_bytes((data / "junior.jsonl").read_bytes() + b"\n")
    assert store.stale("junior") is True
    store.reload("junior")
    assert store.loaded() == {}


async def test_dictionary_accessor_follows_settings(app, tmp_path):
    enable_wordbook(app, make_data_dir(tmp_path))
    first = dictionary(app)
    assert first is dictionary(app) and first.data_dir == tmp_path / "dict"
    enable_wordbook(app, tmp_path / "other")
    assert dictionary(app).data_dir == tmp_path / "other"
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 跑测试确认失败** — Run: `cd server && uv run pytest -q tests/test_wordbook_index.py` → FAIL

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `server/app/wordbook/index.py`:

```python
"""词典文件的内存索引与按需读行(spec §4)。按书懒加载;建索引与读行都在线程里做,不阻塞事件循环。"""
import array
import asyncio
import json
import time
from collections import OrderedDict
from collections.abc import Iterable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from app.wordbook.books import book_by_id
from app.wordbook.entry import WordCard, clean_sentence, headword_key, merge_rows, pos_mask_of, pos_parts
from app.wordbook.textnorm import find_form


class DataUnavailable(Exception):
    def __init__(self, book_id: str):
        super().__init__(book_id)
        self.book_id = book_id


@dataclass
class BookIndex:
    book_id: str
    size: int
    mtime_ns: int
    keys: tuple[str, ...]
    ordinal: dict[str, int]
    offsets: list[tuple[int, ...]]
    pos_mask: array.array
    has_example: array.array
    dropped: int
    built_ms: int

    def __contains__(self, key: object) -> bool:
        return key in self.ordinal

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self.keys)


def build_index(book_id: str, path: Path) -> BookIndex:
    started = time.perf_counter()
    stat = path.stat()
    ordinal: dict[str, int] = {}
    keys: list[str] = []
    offsets: list[tuple[int, ...]] = []
    mask, flags = array.array("B"), array.array("B")
    dropped = offset = 0
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for line in handle:
            if line.strip():
                row = json.loads(line)
                key = headword_key(row.get("word", ""))
                if key is None:
                    dropped += 1
                else:
                    bits = 0
                    for t in row.get("translations") or []:
                        bits |= pos_mask_of(pos_parts(t.get("type")))
                    example = any((en := clean_sentence(s.get("sentence") or "")) and find_form(en, key)
                                  for s in row.get("sentences") or [])
                    i = ordinal.get(key)
                    if i is None:
                        ordinal[key] = len(keys)
                        keys.append(key)
                        offsets.append((offset,))
                        mask.append(bits)
                        flags.append(int(example))
                    else:
                        offsets[i] += (offset,)
                        mask[i] |= bits
                        flags[i] |= int(example)
            offset += len(line)
    return BookIndex(book_id, stat.st_size, stat.st_mtime_ns, tuple(keys), ordinal, offsets, mask, flags, dropped,
                     round((time.perf_counter() - started) * 1000))


def _read_rows(path: Path, groups: list[tuple[int, ...]]) -> list[list[dict]]:
    out: list[list[dict]] = []
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for offsets in groups:
            rows = []
            for offset in offsets:
                handle.seek(offset)
                rows.append(json.loads(handle.readline()))
            out.append(rows)
    return out


class DictionaryStore:
    def __init__(self, data_dir: Path, *, cache_size: int = 1024):
        self.data_dir = Path(data_dir)
        self._cache_size = cache_size
        self._indexes: dict[str, BookIndex] = {}
        self._locks: dict[str, asyncio.Lock] = {}
        self._cards: OrderedDict[tuple[str, str], WordCard] = OrderedDict()
        self.hidden: frozenset[str] = frozenset()
        self.hidden_loaded = False

    def path_for(self, book_id: str) -> Path:
        spec = book_by_id(book_id)
        if spec is None:
            raise DataUnavailable(book_id)
        return self.data_dir / spec.file

    async def index(self, book_id: str) -> BookIndex:
        found = self._indexes.get(book_id)
        if found is not None:
            return found
        async with self._locks.setdefault(book_id, asyncio.Lock()):
            found = self._indexes.get(book_id)
            if found is None:
                path = self.path_for(book_id)
                if not path.is_file():
                    raise DataUnavailable(book_id)
                try:
                    found = await asyncio.to_thread(build_index, book_id, path)
                except (OSError, ValueError):
                    raise DataUnavailable(book_id) from None
                self._indexes[book_id] = found
        return found

    def _remember(self, slot: tuple[str, str], card: WordCard) -> None:
        self._cards[slot] = card
        self._cards.move_to_end(slot)
        while len(self._cards) > self._cache_size:
            self._cards.popitem(last=False)

    async def cards(self, book_id: str, keys: list[str]) -> dict[str, WordCard]:
        index = await self.index(book_id)
        found: dict[str, WordCard] = {}
        missing: list[str] = []
        for key in keys:
            slot = (book_id, key)
            if slot in self._cards:
                self._cards.move_to_end(slot)
                found[key] = self._cards[slot]
            elif key in index and key not in missing:
                missing.append(key)
        if missing:
            try:
                rows = await asyncio.to_thread(_read_rows, self.path_for(book_id),
                                               [index.offsets[index.ordinal[k]] for k in missing])
            except (OSError, ValueError):
                raise DataUnavailable(book_id) from None
            for key, group in zip(missing, rows):
                card = merge_rows(group, hidden=self.hidden)
                if card is not None:
                    found[key] = card
                    self._remember((book_id, key), card)
        return {k: found[k] for k in keys if k in found}

    async def card(self, book_id: str, key: str) -> WordCard | None:
        return (await self.cards(book_id, [key])).get(key)

    def set_hidden(self, ids: Iterable[str]) -> None:
        self.hidden = frozenset(ids)
        self.hidden_loaded = True
        self._cards.clear()

    def reload(self, book_id: str | None = None) -> None:
        if book_id is None:
            self._indexes.clear()
            self._cards.clear()
            return
        self._indexes.pop(book_id, None)
        for slot in [s for s in self._cards if s[0] == book_id]:
            del self._cards[slot]

    def loaded(self) -> dict[str, BookIndex]:
        return dict(self._indexes)

    def stale(self, book_id: str) -> bool:
        index = self._indexes.get(book_id)
        if index is None:
            return False
        try:
            stat = self.path_for(book_id).stat()
        except OSError:
            return True
        return (stat.st_size, stat.st_mtime_ns) != (index.size, index.mtime_ns)


def dictionary(app) -> DictionaryStore:
    """唯一入口(计划 P-W1):数据目录随设置变化时换一个新 store。"""
    want = app.state.settings.resolved_wordbook_data_dir
    store = getattr(app.state, "wordbook_dict", None)
    if store is None or store.data_dir != Path(want):
        store = app.state.wordbook_dict = DictionaryStore(want)
    return store
```

### Task D4: `grading.py` — 四结果判分与逐字母对齐

**Files:**
- Create: `server/app/wordbook/grading.py`
- Test: `server/tests/test_wordbook_grading.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:D1 的 `fold`、`inflections`、`lemma_candidates`
- Produces:`Outcome = Literal["correct", "typo", "inflection", "wrong"]`、`CREDIT: dict[str, int]`、`DiffOp(op: Literal["eq","sub","ins","del"], a: str, b: str)`(`a` = 期望字符,`b` = 输入字符)、`BlankResult(outcome, credit: int, typed: str, expected: str, diff: tuple[DiffOp, ...])`、`levenshtein(a, b) -> int`、`align(expected, typed) -> tuple[DiffOp, ...]`、`typo_limit(n: int) -> int`、`grade_blank(expected: str, lemma: str, typed: str, *, hinted: bool = False) -> BlankResult`、`worst(outcomes: Iterable[Outcome]) -> Outcome`、`srs_grade(outcome: str, hinted: bool) -> Literal["good", "hard", "forgot"]`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

```python
import pytest

from app.wordbook.grading import DiffOp, align, grade_blank, levenshtein, srs_grade, typo_limit, worst


@pytest.mark.parametrize("expected,lemma,typed,outcome,credit", [
    ("talking", "talk", "talking", "correct", 100),
    ("talking", "talk", "  Talking ", "correct", 100),
    ("talking", "talk", "talked", "inflection", 50),
    ("talking", "talk", "talk", "inflection", 50),
    ("went", "go", "go", "inflection", 50),
    ("children", "child", "childs", "inflection", 50),
    ("children", "child", "chilren", "typo", 50),
    ("talking", "talk", "talkng", "typo", 50),
    ("necessary", "necessary", "neccessary", "typo", 50),
    ("necessary", "necessary", "nesesary", "typo", 50),
    ("cat", "cat", "cut", "typo", 50),
    ("cat", "cat", "dog", "wrong", 0),
    ("ability", "ability", "", "wrong", 0),
    ("accident", "accident", "event", "wrong", 0),
])
def test_grade_blank(expected, lemma, typed, outcome, credit):
    result = grade_blank(expected, lemma, typed)
    assert (result.outcome, result.credit) == (outcome, credit)


def test_hint_halves_credit_and_maps_to_hard():
    assert grade_blank("talk", "talk", "talk", hinted=True).credit == 50
    assert grade_blank("talking", "talk", "talkng", hinted=True).credit == 25
    assert srs_grade("correct", False) == "good" and srs_grade("correct", True) == "hard"
    assert srs_grade("typo", False) == "hard" and srs_grade("inflection", False) == "hard"
    assert srs_grade("wrong", False) == "forgot"


def test_levenshtein_and_limits():
    assert levenshtein("kitten", "sitting") == 3 and levenshtein("", "ab") == 2
    assert typo_limit(4) == 1 and typo_limit(5) == 2 and typo_limit(12) == 2


def test_align_marks_missing_extra_and_wrong_letters():
    assert align("talking", "talkng") == (
        DiffOp("eq", "t", "t"), DiffOp("eq", "a", "a"), DiffOp("eq", "l", "l"), DiffOp("eq", "k", "k"),
        DiffOp("del", "i", ""), DiffOp("eq", "n", "n"), DiffOp("eq", "g", "g"))
    assert [d.op for d in align("cat", "cut")] == ["eq", "sub", "eq"]
    assert [d.op for d in align("go", "goo")].count("ins") == 1
    assert grade_blank("talking", "talk", "talkng").diff[4] == DiffOp("del", "i", "")
    assert grade_blank("ability", "ability", "xyzxyzxyzxyz").diff == ()  # 长度差太大不对齐


def test_worst():
    assert worst(["correct", "typo"]) == "typo" and worst(["typo", "inflection"]) == "inflection"
    assert worst(["correct", "wrong", "typo"]) == "wrong"
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 跑测试确认失败** — Run: `cd server && uv run pytest -q tests/test_wordbook_grading.py` → FAIL

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `server/app/wordbook/grading.py`:

```python
"""确定性拼写判分(spec §6.6,裁定 R-W7):正确 / 小笔误 / 词形不对 / 错误 + 逐字母对齐。不调用 LLM。"""
from collections.abc import Iterable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

from app.wordbook.textnorm import fold, inflections, lemma_candidates

Outcome = Literal["correct", "typo", "inflection", "wrong"]
CREDIT: dict[str, int] = {"correct": 100, "typo": 50, "inflection": 50, "wrong": 0}
_ORDER = ("correct", "typo", "inflection", "wrong")
MAX_ALIGN_GAP = 3


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiffOp:
    op: Literal["eq", "sub", "ins", "del"]
    a: str  # expected
    b: str  # typed


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BlankResult:
    outcome: Outcome
    credit: int
    typed: str
    expected: str
    diff: tuple[DiffOp, ...]


def _table(a: str, b: str) -> list[list[int]]:
    d = [[0] * (len(b) + 1) for _ in range(len(a) + 1)]
    for i in range(len(a) + 1):
        d[i][0] = i
    for j in range(len(b) + 1):
        d[0][j] = j
    for i in range(1, len(a) + 1):
        for j in range(1, len(b) + 1):
            d[i][j] = min(d[i - 1][j] + 1, d[i][j - 1] + 1, d[i - 1][j - 1] + (a[i - 1] != b[j - 1]))
    return d


def levenshtein(a: str, b: str) -> int:
    return _table(a, b)[len(a)][len(b)]


def align(expected: str, typed: str) -> tuple[DiffOp, ...]:
    d = _table(expected, typed)
    i, j, ops = len(expected), len(typed), []
    while i or j:
        if i and j and d[i][j] == d[i - 1][j - 1] + (expected[i - 1] != typed[j - 1]):
            same = expected[i - 1] == typed[j - 1]
            ops.append(DiffOp("eq" if same else "sub", expected[i - 1], typed[j - 1]))
            i, j = i - 1, j - 1
        elif i and d[i][j] == d[i - 1][j] + 1:
            ops.append(DiffOp("del", expected[i - 1], ""))
            i -= 1
        else:
            ops.append(DiffOp("ins", "", typed[j - 1]))
            j -= 1
    return tuple(reversed(ops))


def typo_limit(n: int) -> int:
    return 1 if n <= 4 else 2


def grade_blank(expected: str, lemma: str, typed: str, *, hinted: bool = False) -> BlankResult:
    e, t, base = fold(expected), fold(typed), fold(lemma)
    if t == e:
        outcome: Outcome = "correct"
    elif t and (t in inflections(base) or base in lemma_candidates(t)):
        outcome = "inflection"
    elif t and levenshtein(e, t) <= typo_limit(len(e)):
        outcome = "typo"
    else:
        outcome = "wrong"
    credit = CREDIT[outcome] // (2 if hinted else 1)
    diff = align(e, t) if outcome in ("typo", "wrong") and t and abs(len(t) - len(e)) <= MAX_ALIGN_GAP else ()
    return BlankResult(outcome, credit, typed.strip(), expected, diff)


def worst(outcomes: Iterable[Outcome]) -> Outcome:
    return max(outcomes, key=_ORDER.index)


def srs_grade(outcome: str, hinted: bool) -> Literal["good", "hard", "forgot"]:
    if outcome == "wrong":
        return "forgot"
    if outcome == "correct" and not hinted:
        return "good"
    return "hard"
```

### Task D5: `quiz.py` — 题型选择、干扰项、题目构建

**Files:**
- Create: `server/app/wordbook/quiz.py`
- Test: `server/tests/test_wordbook_quiz.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:D2 `WordCard`、`meaning_line`;D3 `BookIndex`;D1 `find_form`、`fold`、`same_lemma`
- Produces:`QType = Literal["meaning", "word", "cloze", "dictation", "spell"]`、`Question(key, qtype, prompt: dict[str, str], options: tuple[str, ...] | None, length: int | None)`、`question_rng(user_id: int, key: str, reps: int) -> random.Random`、`eligible_qtypes(box: int, card: WordCard, *, tts: bool = True) -> tuple[QType, ...]`、`pick_qtype(box, card, rng, *, tts=True) -> QType`、`cloze_example(card) -> tuple[Example, int, int] | None`、`candidate_keys(index: BookIndex, target_key: str, rng, k: int = 24) -> list[str]`、`pick_distractors(target: WordCard, pool: list[WordCard], qtype: QType, rng, n: int = 3) -> list[WordCard]`、`build_question(qtype, card, distractors, rng) -> Question`、`check_choice(qtype, card, choice: str) -> bool`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

```python
import random

import pytest

from app.wordbook.entry import meaning_line
from app.wordbook.index import DictionaryStore, build_index
from app.wordbook.quiz import (build_question, candidate_keys, check_choice, cloze_example, eligible_qtypes,
                               pick_distractors, pick_qtype, question_rng)
from tests.wordbook_helpers import MINI, make_data_dir


@pytest.fixture
async def cards(tmp_path):
    store = DictionaryStore(make_data_dir(tmp_path))
    index = await store.index("junior")
    return index, await store.cards("junior", list(index.keys))


async def test_eligible_qtypes_by_box(cards):
    _, c = cards
    assert eligible_qtypes(0, c["talk"]) == ("meaning", "word")
    assert eligible_qtypes(2, c["abandon"]) == ("word", "cloze", "dictation")
    assert eligible_qtypes(2, c["abandon"], tts=False) == ("word", "cloze")
    assert eligible_qtypes(4, c["abandon"]) == ("dictation", "spell")
    assert eligible_qtypes(4, c["used to"]) == ("meaning",)  # 多词词头不出拼写,回落
    assert cloze_example(c["talk"])[0].en == "After a long talk, we decided on divorce."


def test_question_rng_is_stable():
    assert question_rng(7, "talk", 3).random() == question_rng(7, "talk", 3).random()
    assert question_rng(7, "talk", 3).random() != question_rng(7, "talk", 4).random()


async def test_candidates_share_pos_and_skip_related(cards):
    index, c = cards
    keys = candidate_keys(index, "abandon", random.Random(1), k=6)
    assert "abandon" not in keys and len(keys) == 6
    assert all(index.pos_mask[index.ordinal[k]] & index.pos_mask[index.ordinal["abandon"]] for k in keys)
    assert "ability" not in candidate_keys(index, "able", random.Random(2), k=10) or True  # 前缀不足 5 不算同根


async def test_distractors_avoid_shared_meanings(cards):
    _, c = cards
    fake = c["accept"].__class__(**{**c["accept"].__dict__, "key": "receive", "word": "receive"})
    pool = [c["abandon"], c["absorb"], c["carry"], c["stop"], fake]
    picked = pick_distractors(c["accept"], pool, "meaning", random.Random(3))
    assert len(picked) == 3 and fake not in picked and c["accept"] not in picked


async def test_build_and_check_questions(cards):
    _, c = cards
    others = [c["abandon"], c["absorb"], c["carry"]]
    q = build_question("meaning", c["accept"], others, random.Random(4))
    assert q.prompt == {"word": "accept", "us": "əkˈsɛpt"} and len(q.options) == 4
    assert meaning_line(c["accept"]) in q.options and check_choice("meaning", c["accept"], meaning_line(c["accept"]))
    w = build_question("word", c["accept"], others, random.Random(4))
    assert set(w.options) == {"accept", "abandon", "absorb", "carry"} and w.prompt["meaning"].startswith("v.")
    cz = build_question("cloze", c["talk"], others, random.Random(5))
    assert cz.prompt["sentence"] == "After a long _____, we decided on divorce." and check_choice("cloze", c["talk"], "Talk")
    sp = build_question("spell", c["accept"], [], random.Random(6))
    assert sp.options is None and sp.length == 6 and "word" not in sp.prompt
    dc = build_question("dictation", c["accept"], [], random.Random(6))
    assert dc.prompt == {"word": "accept"} and dc.length == 6
    assert pick_qtype(0, c["talk"], random.Random(1)) in ("meaning", "word")
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 跑测试确认失败** — Run: `cd server && uv run pytest -q tests/test_wordbook_quiz.py` → FAIL

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `server/app/wordbook/quiz.py`:

```python
"""零成本题型与同书干扰项(spec §6.4、§6.5)。纯函数;随机一律用传入的 random.Random。"""
import hashlib
import random
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

from app.wordbook.entry import Example, WordCard, meaning_line
from app.wordbook.index import BookIndex
from app.wordbook.textnorm import find_form, fold, same_lemma

QType = Literal["meaning", "word", "cloze", "dictation", "spell"]
BLANK = "_____"
_SPLIT = re.compile(r"[，；、,;]")
_NOTE = re.compile(r"\[[^\]]*\]")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Question:
    key: str
    qtype: QType
    prompt: dict[str, str]
    options: tuple[str, ...] | None
    length: int | None


def question_rng(user_id: int, key: str, reps: int) -> random.Random:
    digest = hashlib.sha256(f"{user_id}:{key}:{reps}".encode()).digest()
    return random.Random(int.from_bytes(digest[:8], "big"))


def cloze_example(card: WordCard) -> tuple[Example, int, int] | None:
    for example in card.examples:
        span = find_form(example.en, card.key)
        if span and fold(span[2]) == card.key:
            return example, span[0], span[1]
    return None


def eligible_qtypes(box: int, card: WordCard, *, tts: bool = True) -> tuple[QType, ...]:
    out: list[QType] = []
    if box <= 1:
        out.append("meaning")
    if box <= 2:
        out.append("word")
    if 1 <= box <= 3 and cloze_example(card):
        out.append("cloze")
    if box >= 2 and card.spellable and " " not in card.key:
        if tts:
            out.append("dictation")
        if box >= 3:
            out.append("spell")
    return tuple(out) or ("meaning",)


def pick_qtype(box: int, card: WordCard, rng: random.Random, *, tts: bool = True) -> QType:
    return rng.choice(eligible_qtypes(box, card, tts=tts))


def _related(a: str, b: str) -> bool:
    return (min(len(a), len(b)) >= 5 and a[:5] == b[:5]) or same_lemma(a, b)


def candidate_keys(index: BookIndex, target_key: str, rng: random.Random, k: int = 24) -> list[str]:
    target_mask = index.pos_mask[index.ordinal[target_key]]
    n = len(index.keys)
    picked: list[str] = []
    for strict in (True, False):
        tries = 0
        while len(picked) < k and tries < k * 20 and n > 1:
            tries += 1
            j = rng.randrange(n)
            key = index.keys[j]
            if key == target_key or key in picked or _related(key, target_key):
                continue
            if strict and target_mask and not index.pos_mask[j] & target_mask:
                continue
            picked.append(key)
        if len(picked) >= 3:
            break
    return picked


def _fragments(card: WordCard) -> set[str]:
    out: set[str] = set()
    for sense in card.senses:
        out |= {p.strip() for p in _SPLIT.split(_NOTE.sub("", sense.text)) if p.strip()}
    return out


def pick_distractors(target: WordCard, pool: list[WordCard], qtype: QType, rng: random.Random,
                     n: int = 3) -> list[WordCard]:
    mine, line = _fragments(target), meaning_line(target)
    ok = [c for c in pool if c.key != target.key and c.word.lower() != target.word.lower()
          and not _fragments(c) & mine and meaning_line(c) != line]
    if qtype in ("word", "cloze"):
        ok.sort(key=lambda c: abs(len(c.word) - len(target.word)))
        ok = ok[: max(2 * n, n)]
    return rng.sample(ok, min(n, len(ok)))


def build_question(qtype: QType, card: WordCard, distractors: list[WordCard], rng: random.Random) -> Question:
    if qtype in ("dictation", "spell"):
        prompt = {"word": card.word} if qtype == "dictation" else {"meaning": meaning_line(card, 40), "us": card.us or card.uk}
        return Question(card.key, qtype, prompt, None, len(card.word))
    if qtype == "meaning":
        options = [meaning_line(card)] + [meaning_line(d) for d in distractors]
        prompt = {"word": card.word, "us": card.us or card.uk}
    elif qtype == "word":
        options = [card.word] + [d.word for d in distractors]
        prompt = {"meaning": meaning_line(card, 40)}
    else:  # cloze
        found = cloze_example(card)
        if found is None:
            raise ValueError("card has no cloze example")
        example, start, end = found
        options = [card.word] + [d.word for d in distractors]
        prompt = {"sentence": example.en[:start] + BLANK + example.en[end:], "zh": example.zh}
    rng.shuffle(options)
    return Question(card.key, qtype, prompt, tuple(options), None)


def check_choice(qtype: QType, card: WordCard, choice: str) -> bool:
    if qtype == "meaning":
        return choice == meaning_line(card)
    if qtype in ("word", "cloze"):
        return fold(choice) == card.key
    raise ValueError(f"{qtype} is not a choice question")
```

(`dictation` 题面带词头文本,用于客户端调用 TTS,界面不显示;作弊空间与选择题相同,见 spec R-W11。)

### Task D6: `cloze.py` — 拼写句组与按 `sentence_id` 复原

**Files:**
- Create: `server/app/wordbook/cloze.py`
- Test: `server/tests/test_wordbook_cloze.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:D2 `WordCard`、`Example`;D1 `find_form`、`fold`、`tokenize`
- Produces:`Segment(t: Literal["text","blank"], v: str = "", i: int = -1, len: int = 0)`、`ClozeLine(key, sentence_id, segments: tuple[Segment, ...], zh, expected: tuple[str, ...], lemmas: tuple[str, ...])`、`ClozeSet(set_id: str, lines: tuple[ClozeLine, ...])`、`best_example(card) -> Example | None`、`line_for(card, sentence_id: str | None = None, *, start: int = 0) -> ClozeLine | None`、`build_set(cards: list[WordCard], *, size: int = 5) -> ClozeSet | None`(少于 3 句返回 None)、`set_id_for(lines) -> str`、`MIN_LINES = 3`、`MAX_LINES = 6`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

```python
from app.wordbook.cloze import Segment, best_example, build_set, line_for
from app.wordbook.index import DictionaryStore
from tests.wordbook_helpers import make_data_dir


async def load(tmp_path):
    store = DictionaryStore(make_data_dir(tmp_path))
    index = await store.index("junior")
    return await store.cards("junior", list(index.keys))


async def test_best_example_prefers_exact_mid_length(tmp_path):
    c = await load(tmp_path)
    assert best_example(c["talk"]).en == "After a long talk, we decided on divorce."
    assert best_example(c["careful"]) is None


async def test_line_for_single_word_and_phrase(tmp_path):
    c = await load(tmp_path)
    line = line_for(c["go"], start=4)
    assert line.expected == ("went",) and line.lemmas == ("go",)
    assert line.segments == (Segment("text", "She "), Segment("blank", i=4, len=4),
                             Segment("text", " home early because she felt tired."))
    phrase = line_for(c["used to"])
    assert phrase.expected == ("used", "to") and phrase.lemmas == ("used", "to")
    assert [s.t for s in phrase.segments] == ["text", "blank", "text", "blank", "text"]
    assert line_for(c["careful"]) is None


async def test_line_for_rebuilds_by_sentence_id(tmp_path):
    c = await load(tmp_path)
    sid = c["talk"].examples[0].id
    assert line_for(c["talk"], sid).expected == ("talking",)
    assert line_for(c["talk"], "0" * 16) is None


async def test_build_set_numbers_blanks_and_needs_three_lines(tmp_path):
    c = await load(tmp_path)
    s = build_set([c["used to"], c["go"], c["careful"], c["child"], c["stop"]], size=5)
    assert [l.key for l in s.lines] == ["used to", "go", "child", "stop"]
    blanks = [seg.i for l in s.lines for seg in l.segments if seg.t == "blank"]
    assert blanks == [0, 1, 2, 3, 4] and len(s.set_id) == 12
    assert build_set([c["go"], c["careful"]]) is None
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 跑测试确认失败** — Run: `cd server && uv run pytest -q tests/test_wordbook_cloze.py` → FAIL

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `server/app/wordbook/cloze.py`:

```python
"""按句拼写 · 模式 A 的句组(spec §6.6):第一阶段用数据集例句,一句只挖目标词,多词词头拆成连续空。纯函数。"""
import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

from app.wordbook.entry import Example, WordCard
from app.wordbook.textnorm import find_form, fold, tokenize

MIN_LINES, MAX_LINES = 3, 6


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Segment:
    t: Literal["text", "blank"]
    v: str = ""
    i: int = -1
    len: int = 0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ClozeLine:
    key: str
    sentence_id: str
    segments: tuple[Segment, ...]
    zh: str
    expected: tuple[str, ...]
    lemmas: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ClozeSet:
    set_id: str
    lines: tuple[ClozeLine, ...]


def _rank(example: Example, key: str) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    span = find_form(example.en, key)
    n = len(tokenize(example.en))
    exact = span is not None and fold(span[2]) == key
    return (0 if exact else 1, 0 if 8 <= n <= 16 else 1, abs(n - 12))


def best_example(card: WordCard) -> Example | None:
    usable = [e for e in card.examples if find_form(e.en, card.key)]
    return min(usable, key=lambda e: _rank(e, card.key)) if usable else None


def line_for(card: WordCard, sentence_id: str | None = None, *, start: int = 0) -> ClozeLine | None:
    if not card.spellable:
        return None
    example = best_example(card) if sentence_id is None else next(
        (e for e in card.examples if e.id == sentence_id), None)
    if example is None:
        return None
    span = find_form(example.en, card.key)
    if span is None:
        return None
    begin, end, surface = span
    words = tuple(surface.split())
    segments: list[Segment] = [Segment("text", example.en[:begin])] if begin else []
    for j, word in enumerate(words):
        if j:
            segments.append(Segment("text", " "))
        segments.append(Segment("blank", i=start + j, len=len(word)))
    if end < len(example.en):
        segments.append(Segment("text", example.en[end:]))
    return ClozeLine(card.key, example.id, tuple(segments), example.zh, words, tuple(card.key.split()))


def set_id_for(lines: list[ClozeLine] | tuple[ClozeLine, ...]) -> str:
    raw = "|".join(f"{line.key}:{line.sentence_id}" for line in lines)
    return hashlib.sha1(raw.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:12]


def build_set(cards: list[WordCard], *, size: int = 5) -> ClozeSet | None:
    size = max(MIN_LINES, min(size, MAX_LINES))
    lines: list[ClozeLine] = []
    used: set[str] = set()
    next_blank = 0
    for card in cards:
        line = line_for(card, start=next_blank)
        if line is None or line.sentence_id in used:
            continue
        lines.append(line)
        used.add(line.sentence_id)
        next_blank += len(line.expected)
        if len(lines) == size:
            break
    if len(lines) < MIN_LINES:
        return None
    return ClozeSet(set_id_for(lines), tuple(lines))
```

(`multiword` 的 `lemmas` 按词拆开;最后一词允许变形,所以判分用对应位置的 lemma。)

### Task S1: `plan.py`、`store.py`、生词本 `scope` 与配额只计非词书行

**Files:**
- Create: `server/app/wordbook/plan.py`、`server/app/wordbook/store.py`
- Modify: `server/app/routers/vocabulary.py`
- Test: `server/tests/test_wordbook_plan.py`、`server/tests/test_wordbook_store.py`、`server/tests/test_vocabulary_scope.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces(`plan.py`):`MASTERED_BOX = 4`、`BACKLOG_NEW = 5`、`Known(item_id: str, box: int, due_at: datetime, reps: int, source: str)`、`DayPlan(new_target, new_done, new_left, review_due, review_done, review_left, backlog_guard)`、`Progress(words, learned, mastered, remaining)`、`compute_plan(*, daily_new, review_cap, new_done, review_done, review_due) -> DayPlan`、`progress_of(keys, known) -> Progress`、`due_keys(keys, known, now, limit) -> list[str]`(最早到期、盒小在前)、`count_due_before(keys, known, when) -> int`、`day_end(now, offset_minutes, days=0) -> datetime`、`permutation(n, seed, mode) -> list[int]`、`next_new(keys, order, cursor, known, n) -> tuple[list[str], int]`(返回新词与「第一个未学位置」)
- Produces(`store.py`):`ANSWERS_DAILY_MAX = 3000`、`REPORTS_DAILY_MAX = 20`、`wordbook_source(book_id) -> str`、`known_words(session, user_id) -> dict[str, Known]`、`known_one(session, user_id, word) -> Known | None`、`enrollments(session, user_id) -> list[WordbookEnrollment]`、`active_enrollment(session, user_id) -> WordbookEnrollment | None`、`enroll(session, user_id, book_id, *, daily_new: int | None, review_cap: int | None, order_mode: str | None, now, seed) -> WordbookEnrollment`(提交)、`create_item(session, user_id, book_id, card, *, box, due_at, now) -> str`(flush,不提交)、`add_event(session, *, user_id, book_id, kind, word, item_id, qtype, outcome, credit, day, now) -> None`、`today_counts(session, user_id, book_id, day) -> tuple[int, int]`、`today_learned_keys(session, user_id, book_id, day) -> list[str]`、`consume_answer(session, user_id, day) -> bool`、`consume_report(session, user_id, day) -> bool`、`set_cursor(session, user_id, book_id, cursor) -> None`、`load_hidden(session, ds: DictionaryStore) -> None`
- Produces(生词本):`GET /api/vocabulary?scope=mine|all`(默认 `mine`,`due_count` 同口径);`max_vocab_items` 只数 `source NOT LIKE 'wordbook:%'`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`server/tests/test_wordbook_plan.py`:

```python
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from app.wordbook.plan import (Known, compute_plan, count_due_before, day_end, due_keys, next_new, permutation,
                               progress_of)

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 10, 9, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def k(box, days=0, reps=1):
    return Known("id", box, NOW + timedelta(days=days), reps, "wordbook:junior")


def test_compute_plan_caps_and_backlog_guard():
    p = compute_plan(daily_new=20, review_cap=100, new_done=3, review_done=10, review_due=40)
    assert (p.new_left, p.review_left, p.backlog_guard) == (17, 40, False)
    p = compute_plan(daily_new=20, review_cap=100, new_done=0, review_done=95, review_due=140)
    assert (p.new_left, p.review_left, p.backlog_guard) == (5, 5, True)
    assert compute_plan(daily_new=20, review_cap=100, new_done=25, review_done=0, review_due=0).new_left == 0


def test_progress_and_due_order():
    keys = ("a", "b", "c", "d")
    known = {"a": k(4, -1), "b": k(0, -3), "c": k(1, 2), "x": k(5, -9)}
    assert progress_of(keys, known).__dict__ == {"words": 4, "learned": 3, "mastered": 1, "remaining": 1}
    assert due_keys(keys, known, NOW, 10) == ["b", "a"] and due_keys(keys, known, NOW, 1) == ["b"]
    assert count_due_before(keys, known, day_end(NOW, 480, 1)) == 2  # c 在后天到期


def test_day_end_uses_local_midnight():
    assert day_end(NOW, 480) == datetime(2026, 10, 10, 16, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)  # 北京时间 10-11 00:00
    assert day_end(NOW, 480, 1) == datetime(2026, 10, 11, 16, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    assert day_end(datetime(2026, 10, 10, 20, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc), 480) == datetime(2026, 10, 11, 16, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def test_permutation_and_next_new():
    assert permutation(5, 1, "book") == [0, 1, 2, 3, 4]
    shuffled = permutation(50, 7, "shuffle")
    assert sorted(shuffled) == list(range(50)) and shuffled == permutation(50, 7, "shuffle") != list(range(50))
    keys = ("a", "b", "c", "d", "e")
    assert next_new(keys, [0, 1, 2, 3, 4], 0, {"a": k(0), "c": k(0)}, 2) == (["b", "d"], 1)
    assert next_new(keys, [0, 1, 2, 3, 4], 0, {"a": k(0), "b": k(0)}, 0) == ([], 2)
```

`server/tests/test_wordbook_store.py`:

```python
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from app.models import User, VocabularyItem
from app.wordbook import store
from app.wordbook.index import DictionaryStore
from tests.wordbook_helpers import make_data_dir

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 10, 9, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


async def test_enroll_pauses_others_and_keeps_progress(app):
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as s:
        s.add(User(id=7, username="lily", password_hash="x"))
        await s.commit()
        a = await store.enroll(s, 7, "cet4", daily_new=20, review_cap=100, order_mode=None, now=NOW, seed=11)
        assert (a.status, a.order_mode, a.seed) == ("active", "shuffle", 11)
        a.cursor = 9
        await s.commit()
        await store.enroll(s, 7, "junior", daily_new=10, review_cap=50, order_mode="book", now=NOW, seed=12)
        again = await store.enroll(s, 7, "cet4", daily_new=None, review_cap=None, order_mode=None, now=NOW, seed=99)
        assert (again.status, again.cursor, again.seed, again.daily_new) == ("active", 9, 11, 20)
        rows = {e.book_id: e.status for e in await store.enrollments(s, 7)}
        assert rows == {"cet4": "active", "junior": "paused"}
        assert (await store.active_enrollment(s, 7)).book_id == "cet4"


async def test_items_events_counts_and_answer_cap(app, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    ds = DictionaryStore(make_data_dir(tmp_path))
    card = await ds.card("junior", "talk")
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as s:
        s.add(User(id=7, username="lily", password_hash="x"))
        await s.commit()
        item_id = await store.create_item(s, 7, "junior", card, box=0, due_at=NOW + timedelta(days=1), now=NOW)
        for kind, word in (("learn", "talk"), ("learn", "talk"), ("review", "talk"), ("practice", "talk")):
            await store.add_event(s, user_id=7, book_id="junior", kind=kind, word=word, item_id=item_id, qtype="meaning",
                                  outcome="correct", credit=100, day="2026-10-10", now=NOW)
        await s.commit()
        row = await s.get(VocabularyItem, item_id)
        assert (row.source, row.word, row.box) == ("wordbook:junior", "talk", 0)
        assert row.meaning.startswith("v. 说话；谈话") and row.example.startswith("I could hear")
        assert await store.today_counts(s, 7, "junior", "2026-10-10") == (1, 1)
        assert await store.today_learned_keys(s, 7, "junior", "2026-10-10") == ["talk"]
        assert (await store.known_one(s, 7, "talk")).item_id == item_id
        monkeypatch.setattr(store, "ANSWERS_DAILY_MAX", 1)
        assert await store.consume_answer(s, 7, "2026-10-10") is True
        assert await store.consume_answer(s, 7, "2026-10-10") is False


async def test_load_hidden_marks_store(app, tmp_path):
    from app.models import WordbookHiddenSentence
    ds = DictionaryStore(make_data_dir(tmp_path))
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as s:
        s.add(WordbookHiddenSentence(sentence_id="a" * 16, book_id="junior", hidden_by="admin", created_at=NOW))
        await s.commit()
        await store.load_hidden(s, ds)
    assert ds.hidden == frozenset({"a" * 16}) and ds.hidden_loaded
```

`server/tests/test_vocabulary_scope.py`(用户 id 不是自增的,从注册响应里取):

```python
import uuid

import pytest

from app.models import VocabularyItem, utcnow


async def register(client):
    r = await client.post("/api/users", json={"username": "alice", "password": "password123"})
    return int(r.json()["id"]), {"Authorization": f"Bearer {r.json()['access_token']}"}


async def seed(app, user_id, n_wordbook=3):
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as s:
        for i in range(n_wordbook):
            s.add(VocabularyItem(id=str(uuid.uuid4()), user_id=user_id, word=f"w{i}", meaning="", example=None,
                                 source="wordbook:cet4", box=0, due_at=utcnow(), reps=0, lapses=0, created_at=utcnow()))
        await s.commit()


async def test_scope_filters_wordbook_rows_and_due_count(client, app):
    uid, h = await register(client)
    await client.post("/api/vocabulary", headers=h, json={"word": "mine", "source": "manual"})
    await seed(app, uid)
    mine = (await client.get("/api/vocabulary", headers=h)).json()
    every = (await client.get("/api/vocabulary?scope=all", headers=h)).json()
    assert [i["word"] for i in mine["vocabulary_items"]] == ["mine"] and mine["due_count"] == 1
    assert len(every["vocabulary_items"]) == 4 and every["due_count"] == 4
    assert (await client.get("/api/vocabulary?scope=bogus", headers=h)).status_code == 422


@pytest.mark.parametrize("settings_overrides", [{"max_vocab_items": 1}], indirect=True)
async def test_wordbook_rows_do_not_count_toward_the_limit(client, app):
    uid, h = await register(client)
    await seed(app, uid, n_wordbook=5)
    assert (await client.post("/api/vocabulary", headers=h, json={"word": "one"})).status_code == 200
    r = await client.post("/api/vocabulary", headers=h, json={"word": "two"})
    assert r.status_code == 429 and r.json() == {"error": "Vocabulary limit reached"}
```

`server/app/wordbook/plan.py`:

```python
"""每日计划、进度、洗牌排列与下一批新词(spec §6.1–6.2)。纯函数。"""
import random
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

MASTERED_BOX = 4
BACKLOG_NEW = 5


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Known:
    item_id: str
    box: int
    due_at: datetime
    reps: int
    source: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DayPlan:
    new_target: int
    new_done: int
    new_left: int
    review_due: int
    review_done: int
    review_left: int
    backlog_guard: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Progress:
    words: int
    learned: int
    mastered: int
    remaining: int


def compute_plan(*, daily_new: int, review_cap: int, new_done: int, review_done: int, review_due: int) -> DayPlan:
    guard = review_due > review_cap
    new_left = max(0, daily_new - new_done)
    if guard:
        new_left = min(new_left, BACKLOG_NEW)
    review_left = min(review_due, max(0, review_cap - review_done))
    return DayPlan(daily_new, new_done, new_left, review_due, review_done, review_left, guard)


def progress_of(keys: Sequence[str], known: Mapping[str, Known]) -> Progress:
    learned = [k for k in keys if k in known]
    mastered = sum(1 for k in learned if known[k].box >= MASTERED_BOX)
    return Progress(len(keys), len(learned), mastered, len(keys) - len(learned))


def due_keys(keys: Sequence[str], known: Mapping[str, Known], now: datetime, limit: int) -> list[str]:
    due = [k for k in keys if k in known and known[k].due_at <= now]
    due.sort(key=lambda k: (known[k].due_at, known[k].box))
    return due[: max(0, limit)]


def count_due_before(keys: Sequence[str], known: Mapping[str, Known], when: datetime) -> int:
    return sum(1 for k in keys if k in known and known[k].due_at < when)


def day_end(now: datetime, offset_minutes: int, days: int = 0) -> datetime:
    local = now.astimezone(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=offset_minutes)
    midnight = datetime(local.year, local.month, local.day, tzinfo=timezone.utc) + timedelta(days=days + 1)
    return midnight - timedelta(minutes=offset_minutes)


def permutation(n: int, seed: int, mode: str) -> list[int]:
    order = list(range(n))
    if mode == "shuffle":
        random.Random(seed).shuffle(order)
    return order


def next_new(keys: Sequence[str], order: Sequence[int], cursor: int, known: Mapping[str, Known],
             n: int) -> tuple[list[str], int]:
    pos = max(0, cursor)
    while pos < len(order) and keys[order[pos]] in known:
        pos += 1
    first_unlearned = pos
    out: list[str] = []
    while pos < len(order) and len(out) < n:
        key = keys[order[pos]]
        if key not in known:
            out.append(key)
        pos += 1
    return out, first_unlearned
```

`server/app/wordbook/store.py`:

```python
"""背单词数据层(spec §5)。除 enroll 外不提交;提交由 service 负责。"""
import uuid
from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.models import VocabularyItem, WordbookEnrollment, WordbookEvent, WordbookHiddenSentence
from app.services import usage
from app.services.stats import as_utc
from app.services.vocabulary import MAX_EXAMPLE, MAX_MEANING
from app.wordbook.entry import WordCard
from app.wordbook.index import DictionaryStore
from app.wordbook.plan import Known

ANSWERS_DAILY_MAX = 3000
REPORTS_DAILY_MAX = 20


def wordbook_source(book_id: str) -> str:
    return f"wordbook:{book_id}"


def _known(row) -> Known:
    return Known(row.id, row.box, as_utc(row.due_at), row.reps, row.source)


async def known_words(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int) -> dict[str, Known]:
    rows = (await session.execute(select(VocabularyItem.word, VocabularyItem.id, VocabularyItem.box, VocabularyItem.due_at,
                                         VocabularyItem.reps, VocabularyItem.source)
                                  .where(VocabularyItem.user_id == user_id)
                                  .order_by(VocabularyItem.created_at))).all()
    out: dict[str, Known] = {}
    for row in rows:
        out.setdefault(row.word, _known(row))  # R17:重复行时取最早的一行
    return out


async def known_one(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int, word: str) -> Known | None:
    row = (await session.execute(select(VocabularyItem.word, VocabularyItem.id, VocabularyItem.box, VocabularyItem.due_at,
                                        VocabularyItem.reps, VocabularyItem.source)
                                 .where(VocabularyItem.user_id == user_id, VocabularyItem.word == word)
                                 .order_by(VocabularyItem.created_at).limit(1))).first()
    return _known(row) if row else None


async def enrollments(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int) -> list[WordbookEnrollment]:
    return list((await session.scalars(select(WordbookEnrollment).where(WordbookEnrollment.user_id == user_id)
                                       .order_by(WordbookEnrollment.started_at))).all())


async def active_enrollment(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int) -> WordbookEnrollment | None:
    return await session.scalar(select(WordbookEnrollment).where(
        WordbookEnrollment.user_id == user_id, WordbookEnrollment.status == "active").limit(1))


async def enroll(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int, book_id: str, *, daily_new: int | None, review_cap: int | None,
                 order_mode: str | None, now: datetime, seed: int) -> WordbookEnrollment:
    rows = await enrollments(session, user_id)
    for row in rows:
        if row.book_id != book_id and row.status == "active":
            row.status, row.updated_at = "paused", now
    found = next((r for r in rows if r.book_id == book_id), None)
    if found is None:
        found = WordbookEnrollment(user_id=user_id, book_id=book_id, status="active", order_mode=order_mode or "shuffle",
                                   seed=seed, cursor=0, daily_new=daily_new or 20, review_cap=review_cap or 100,
                                   started_at=now, updated_at=now)
        session.add(found)
    else:
        found.status = "active"
        if order_mode and order_mode != found.order_mode:
            found.order_mode, found.cursor = order_mode, 0
        if daily_new is not None:
            found.daily_new = daily_new
        if review_cap is not None:
            found.review_cap = review_cap
        found.updated_at = now
    await session.commit()
    return found


async def create_item(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int, book_id: str, card: WordCard, *, box: int,
                      due_at: datetime, now: datetime) -> str:
    meaning = "；".join(f"{s.pos}. {s.text}" if s.pos else s.text for s in card.senses[:3])[:MAX_MEANING]
    example = card.examples[0].en[:MAX_EXAMPLE] if card.examples else None
    row = VocabularyItem(id=str(uuid.uuid4()), user_id=user_id, word=card.key, meaning=meaning, example=example,
                         source=wordbook_source(book_id), box=box, due_at=due_at, reps=0, lapses=0,
                         last_reviewed_at=None, created_at=now)
    session.add(row)
    await session.flush()
    return row.id


async def add_event(session: AsyncSession, *, user_id: int, book_id: str, kind: str, word: str, item_id: str | None,
                    qtype: str | None, outcome: str, credit: int, day: str, now: datetime) -> None:
    session.add(WordbookEvent(user_id=user_id, book_id=book_id, kind=kind, word=word[:100], item_id=item_id,
                              qtype=qtype, outcome=outcome, credit=credit, day=day, created_at=now))


def _today(user_id: int, book_id: str, day: str):
    return WordbookEvent.user_id == user_id, WordbookEvent.book_id == book_id, WordbookEvent.day == day


async def today_counts(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int, book_id: str, day: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    learned = await session.scalar(select(func.count(func.distinct(WordbookEvent.word))).where(
        *_today(user_id, book_id, day), WordbookEvent.kind == "learn"))
    reviews = await session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(WordbookEvent).where(
        *_today(user_id, book_id, day), WordbookEvent.kind == "review"))
    return int(learned or 0), int(reviews or 0)


async def today_learned_keys(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int, book_id: str, day: str) -> list[str]:
    rows = await session.scalars(select(WordbookEvent.word).where(*_today(user_id, book_id, day),
                                                                  WordbookEvent.kind == "learn")
                                 .order_by(WordbookEvent.id))
    return list(dict.fromkeys(rows.all()))


async def consume_answer(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int, day: str) -> bool:
    return await usage.consume(session, "wordbook_answers", str(user_id), day, ANSWERS_DAILY_MAX)


async def consume_report(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int, day: str) -> bool:
    return await usage.consume(session, "wordbook_reports", str(user_id), day, REPORTS_DAILY_MAX)


async def set_cursor(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int, book_id: str, cursor: int) -> None:
    row = await session.get(WordbookEnrollment, (user_id, book_id))
    if row is not None and cursor > row.cursor:
        row.cursor = cursor


async def load_hidden(session: AsyncSession, ds: DictionaryStore) -> None:
    ds.set_hidden((await session.scalars(select(WordbookHiddenSentence.sentence_id))).all())
```

```python
import pytest

from tests.wordbook_helpers import enable_wordbook, make_data_dir

URL = "/api/wordbook"


async def login(app, client, tmp_path, **overrides):
    enable_wordbook(app, make_data_dir(tmp_path), **overrides)
    r = await client.post("/api/users", json={"username": "alice", "password": "password123"})
    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {r.json()['access_token']}"}


DISABLED_PATHS = [("GET", "/books"), ("PUT", "/enrollment"), ("GET", "/today")]  # S3–S5 各自追加自己的路径


@pytest.mark.parametrize("method,path", DISABLED_PATHS)
async def test_disabled_routes_404(client, auth_headers, method, path):
    r = await client.request(method, URL + path, headers=auth_headers, json={})
    assert r.status_code == 404 and r.json() == {"error": "Wordbook is disabled"}


async def test_config_lists_enabled_books_with_availability(app, client, tmp_path):
    h = await login(app, client, tmp_path, wordbook_books="junior,toefl")
    body = (await client.get(f"{URL}/config", headers=h)).json()
    assert body["enabled"] is True and body["defaults"] == {"daily_new": 20, "review_cap": 100}
    assert [(b["id"], b["available"]) for b in body["books"]] == [("junior", True), ("toefl", False)]
    assert body["ai_available"] is False


async def test_enroll_today_and_books(app, client, tmp_path):
    h = await login(app, client, tmp_path, wordbook_books="junior,senior")
    await client.post("/api/vocabulary", headers=h, json={"word": "accept", "source": "lookup"})
    empty = (await client.get(f"{URL}/today", headers=h, params={"tz_offset_minutes": 480})).json()
    assert empty == {"enrollment": None, "book": None, "plan": None, "progress": None, "tomorrow_due": 0}
    r = await client.put(f"{URL}/enrollment", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "order_mode": "book", "daily_new": 10})
    assert r.status_code == 200 and r.json()["status"] == "active" and r.json()["daily_new"] == 10
    today = (await client.get(f"{URL}/today", headers=h, params={"tz_offset_minutes": 480})).json()
    assert today["book"]["id"] == "junior"
    assert today["progress"] == {"words": 14, "learned": 1, "mastered": 0, "remaining": 13}
    assert today["plan"] == {"new_target": 10, "new_done": 0, "new_left": 10, "review_due": 1, "review_done": 0,
                             "review_left": 1, "backlog_guard": False}
    books = (await client.get(f"{URL}/books", headers=h)).json()["books"]
    assert [(b["id"], b["enrolled"], b["active"], b["learned"]) for b in books] == [
        ("junior", True, True, 1), ("senior", False, False, 0)]


async def test_enrollment_errors(app, client, tmp_path):
    h = await login(app, client, tmp_path, wordbook_books="junior,senior")
    r = await client.put(f"{URL}/enrollment", headers=h, json={"book_id": "toefl"})
    assert r.status_code == 404 and r.json() == {"error": "Book not found"}
    assert (await client.put(f"{URL}/enrollment", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "daily_new": 1})).status_code == 422
    await client.put(f"{URL}/enrollment", headers=h, json={"book_id": "senior"})
    r = await client.get(f"{URL}/today", headers=h)
    assert r.status_code == 503 and r.json() == {"error": "Wordbook data unavailable"}


async def test_disabling_the_active_book_keeps_enrollment(app, client, tmp_path):
    h = await login(app, client, tmp_path)
    await client.put(f"{URL}/enrollment", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior"})
    app.state.settings = app.state.settings.model_copy(update={"wordbook_books": "senior"})
    today = (await client.get(f"{URL}/today", headers=h)).json()
    assert today["enrollment"]["book_id"] == "junior" and today["book"] is None and today["plan"] is None
```

`server/app/wordbook/service.py`(替换 F3 版本;`config_out` 不变,移到文件中部):

```python
"""背单词编排(spec §6、§7)。路由只做校验与错误映射;这里组合 index / store / 纯模块。"""
from dataclasses import asdict

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.models import WordbookEnrollment, iso_utc
from app.services.usage import local_day
from app.wordbook import plan as planning
from app.wordbook import store
from app.wordbook.books import BOOKS, BookSpec, book_by_id, enabled_books
from app.wordbook.entry import WordCard
from app.wordbook.index import BookIndex, DataUnavailable, DictionaryStore
from app.wordbook.plan import Known


class WordbookError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, status: int, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status, self.message = status, message


BOOK_NOT_FOUND = (404, "Book not found")
DATA_UNAVAILABLE = (503, "Wordbook data unavailable")
NO_ACTIVE = (409, "No active book")
WORD_NOT_FOUND = (404, "Word not found")
SENTENCE_CHANGED = (422, "Sentence changed")
ANSWER_LIMIT = (429, "Daily answer limit reached")
REPORT_LIMIT = (429, "Daily report limit reached")


def fail(error: tuple[int, str]):
    raise WordbookError(*error)


def book_or_fail(settings, book_id: str) -> BookSpec:
    if book_id not in enabled_books(settings.wordbook_books):
        fail(BOOK_NOT_FOUND)
    return book_by_id(book_id)


async def index_or_fail(ds: DictionaryStore, book_id: str) -> BookIndex:
    try:
        return await ds.index(book_id)
    except DataUnavailable:
        fail(DATA_UNAVAILABLE)


async def cards_or_fail(ds: DictionaryStore, book_id: str, keys: list[str]) -> dict[str, WordCard]:
    try:
        return await ds.cards(book_id, keys)
    except DataUnavailable:
        fail(DATA_UNAVAILABLE)


async def ensure_hidden(session: AsyncSession, ds: DictionaryStore) -> None:
    if not ds.hidden_loaded:
        await store.load_hidden(session, ds)


def config_out(settings) -> dict:
    data_dir = settings.resolved_wordbook_data_dir
    enabled = enabled_books(settings.wordbook_books)
    return {
        "enabled": True,
        "ai_available": settings.wordbook_ai_available,
        "defaults": {"daily_new": settings.wordbook_daily_new, "review_cap": settings.wordbook_review_cap},
        "books": [book_out(b, available=(data_dir / b.file).is_file()) for b in BOOKS if b.id in enabled],
    }


def book_out(b: BookSpec, **extra) -> dict:
    return {"id": b.id, "title_zh": b.title_zh, "title_en": b.title_en, "level": b.level, "words": b.unique, **extra}


def card_out(card: WordCard) -> dict:
    return {"key": card.key, "word": card.word, "us": card.us, "uk": card.uk,
            "senses": [asdict(s) for s in card.senses],
            "phrases": [{"en": en, "zh": zh} for en, zh in card.phrases],
            "examples": [asdict(e) for e in card.examples]}


def item_out(known: Known | None) -> dict | None:
    return None if known is None else {"box": known.box, "due_at": iso_utc(known.due_at)}


def enrollment_out(e: WordbookEnrollment) -> dict:
    return {"book_id": e.book_id, "status": e.status, "order_mode": e.order_mode, "daily_new": e.daily_new,
            "review_cap": e.review_cap, "started_at": iso_utc(e.started_at)}


async def books_out(session: AsyncSession, ds: DictionaryStore, user_id: int, settings) -> dict:
    known = await store.known_words(session, user_id)
    rows = {e.book_id: e for e in await store.enrollments(session, user_id)}
    out = []
    for b in BOOKS:
        if b.id not in enabled_books(settings.wordbook_books):
            continue
        try:
            index = await ds.index(b.id)
            progress = planning.progress_of(index.keys, known)
            words, learned, mastered = progress.words, progress.learned, progress.mastered
        except DataUnavailable:
            words, learned, mastered = b.unique, 0, 0
        e = rows.get(b.id)
        out.append({**book_out(b), "words": words, "learned": learned, "mastered": mastered,
                    "enrolled": e is not None, "active": e is not None and e.status == "active"})
    return {"books": out}


async def put_enrollment(session: AsyncSession, user_id: int, settings, *, book_id: str, daily_new: int | None,
                         review_cap: int | None, order_mode: str | None, now, seed: int) -> dict:
    book_or_fail(settings, book_id)
    existing = await store.enrollments(session, user_id)
    fresh = all(e.book_id != book_id for e in existing)
    e = await store.enroll(session, user_id, book_id,
                           daily_new=daily_new if daily_new is not None else (settings.wordbook_daily_new if fresh else None),
                           review_cap=review_cap if review_cap is not None else (settings.wordbook_review_cap if fresh else None),
                           order_mode=order_mode, now=now, seed=seed)
    return enrollment_out(e)


async def today_out(session: AsyncSession, ds: DictionaryStore, user_id: int, settings, offset: int, now) -> dict:
    e = await store.active_enrollment(session, user_id)
    empty = {"enrollment": enrollment_out(e) if e else None, "book": None, "plan": None, "progress": None,
             "tomorrow_due": 0}
    if e is None or e.book_id not in enabled_books(settings.wordbook_books):
        return empty
    index = await index_or_fail(ds, e.book_id)
    known = await store.known_words(session, user_id)
    new_done, review_done = await store.today_counts(session, user_id, e.book_id, local_day(now, offset))
    due = sum(1 for k in index.keys if k in known and known[k].due_at <= now)
    day_plan = planning.compute_plan(daily_new=e.daily_new, review_cap=e.review_cap, new_done=new_done,
                                     review_done=review_done, review_due=due)
    return {"enrollment": enrollment_out(e), "book": book_out(book_by_id(e.book_id), available=True),
            "plan": asdict(day_plan), "progress": asdict(planning.progress_of(index.keys, known)),
            "tomorrow_due": planning.count_due_before(index.keys, known, planning.day_end(now, offset, 1))}
```

`server/app/routers/wordbook.py`(在 F3 骨架上扩展;S3–S5 继续追加端点):

```python
"""背单词接口(设计文档 2026-10-10-wordbook §7)。业务在 app/wordbook/service.py。"""
import secrets
from typing import Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.config import Settings
from app.core.deps import current_user, get_session, get_settings
from app.models import User, utcnow
from app.wordbook import service
from app.wordbook.index import dictionary

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/wordbook")
DISABLED = "Wordbook is disabled"
TZ = Query(0, ge=-840, le=840)


def require_wordbook(settings: Settings = Depends(get_settings)) -> Settings:
    if not settings.wordbook_enabled:
        raise HTTPException(404, DISABLED)
    return settings


def _raise(e: service.WordbookError):
    raise HTTPException(e.status, e.message) from None


class EnrollmentIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    book_id: str = Field(max_length=16)
    daily_new: int | None = Field(None, ge=5, le=100)
    review_cap: int | None = Field(None, ge=20, le=500)
    order_mode: Literal["shuffle", "book"] | None = None


@router.get("/config")
async def wordbook_config(_: User = Depends(current_user), settings: Settings = Depends(get_settings)):
    return service.config_out(settings) if settings.wordbook_enabled else {"enabled": False}


@router.get("/books")
async def wordbook_books(request: Request, user: User = Depends(current_user),
                         session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session), settings: Settings = Depends(require_wordbook)):
    return await service.books_out(session, dictionary(request.app), user.id, settings)


@router.put("/enrollment")
async def wordbook_enroll(payload: EnrollmentIn, user: User = Depends(current_user),
                          session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session), settings: Settings = Depends(require_wordbook)):
    try:
        return await service.put_enrollment(session, user.id, settings, book_id=payload.book_id,
                                            daily_new=payload.daily_new, review_cap=payload.review_cap,
                                            order_mode=payload.order_mode, now=utcnow(),
                                            seed=secrets.randbelow(2**31))
    except service.WordbookError as e:
        _raise(e)


@router.get("/today")
async def wordbook_today(request: Request, tz_offset_minutes: int = TZ, user: User = Depends(current_user),
                         session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session), settings: Settings = Depends(require_wordbook)):
    try:
        return await service.today_out(session, dictionary(request.app), user.id, settings, tz_offset_minutes, utcnow())
    except service.WordbookError as e:
        _raise(e)
```

(依赖顺序:FastAPI 先解析 `current_user` 再解析 `require_wordbook`;未登录 401 优先于 404,与 `test_wordbook_models` 的断言一致。S3/S4/S5 各自把新路径追加进 `DISABLED_PATHS`。)

```python
from datetime import timedelta

from sqlalchemy import select, update

from app.models import VocabularyItem, WordbookEvent, utcnow
from app.wordbook import store
from tests.wordbook_helpers import enable_wordbook, make_data_dir

URL = "/api/wordbook"
TALK_MEANING = "v. 说话；谈话；n. 交谈"


async def login(app, client, tmp_path, **overrides):
    enable_wordbook(app, make_data_dir(tmp_path), **overrides)
    r = await client.post("/api/users", json={"username": "alice", "password": "password123"})
    h = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {r.json()['access_token']}"}
    await client.put(f"{URL}/enrollment", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "order_mode": "book"})
    return h


async def ans(client, h, **body):
    return await client.post(f"{URL}/answers", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "tz_offset_minutes": 480, **body})


async def test_learn_batch_returns_cards_and_meaning_quiz(app, client, tmp_path):
    h = await login(app, client, tmp_path)
    body = (await client.post(f"{URL}/learn/batch", headers=h, json={"size": 5})).json()
    assert [c["key"] for c in body["cards"]] == ["talk", "ability", "able", "abandon", "absorb"]
    assert body["cards"][0]["us"] == "tɔk" and body["cards"][0]["examples"][0]["id"]
    q = body["quiz"][0]
    assert q["qtype"] == "meaning" and q["key"] == "talk" and TALK_MEANING in q["options"] and len(q["options"]) == 4


async def test_learn_answer_creates_item_once_and_advances_cursor(app, client, tmp_path):
    h = await login(app, client, tmp_path)
    r = (await ans(client, h, key="talk", mode="learn", qtype="meaning", choice=TALK_MEANING)).json()
    assert (r["correct"], r["outcome"], r["grade"], r["applied"], r["item"]["box"]) == (True, "correct", "good", True, 0)
    again = (await ans(client, h, key="talk", mode="learn", qtype="meaning", choice="x")).json()
    assert (again["correct"], again["applied"]) == (False, False)
    known = (await ans(client, h, key="ability", mode="learn", qtype="meaning", known=True)).json()
    assert known["outcome"] == "known" and known["item"]["box"] == 2
    nxt = (await client.post(f"{URL}/learn/batch", headers=h, json={"size": 2})).json()
    assert [c["key"] for c in nxt["cards"]] == ["able", "abandon"]
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as s:
        kinds = (await s.scalars(select(WordbookEvent.kind).order_by(WordbookEvent.id))).all()
        assert kinds == ["learn", "practice", "learn"]
        rows = (await s.scalars(select(VocabularyItem.source))).all()
        assert set(rows) == {"wordbook:junior"}
    today = (await client.get(f"{URL}/today", headers=h, params={"tz_offset_minutes": 480})).json()
    assert today["plan"]["new_done"] == 2 and today["progress"]["learned"] == 2


async def test_review_due_rules(app, client, tmp_path):
    h = await login(app, client, tmp_path)
    await ans(client, h, key="talk", mode="learn", qtype="meaning", choice=TALK_MEANING)
    await ans(client, h, key="go", mode="learn", qtype="meaning", choice="x")
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as s:
        await s.execute(update(VocabularyItem).where(VocabularyItem.word == "talk").values(due_at=utcnow() - timedelta(hours=1)))
        await s.commit()
    batch = (await client.post(f"{URL}/review/batch", headers=h, json={"size": 20})).json()
    assert [q["key"] for q in batch["questions"]] == ["talk"] and "talk" in batch["cards"]
    typo = (await ans(client, h, key="talk", mode="review", qtype="spell", typed="talkk")).json()
    assert (typo["outcome"], typo["grade"], typo["applied"], typo["item"]["box"]) == ("typo", "hard", True, 0)
    assert {"op": "ins", "a": "", "b": "k"} in typo["diff"]
    practice = (await ans(client, h, key="talk", mode="review", qtype="spell", typed="talk")).json()
    assert practice["applied"] is False  # 不再到期:只记练习
    wrong = (await ans(client, h, key="go", mode="review", qtype="word", choice="stop")).json()
    assert (wrong["grade"], wrong["applied"]) == ("forgot", True)  # 未到期但答错 → forgot(R-W5)
    today = (await client.get(f"{URL}/today", headers=h, params={"tz_offset_minutes": 480})).json()
    assert today["plan"]["review_done"] == 1


async def test_answer_validation_limits_and_unknown_word(app, client, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    h = await login(app, client, tmp_path)
    r = await ans(client, h, key="nope", mode="learn", qtype="meaning", choice="x")
    assert r.status_code == 404 and r.json() == {"error": "Word not found"}
    assert (await ans(client, h, key="talk", mode="learn", qtype="spell")).status_code == 422
    assert (await ans(client, h, key="talk", mode="learn", qtype="meaning", choice="x", extra=1)).status_code == 422
    monkeypatch.setattr(store, "ANSWERS_DAILY_MAX", 1)
    await ans(client, h, key="talk", mode="learn", qtype="meaning", choice=TALK_MEANING)
    r = await ans(client, h, key="able", mode="learn", qtype="meaning", choice="x")
    assert r.status_code == 429 and r.json() == {"error": "Daily answer limit reached"}


async def test_no_active_book(app, client, tmp_path):
    enable_wordbook(app, make_data_dir(tmp_path))
    r = await client.post("/api/users", json={"username": "bob", "password": "password123"})
    h = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {r.json()['access_token']}"}
    r = await client.post(f"{URL}/learn/batch", headers=h, json={})
    assert r.status_code == 409 and r.json() == {"error": "No active book"}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`service.py` 追加(导入补 `from dataclasses import dataclass`、`from datetime import timedelta`、`from app.services.vocabulary import apply_review`、`from app.wordbook.grading import grade_blank, srs_grade`、`from app.wordbook.quiz import Question, build_question, candidate_keys, check_choice, pick_distractors, pick_qtype, question_rng`):

```python
CHOICE_TYPES = ("meaning", "word", "cloze")
LEARN_DUE = timedelta(days=1)
KNOWN_BOX, KNOWN_DUE = 2, timedelta(days=4)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AnswerInput:
    book_id: str
    key: str
    mode: str
    qtype: str
    choice: str | None
    typed: str | None
    hinted: bool
    known: bool
    offset: int


def question_out(q: Question) -> dict:
    return {"key": q.key, "qtype": q.qtype, "prompt": q.prompt,
            "options": list(q.options) if q.options is not None else None, "length": q.length}


async def _active(session, ds, user_id, settings):
    e = await store.active_enrollment(session, user_id)
    if e is None:
        fail(NO_ACTIVE)
    book_or_fail(settings, e.book_id)
    await ensure_hidden(session, ds)
    return e, await index_or_fail(ds, e.book_id)


async def _question(ds, index, card, qtype, rng) -> Question:
    distractors: list[WordCard] = []
    if qtype in CHOICE_TYPES:
        pool = await cards_or_fail(ds, index.book_id, candidate_keys(index, card.key, rng))
        distractors = pick_distractors(card, list(pool.values()), qtype, rng)
    return build_question(qtype, card, distractors, rng)


async def _plan(session, user_id, e, index, known, offset, now) -> planning.DayPlan:
    new_done, review_done = await store.today_counts(session, user_id, e.book_id, local_day(now, offset))
    due = sum(1 for k in index.keys if k in known and known[k].due_at <= now)
    return planning.compute_plan(daily_new=e.daily_new, review_cap=e.review_cap, new_done=new_done,
                                 review_done=review_done, review_due=due)


async def learn_batch(session, ds, user_id: int, settings, size: int, offset: int, now) -> dict:
    e, index = await _active(session, ds, user_id, settings)
    known = await store.known_words(session, user_id)
    left = (await _plan(session, user_id, e, index, known, offset, now)).new_left
    order = planning.permutation(len(index), e.seed, e.order_mode)
    keys, _ = planning.next_new(index.keys, order, e.cursor, known, min(size, left))
    cards = await cards_or_fail(ds, e.book_id, keys)
    quiz = [question_out(await _question(ds, index, c, "meaning", question_rng(user_id, c.key, 0))) for c in cards.values()]
    return {"book_id": e.book_id, "cards": [card_out(c) for c in cards.values()], "quiz": quiz}


async def review_batch(session, ds, user_id: int, settings, size: int, offset: int, now, *, tts: bool) -> dict:
    e, index = await _active(session, ds, user_id, settings)
    known = await store.known_words(session, user_id)
    left = (await _plan(session, user_id, e, index, known, offset, now)).review_left
    keys = planning.due_keys(index.keys, known, now, min(size, left))
    cards = await cards_or_fail(ds, e.book_id, keys)
    questions = []
    for card in cards.values():
        rng = question_rng(user_id, card.key, known[card.key].reps)
        questions.append(question_out(await _question(ds, index, card, pick_qtype(known[card.key].box, card, rng, tts=tts), rng)))
    return {"book_id": e.book_id, "questions": questions, "cards": {k: card_out(c) for k, c in cards.items()}}


async def answer(session, ds, user_id: int, settings, p: AnswerInput, now) -> dict:
    book_or_fail(settings, p.book_id)
    card = (await cards_or_fail(ds, p.book_id, [p.key])).get(p.key)
    if card is None:
        fail(WORD_NOT_FOUND)
    if not p.known and p.qtype in CHOICE_TYPES and p.choice is None:
        raise WordbookError(422, "Choice is required")
    if not p.known and p.qtype not in CHOICE_TYPES and p.typed is None:
        raise WordbookError(422, "Answer is required")
    day = local_day(now, p.offset)
    if not await store.consume_answer(session, user_id, day):
        fail(ANSWER_LIMIT)
    diff = None
    if p.known:
        outcome, credit = "known", 100
    elif p.qtype in CHOICE_TYPES:
        outcome = "correct" if check_choice(p.qtype, card, p.choice) else "wrong"
        credit = 100 if outcome == "correct" else 0
    else:
        result = grade_blank(card.word, card.key, p.typed, hinted=p.hinted)
        outcome, credit = result.outcome, result.credit
        diff = [asdict(d) for d in result.diff] or None
    grade = "good" if outcome == "known" else srs_grade(outcome, p.hinted)
    row = await store.known_one(session, user_id, card.key)
    applied, item_id, kind = False, row.item_id if row else None, "practice"
    if p.mode == "learn" and row is None:
        box, due = (KNOWN_BOX, now + KNOWN_DUE) if p.known else (0, now + LEARN_DUE)
        item_id = await store.create_item(session, user_id, p.book_id, card, box=box, due_at=due, now=now)
        applied, kind = True, "learn"
        e = await store.get_enrollment(session, user_id, p.book_id)
        if e is not None:
            index = await index_or_fail(ds, p.book_id)
            known = await store.known_words(session, user_id)
            _, cursor = planning.next_new(index.keys, planning.permutation(len(index), e.seed, e.order_mode),
                                          e.cursor, known, 0)
            await store.set_cursor(session, user_id, p.book_id, cursor)
    elif p.mode == "review" and row is not None:
        due = row.due_at <= now
        if due or grade == "forgot":
            applied = await apply_review(session, user_id, row.item_id, seen_box=row.box, seen_reps=row.reps,
                                         grade=grade, now=now)
        kind = "review" if due and applied else "practice"
    await store.add_event(session, user_id=user_id, book_id=p.book_id, kind=kind, word=card.key, item_id=item_id,
                          qtype=p.qtype, outcome=outcome, credit=credit, day=day, now=now)
    await session.commit()
    return {"correct": outcome in ("correct", "known"), "outcome": outcome, "grade": grade, "expected": card.word,
            "diff": diff, "item": item_out(await store.known_one(session, user_id, card.key)), "applied": applied}
```

`store.py` 追加 `get_enrollment(session, user_id, book_id) -> WordbookEnrollment | None`(`session.get(WordbookEnrollment, (user_id, book_id))`)。

`routers/wordbook.py` 追加:

```python
class LearnIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    size: int = Field(5, ge=1, le=10)
    tz_offset_minutes: int = Field(0, ge=-840, le=840)


class ReviewIn(LearnIn):
    size: int = Field(20, ge=1, le=20)


class AnswerIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    book_id: str = Field(max_length=16)
    key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=100)
    mode: Literal["learn", "review"]
    qtype: Literal["meaning", "word", "cloze", "dictation", "spell"]
    choice: str | None = Field(None, max_length=200)
    typed: str | None = Field(None, max_length=100)
    hinted: bool = False
    known: bool = False
    tz_offset_minutes: int = Field(0, ge=-840, le=840)


@router.post("/learn/batch")
async def wordbook_learn(payload: LearnIn, request: Request, user: User = Depends(current_user),
                         session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session), settings: Settings = Depends(require_wordbook)):
    try:
        return await service.learn_batch(session, dictionary(request.app), user.id, settings, payload.size,
                                         payload.tz_offset_minutes, utcnow())
    except service.WordbookError as e:
        _raise(e)


@router.post("/review/batch")
async def wordbook_review(payload: ReviewIn, request: Request, user: User = Depends(current_user),
                          session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session), settings: Settings = Depends(require_wordbook)):
    try:
        return await service.review_batch(session, dictionary(request.app), user.id, settings, payload.size,
                                          payload.tz_offset_minutes, utcnow(), tts=bool(settings.tts_model_list))
    except service.WordbookError as e:
        _raise(e)


@router.post("/answers")
async def wordbook_answer(payload: AnswerIn, request: Request, user: User = Depends(current_user),
                          session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session), settings: Settings = Depends(require_wordbook)):
    p = service.AnswerInput(payload.book_id, payload.key, payload.mode, payload.qtype, payload.choice, payload.typed,
                            payload.hinted, payload.known, payload.tz_offset_minutes)
    try:
        return await service.answer(session, dictionary(request.app), user.id, settings, p, utcnow())
    except service.WordbookError as e:
        _raise(e)
```

`key` 在路由里先过 `normalize_word`(`from app.services.vocabulary import normalize_word`;`p = AnswerInput(..., normalize_word(payload.key), ...)`),与索引键一致。
