- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试** — `server/tests/test_wordbook_spell.py`:

```python
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.models import VocabularyItem, WordbookEvent, WordbookReport
from tests.wordbook_helpers import enable_wordbook, make_data_dir

URL = "/api/wordbook"
FIVE = ["talk", "ability", "able", "abandon", "absorb"]


async def ready(app, client, tmp_path):
    enable_wordbook(app, make_data_dir(tmp_path))
    r = await client.post("/api/users", json={"username": "alice", "password": "password123"})
    h = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {r.json()['access_token']}"}
    await client.put(f"{URL}/enrollment", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "order_mode": "book"})
    for key in FIVE:
        await client.post(f"{URL}/answers", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "key": key, "mode": "learn",
                                                            "qtype": "meaning", "known": True, "tz_offset_minutes": 480})
    return h


async def test_spell_sets_use_today_words_and_hide_answers(app, client, tmp_path):
    h = await ready(app, client, tmp_path)
    body = (await client.post(f"{URL}/spell/sets", headers=h, json={"count": 1, "size": 5, "tz_offset_minutes": 480})).json()
    (s,) = body["sets"]
    assert sorted(l["key"] for l in s["lines"]) == sorted(FIVE)
    blanks = [seg for l in s["lines"] for seg in l["segments"] if seg["t"] == "blank"]
    assert sorted(b["i"] for b in blanks) == [0, 1, 2, 3, 4] and all(set(b) == {"t", "i", "len"} for b in blanks)
    text = " ".join(seg.get("v", "") for l in s["lines"] for seg in l["segments"])
    assert "abandon" not in text and "absorb" not in text


async def test_spell_submit_four_outcomes_and_srs(app, client, tmp_path):
    h = await ready(app, client, tmp_path)
    s = (await client.post(f"{URL}/spell/sets", headers=h, json={"count": 1, "size": 5})).json()["sets"][0]
    typed = {"talk": ("talk", False), "ability": ("abilty", False), "able": ("able", True),
             "abandon": ("abandoned", False), "absorb": ("xyz", False)}
    lines = [{"key": l["key"], "sentence_id": l["sentence_id"],
              "answers": [{"typed": typed[l["key"]][0], "hinted": typed[l["key"]][1]}]} for l in s["lines"]]
    r = await client.post(f"{URL}/spell/submit", headers=h,
                          json={"book_id": "junior", "set_id": s["set_id"], "lines": lines, "tz_offset_minutes": 480})
    body = r.json()
    by = {w["key"]: w for w in body["words"]}
    assert {k: (w["outcome"], w["credit"]) for k, w in by.items()} == {
        "talk": ("correct", 100), "ability": ("typo", 50), "able": ("correct", 50),
        "abandon": ("inflection", 50), "absorb": ("wrong", 0)}
    assert by["ability"]["blanks"][0]["diff"] and by["absorb"]["card"]["word"] == "absorb"
    assert (body["credit"], body["counted"]) == (50, False)
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as sess:
        absorb = (await sess.scalars(select(VocabularyItem).where(VocabularyItem.word == "absorb"))).one()
        talk = (await sess.scalars(select(VocabularyItem).where(VocabularyItem.word == "talk"))).one()
        assert (absorb.box, absorb.lapses) == (0, 1) and (talk.box, talk.reps) == (2, 0)  # 未到期:只有错误改盒
        assert "spell_set" not in (await sess.scalars(select(WordbookEvent.kind))).all()


async def test_spell_submit_counts_a_passing_set(app, client, tmp_path):
    h = await ready(app, client, tmp_path)
    s = (await client.post(f"{URL}/spell/sets", headers=h, json={"count": 1, "size": 5})).json()["sets"][0]
    lines = [{"key": l["key"], "sentence_id": l["sentence_id"], "answers": [{"typed": l["key"], "hinted": False}]}
             for l in s["lines"]]
    body = (await client.post(f"{URL}/spell/submit", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "set_id": s["set_id"],
                                                                      "lines": lines})).json()
    assert body["counted"] is True and body["credit"] >= 60
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as sess:
        assert (await sess.scalars(select(WordbookEvent.kind).where(WordbookEvent.kind == "spell_set"))).all() == ["spell_set"]


async def test_spell_submit_rejects_changed_sentences(app, client, tmp_path):
    h = await ready(app, client, tmp_path)
    bad = {"book_id": "junior", "set_id": "x", "lines": [{"key": "talk", "sentence_id": "0" * 16,
                                                          "answers": [{"typed": "talk", "hinted": False}]}]}
    r = await client.post(f"{URL}/spell/submit", headers=h, json=bad)
    assert r.status_code == 422 and r.json() == {"error": "Sentence changed"}


async def test_too_few_words_gives_no_sets(app, client, tmp_path):
    enable_wordbook(app, make_data_dir(tmp_path))
    r = await client.post("/api/users", json={"username": "bob", "password": "password123"})
    h = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {r.json()['access_token']}"}
    await client.put(f"{URL}/enrollment", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior"})
    assert (await client.post(f"{URL}/spell/sets", headers=h, json={})).json()["sets"] == []


async def test_card_and_report(app, client, tmp_path):
    h = await ready(app, client, tmp_path)
    card = (await client.get(f"{URL}/cards/junior/talk", headers=h)).json()
    assert card["word"] == "talk" and len(card["examples"]) == 3
    assert (await client.get(f"{URL}/cards/junior/nope", headers=h)).status_code == 404
    sid = card["examples"][0]["id"]
    r = await client.post(f"{URL}/reports", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "key": "talk", "sentence_id": sid,
                                                             "reason": "wrong"})
    assert r.status_code == 200 and isinstance(r.json()["id"], int)
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as sess:
        row = (await sess.scalars(select(WordbookReport))).one()
        assert (row.status, row.kind, row.sentence_id) == ("open", "sentence", sid)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `service.py` 追加(导入补 `import random`、`from app.models import WordbookReport`、`from app.wordbook.cloze import ClozeSet, build_set, line_for`、`from app.wordbook.grading import worst`):

```python
SPELL_PASS = 60
SPELL_QTYPE = "spell_ctx"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SpellLine:
    key: str
    sentence_id: str
    answers: list[tuple[str, bool]]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SpellInput:
    book_id: str
    set_id: str
    lines: list[SpellLine]
    offset: int


def set_out(s: ClozeSet) -> dict:
    def seg(x):
        return {"t": "text", "v": x.v} if x.t == "text" else {"t": "blank", "i": x.i, "len": x.len}
    return {"set_id": s.set_id, "lines": [{"key": l.key, "sentence_id": l.sentence_id,
                                           "segments": [seg(x) for x in l.segments], "zh": l.zh} for l in s.lines]}


async def spell_sets(session, ds, user_id: int, settings, count: int, size: int, offset: int, now,
                     rng: random.Random | None = None) -> dict:
    e, index = await _active(session, ds, user_id, settings)
    rng = rng or random.Random()
    known = await store.known_words(session, user_id)
    today = await store.today_learned_keys(session, user_id, e.book_id, local_day(now, offset))
    due = planning.due_keys(index.keys, known, now, 200)
    low = [k for k in index.keys if k in known and known[k].box <= 3]
    ordered = [k for k in dict.fromkeys(due + today + low) if k in index]
    head = ordered[: count * size * 2]
    rng.shuffle(head)
    cards = list((await cards_or_fail(ds, e.book_id, head + ordered[len(head): count * size * 3])).values())
    sets: list[ClozeSet] = []
    while len(sets) < count:
        built = build_set(cards, size=size)
        if built is None:
            break
        sets.append(built)
        used = {line.key for line in built.lines}
        cards = [c for c in cards if c.key not in used]
    return {"book_id": e.book_id, "sets": [set_out(s) for s in sets]}


async def spell_submit(session, ds, user_id: int, settings, p: SpellInput, now) -> dict:
    book_or_fail(settings, p.book_id)
    await ensure_hidden(session, ds)
    cards = await cards_or_fail(ds, p.book_id, [line.key for line in p.lines])
    graded = []
    for line in p.lines:
        card = cards.get(line.key)
        if card is None:
            fail(WORD_NOT_FOUND)
        cloze = line_for(card, line.sentence_id)
        if cloze is None or len(cloze.expected) != len(line.answers):
            fail(SENTENCE_CHANGED)
        graded.append((card, cloze, line))
    day = local_day(now, p.offset)
    if not await store.consume_answer(session, user_id, day):
        fail(ANSWER_LIMIT)
    known = await store.known_words(session, user_id)
    words, next_blank = [], 0
    for card, cloze, line in graded:
        results = [grade_blank(exp, lemma, typed, hinted=hinted)
                   for exp, lemma, (typed, hinted) in zip(cloze.expected, cloze.lemmas, line.answers)]
        outcome = worst(r.outcome for r in results)
        hinted = any(h for _, h in line.answers)
        credit = round(sum(r.credit for r in results) / len(results))
        row, kind = known.get(card.key), "blank"
        if row is not None:
            grade = srs_grade(outcome, hinted)
            due = row.due_at <= now
            applied = False
            if due or grade == "forgot":
                applied = await apply_review(session, user_id, row.item_id, seen_box=row.box, seen_reps=row.reps,
                                             grade=grade, now=now)
            kind = "review" if due and applied else "blank"
        await store.add_event(session, user_id=user_id, book_id=p.book_id, kind=kind, word=card.key,
                              item_id=row.item_id if row else None, qtype=SPELL_QTYPE, outcome=outcome,
                              credit=credit, day=day, now=now)
        blanks = []
        for r in results:
            blanks.append({"i": next_blank, "outcome": r.outcome, "credit": r.credit, "typed": r.typed,
                           "expected": r.expected, "diff": [asdict(d) for d in r.diff]})
            next_blank += 1
        words.append({"key": card.key, "outcome": outcome, "credit": credit, "blanks": blanks, "card": card_out(card)})
    set_credit = round(sum(w["credit"] for w in words) / len(words))
    counted = set_credit >= SPELL_PASS
    if counted:
        await store.add_event(session, user_id=user_id, book_id=p.book_id, kind="spell_set", word=p.set_id[:100],
                              item_id=None, qtype=None, outcome="done", credit=set_credit, day=day, now=now)
    await session.commit()
    fresh = await store.known_words(session, user_id)
    for w in words:
        w["item"] = item_out(fresh.get(w["key"]))
    return {"credit": set_credit, "counted": counted, "words": words}


async def card_detail(ds, settings, book_id: str, key: str) -> dict:
    book_or_fail(settings, book_id)
    card = (await cards_or_fail(ds, book_id, [key])).get(key)
    if card is None:
        fail(WORD_NOT_FOUND)
    return card_out(card)


async def report(session, ds, user_id: int, settings, *, book_id: str, key: str, sentence_id: str, reason: str,
                 offset: int, now) -> dict:
    book_or_fail(settings, book_id)
    card = (await cards_or_fail(ds, book_id, [key])).get(key)
    if card is None:
        fail(WORD_NOT_FOUND)
    if all(e.id != sentence_id for e in card.examples):
        fail(SENTENCE_CHANGED)
    if not await store.consume_report(session, user_id, local_day(now, offset)):
        fail(REPORT_LIMIT)
    row = WordbookReport(user_id=user_id, book_id=book_id, word=key, sentence_id=sentence_id, kind="sentence",
                         reason=reason, status="open", created_at=now)
    session.add(row)
    await session.commit()
    return {"id": row.id}
```

```python
SENTENCE_ID = Field(pattern=r"^[0-9a-f]{16}$")


class SpellSetsIn(BaseModel):

    size: int = Field(5, ge=3, le=6)

class SpellAnswerIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    typed: str = Field(max_length=100)
    hinted: bool = False


class SpellLineIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=100)
    sentence_id: str = SENTENCE_ID
    answers: list[SpellAnswerIn] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=6)


class SpellSubmitIn(BaseModel):

    book_id: str = Field(max_length=16)
    set_id: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=12)

class ReportIn(BaseModel):

    book_id: str = Field(max_length=16)
    key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=100)
    sentence_id: str = SENTENCE_ID
    reason: Literal["wrong", "offensive", "other"]

@router.post("/spell/sets")
async def wordbook_spell_sets(payload: SpellSetsIn, request: Request, user: User = Depends(current_user),
                              session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
                              settings: Settings = Depends(require_wordbook)):

        return await service.spell_sets(session, dictionary(request.app), user.id, settings, payload.count,
                                        payload.size, payload.tz_offset_minutes, utcnow())

@router.post("/spell/submit")
async def wordbook_spell_submit(payload: SpellSubmitIn, request: Request, user: User = Depends(current_user),
                                session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session),
                                settings: Settings = Depends(require_wordbook)):
    p = service.SpellInput(payload.book_id, payload.set_id, [
        service.SpellLine(normalize_word(l.key), l.sentence_id, [(a.typed, a.hinted) for a in l.answers])
        for l in payload.lines], payload.tz_offset_minutes)
    try:
        return await service.spell_submit(session, dictionary(request.app), user.id, settings, p, utcnow())

@router.get("/cards/{book_id}/{key}")

                          session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session), settings: Settings = Depends(require_wordbook)):

        return await service.report(session, dictionary(request.app), user.id, settings, book_id=payload.book_id,
                                    key=normalize_word(payload.key), sentence_id=payload.sentence_id,
                                    reason=payload.reason, offset=payload.tz_offset_minutes, now=utcnow())
    except service.WordbookError as e:
        _raise(e)
```

```python
async def login(app, client, tmp_path, **overrides):

    r = await client.post("/api/users", json={"username": "alice", "password": "password123"})
    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {r.json()['access_token']}"}


def reply(text=SECRET_TEXT):
    return httpx.Response(200, json={"choices": [{"message": {"content": text}}], "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 50, "completion_tokens": 80}})


@respx.mock
async def test_first_call_hits_llm_then_cache(app, client, tmp_path, caplog):
    route = respx.post(LLM).mock(return_value=reply())
    h = await login(app, client, tmp_path)
    first = (await client.post(URL, headers=h, json={"key": "talk"})).json()
    second = (await client.post(URL, headers=h, json={"key": "Talk"})).json()
    assert first == {"key": "talk", "text": SECRET_TEXT, "cached": False}
    assert second["cached"] is True and route.call_count == 1
    sent = json.loads(route.calls[0].request.content)["messages"][0]["content"]
    assert "<word>talk</word>" in sent and "说话" in sent
    assert SECRET_TEXT not in caplog.text and "<word>" not in caplog.text

        counts = {row.scope: row.count for row in (await s.scalars(select(DailyUsage))).all()}
    assert counts.get("wordbook_ai_user", 0) == 0 and counts.get("wordbook_ai_global", 0) == 0


@respx.mock
async def test_caps_and_switch(app, client, tmp_path):
    respx.post(LLM).mock(return_value=reply())
    h = await login(app, client, tmp_path, wordbook_ai_daily_per_user=1)
    assert (await client.post(URL, headers=h, json={"key": "talk"})).status_code == 200
    assert (await client.post(URL, headers=h, json={"key": "able"})).status_code == 503
    assert (await client.post(URL, headers=h, json={"key": "talk"})).json()["cached"] is True  # 缓存不计次
    app.state.settings = app.state.settings.model_copy(update={"wordbook_ai_enabled": False})
    assert (await client.post(URL, headers=h, json={"key": "go"})).status_code == 503
    assert (await client.post(URL, headers=h, json={"key": "nope"})).status_code == 404


def test_prompt_registered():
    spec = PROMPTS["wordbook.explain"]
    assert spec.required == frozenset({"word", "meanings", "example"}) and "<word>" in spec.boundaries
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `server/app/wordbook/explain.py`:

```python
"""AI「为什么」讲解(spec §6.7):按需、一个词一条全站缓存、每日额度、上游失败降级。不记录正文。"""
import logging

from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from app.core.quotas import quota_for
from app.models import WordbookExplanation
from app.providers import llm
from app.services import usage
from app.wordbook.entry import WordCard

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
MAX_TOKENS = 400
MAX_BODY = 1200
DEFAULT_EXPLAIN_TEMPLATE = (
    "你是耐心的英语老师。请用简体中文为中国学习者讲解下面这个英语单词,不超过 300 字,分三点:"
    "1) 常见用法与搭配;2) 容易混淆的词或词形;3) 拼写记忆要点。只输出讲解正文。\n"
    "<word>{word}</word>\n释义:{meanings}\n例句:{example}"
)


class ExplainUnavailable(Exception):
    def __init__(self, kind: str):
        super().__init__(kind)
        self.kind = kind


def _plain(text: str, limit: int) -> str:
    return " ".join(text.replace("<", "‹").replace(">", "›").split())[:limit]


def render_test(template: str, sample: dict[str, str]) -> list[dict]:
    body = template.format(word=_plain(sample.get("word", "talk"), 100),
                           meanings=_plain(sample.get("meanings", "v. 说话"), 300),
                           example=_plain(sample.get("example", ""), 300))
    return [{"role": "user", "content": body}]


def render_messages(template: str, card: WordCard) -> list[dict]:
    meanings = "；".join(f"{s.pos}. {s.text}" if s.pos else s.text for s in card.senses[:4])
    example = card.examples[0].en if card.examples else ""
    return render_test(template, {"word": card.word, "meanings": meanings, "example": example})


async def explain(session, http, settings, card: WordCard, user_id: int, now, *, template: str,
                  prompt_version: int | None) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    cached = await session.get(WordbookExplanation, card.key)
    if cached is not None:
        return cached.body, True
    if not settings.wordbook_ai_available:
        raise ExplainUnavailable("off")
    day = usage.utc_day(now)
    per_user = await quota_for(session, settings, user_id, "wordbook_ai_daily_per_user", now)
    if not await usage.consume(session, "wordbook_ai_user", str(user_id), day, per_user):
        raise ExplainUnavailable("user_cap")
    if not await usage.consume(session, "wordbook_ai_global", "all", day, settings.wordbook_ai_global_daily):
        await usage.refund(session, "wordbook_ai_user", str(user_id), day)
        raise ExplainUnavailable("global_cap")
    try:
        text = await llm.complete(http, settings, render_messages(template, card),
                                  model=settings.resolved_wordbook_ai_model, max_tokens=MAX_TOKENS, temperature=0.3)
    except llm.LlmUnavailable as e:
        await usage.refund(session, "wordbook_ai_user", str(user_id), day)
        await usage.refund(session, "wordbook_ai_global", "all", day)
        logger.warning("wordbook explain unavailable kind=%s word_len=%d", e.kind, len(card.key))
        raise ExplainUnavailable("upstream") from None
    body = text.strip()[:MAX_BODY]
    session.add(WordbookExplanation(word=card.key, body=body, model=settings.resolved_wordbook_ai_model,
                                    prompt_version=prompt_version, created_at=now))
    try:
        await session.commit()
    except IntegrityError:  # 并发的同词请求先写入了
        await session.rollback()
    return body, False
```

`routers/wordbook.py` 追加(`limited_user` 走 AI 限流;`from app.core.deps import limited_user`、`from app.services.prompts import prompt_cache`、`from app.wordbook import explain as wb_explain`):

```python
class ExplainIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    key: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=100)


@router.post("/explain")
async def wordbook_explain(payload: ExplainIn, request: Request, user: User = Depends(limited_user),
                           session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session), settings: Settings = Depends(require_wordbook)):
    key = normalize_word(payload.key)
    e = await service.store.active_enrollment(session, user.id)
    books = [e.book_id] if e else []
    books += [b for b in service.enabled_books(settings.wordbook_books) if b not in books]
    card = None
    for book_id in books:
        try:
            card = await dictionary(request.app).card(book_id, key)
        except service.DataUnavailable:
            continue
        if card is not None:
            break
    if card is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, service.WORD_NOT_FOUND[1])
    version, body = await prompt_cache(request.app).get("wordbook.explain")
    try:
        text, cached = await wb_explain.explain(session, request.app.state.http, settings, card, user.id, utcnow(),
                                                template=body or wb_explain.DEFAULT_EXPLAIN_TEMPLATE,
                                                prompt_version=version)
    except wb_explain.ExplainUnavailable:
        raise HTTPException(503, "AI explanation unavailable") from None
    return {"key": key, "text": text, "cached": cached}
```

(`service` 模块已导入 `enabled_books`、`DataUnavailable`、`store`,这里经 `service.` 访问,避免路由多一串导入。查词顺序:先当前书,再其他启用的书。)

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:`wordbook_contract.json` = `{"config", "books", "enrollment", "today", "learn_batch", "answer_choice", "answer_typed", "review_batch", "spell_sets", "spell_submit", "card", "explain_unavailable"}` 各端点的真实响应(网页 WC5 逐键核对)

```python
"""背单词端点契约(计划 P-W5)。web/src/features/wordbook/wordbook-contract.test.ts 读取同一个文件。"""
import json
import os
import random
from datetime import timedelta
from pathlib import Path

from sqlalchemy import update

from app.models import VocabularyItem, utcnow
from tests.test_web_contract import shape
from tests.wordbook_helpers import enable_wordbook, make_data_dir

FIXTURE = Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "wordbook_contract.json"
URL = "/api/wordbook"


async def collect(app, client, tmp_path) -> dict:
    enable_wordbook(app, make_data_dir(tmp_path), wordbook_books="junior")
    r = await client.post("/api/users", json={"username": "alice", "password": "password123"})
    h = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {r.json()['access_token']}"}
    out = {"config": (await client.get(f"{URL}/config", headers=h)).json()}
    out["enrollment"] = (await client.put(f"{URL}/enrollment", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "order_mode": "book"})).json()
    out["books"] = (await client.get(f"{URL}/books", headers=h)).json()
    out["learn_batch"] = (await client.post(f"{URL}/learn/batch", headers=h, json={"size": 5})).json()
    choice = out["learn_batch"]["quiz"][0]["options"][0]
    out["answer_choice"] = (await client.post(f"{URL}/answers", headers=h, json={
        "book_id": "junior", "key": "talk", "mode": "learn", "qtype": "meaning", "choice": choice})).json()
    for key in ("ability", "able", "abandon", "absorb"):
        await client.post(f"{URL}/answers", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "key": key, "mode": "learn",
                                                            "qtype": "meaning", "known": True})
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as s:  # 只让 talk(盒 0)到期:题型只会是选择题
        await s.execute(update(VocabularyItem).where(VocabularyItem.word == "talk").values(due_at=utcnow() - timedelta(hours=1)))
        await s.commit()
    out["today"] = (await client.get(f"{URL}/today", headers=h)).json()
    out["review_batch"] = (await client.post(f"{URL}/review/batch", headers=h, json={"size": 3})).json()
    for q in out["review_batch"]["questions"]:  # 题面字段随题型变(种子含随机的用户 id),契约只核对外层形状
        q["prompt"] = {}
    out["answer_typed"] = (await client.post(f"{URL}/answers", headers=h, json={
        "book_id": "junior", "key": "talk", "mode": "review", "qtype": "spell", "typed": "talkk"})).json()
    out["spell_sets"] = (await client.post(f"{URL}/spell/sets", headers=h, json={"count": 1, "size": 3})).json()
    s0 = out["spell_sets"]["sets"][0]
    out["spell_submit"] = (await client.post(f"{URL}/spell/submit", headers=h, json={
        "book_id": "junior", "set_id": s0["set_id"],
        "lines": [{"key": l["key"], "sentence_id": l["sentence_id"], "answers": [{"typed": "x", "hinted": False}]}
                  for l in s0["lines"]]})).json()
    out["card"] = (await client.get(f"{URL}/cards/junior/talk", headers=h)).json()
    out["explain_unavailable"] = (await client.post(f"{URL}/explain", headers=h, json={"key": "talk"})).json()
    return out


async def test_wordbook_payloads_match_the_web_contract(app, client, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    real = random.Random
    monkeypatch.setattr(random, "Random", lambda seed=0: real(seed))  # 句组抽词固定,样例稳定
    actual = await collect(app, client, tmp_path)
    if os.environ.get("CONTRACT_UPDATE"):
        FIXTURE.write_text(json.dumps(actual, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    expected = json.loads(FIXTURE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert shape(actual) == shape(expected)
```

(若 `shape` 对空列表与非空列表给出不同结果,录制的数据要让每个列表非空——上面的流程已保证。)

```python
NOW = datetime(2026, 11, 10, 9, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def test_points_table_and_cap_membership():
    assert points.POINTS["word_learned"] == 1 and points.POINTS["spelling_set"] == 2
    assert {"word_learned", "spelling_set"} <= points.CAPPED
    assert DEFAULT_RULES.points.word_learned == 1 and DEFAULT_RULES.points.spelling_set == 2


def test_old_published_rules_without_new_fields_still_parse():

    parsed = rules_from_json(data)
    assert (parsed.points.word_learned, parsed.points.spelling_set) == (1, 2)

                                outcome="good", credit=100, day="2026-11-10", created_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=10 - i)))

    assert rows[0][1].startswith("wl:") and rows[-1][1].startswith("ws:")
```

```python
from app.admin.rank_rules_admin import wordbook_errors
from app.rank.rules import StageTaskRule
from tests.conftest import make_settings


def test_publishing_words_learned_requires_wordbook():
    tasks = dict(DEFAULT_RULES.stage_tasks)
    tasks[0] = tasks[0][:2] + (StageTaskRule(key="words", kind="words_learned", target=50),)
    draft = DEFAULT_RULES.model_copy(update={"stage_tasks": tasks})
    assert wordbook_errors(draft, make_settings()) == ["stage_tasks:wordbook_disabled"]
    assert wordbook_errors(draft, make_settings(wordbook_enabled=True)) == []
    assert wordbook_errors(DEFAULT_RULES, make_settings()) == []
```

`admin/src/features/rank/rules-schema.test.ts` 追加:`words_learned` 出现在阶段类型选项里,标签为「背单词新词」,且 `min_score` 输入对它隐藏(与 `vocab_reviews` 相同处理)。

- [ ] **Step 2: 跑测试确认失败** — Run: `cd server && uv run pytest -q tests/test_rank_wordbook.py::test_publishing_words_learned_requires_wordbook`;`cd admin && yarn vitest run src/features/rank/rules-schema.test.ts` → FAIL

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `rank_rules_admin.py`:

```python
def wordbook_errors(draft: RankRules, settings) -> list[str]:
    """words_learned 阶段练习只在背单词开启时可发布(spec 2026-10-10-wordbook §8)。"""
    uses = any(t.kind == "words_learned" for tasks in draft.stage_tasks.values() for t in tasks)
    return ["stage_tasks:wordbook_disabled"] if uses and not getattr(settings, "wordbook_enabled", False) else []
```

`validate_draft(session, cache, today, settings=None)`:`errors = validate_rules(...)`;`if settings is not None: errors += wordbook_errors(draft, settings)`;返回 `errors`。`publish` 里 `errors = validate_rules(...) + wordbook_errors(rules_from_json(row.rules), app.state.settings)`。`routers/admin_rank.py` 的 `draft_validate` 调用处传 `settings=request.app.state.settings`(该行属于 R3)。
admin:`api.ts` 的 `StageKind` 加 `"words_learned"`;`strings.ts` 加 `words_learned: "背单词新词"`;`rules-schema.ts` 的阶段类型列表与「无分数门槛」集合加入 `words_learned`;错误码 `stage_tasks:wordbook_disabled` 的中文「背单词未开启,不能使用『背单词新词』阶段练习」加入错误文案表。

### Task A1: 管理端词书统计、重新加载、例句举报处理、诊断

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试** — `server/tests/test_admin_wordbook.py`:

```python
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from app.admin.doctor import CODES, diagnose
from app.models import AdminAudit, WordbookHiddenSentence, WordbookReport
from app.wordbook.index import dictionary
from tests.admin_helpers import ADMIN_ENABLED, assert_no_secrets, login_as
from tests.conftest import make_settings
from tests.test_admin_doctor import OK
from tests.wordbook_helpers import enable_wordbook, make_data_dir

API = "/api/admin/wordbook"


@pytest.fixture
def settings_overrides():
    return ADMIN_ENABLED


async def learner(app, client, tmp_path):
    enable_wordbook(app, make_data_dir(tmp_path), wordbook_books="junior,senior")
    r = await client.post("/api/users", json={"username": "alice", "password": "password123"})
    h = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {r.json()['access_token']}"}
    await client.put("/api/wordbook/enrollment", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "order_mode": "book"})
    await client.post("/api/wordbook/answers", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "key": "talk", "mode": "learn",
                                                               "qtype": "meaning", "known": True})
    card = (await client.get("/api/wordbook/cards/junior/talk", headers=h)).json()
    await client.post("/api/wordbook/reports", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "key": "talk",
                                                               "sentence_id": card["examples"][0]["id"], "reason": "wrong"})
    return h, card


async def test_overview_lists_books_files_and_learning(app, client, tmp_path):
    await learner(app, client, tmp_path)
    await login_as(app, client, "viewer")
    body = (await client.get(API)).json()
    junior, senior = body["books"]
    assert (junior["id"], junior["enabled"], junior["learners"], junior["learned"], junior["reports_open"]) == (
        "junior", True, 1, 1, 1)
    assert junior["file"]["bytes"] > 0 and junior["file"]["sha256_ok"] is None  # 测试数据不在清单里:不判定
    assert junior["index"]["loaded"] is True and junior["index"]["keys"] == 14 and junior["index"]["dropped"] == 2
    assert senior["file"]["bytes"] == 0 and senior["index"]["loaded"] is False
    assert [b["id"] for b in body["books"]] == ["junior", "senior"]  # 只列启用的书
    assert_no_secrets(body, app.state.settings)


async def test_reload_requires_operator_and_is_audited(app, client, tmp_path):
    await learner(app, client, tmp_path)
    await login_as(app, client, "viewer", "viewer2")
    assert (await client.post(f"{API}/reload", json={"reason": "x"})).status_code == 403
    h = await login_as(app, client, "operator")
    r = await client.post(f"{API}/reload", headers=h, json={"book_id": "junior", "reason": "数据更新"})
    assert r.status_code == 200 and dictionary(app).loaded() == {}
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as s:
        assert "wordbook.reload" in (await s.scalars(select(AdminAudit.action))).all()


async def test_hide_sentence_updates_reports_and_live_cards(app, client, tmp_path):
    h_user, card = await learner(app, client, tmp_path)
    h = await login_as(app, client, "operator")
    (row,) = (await client.get(f"{API}/reports", headers=h)).json()["reports"]
    assert row["sentence"] == card["examples"][0]["en"] and row["count"] == 1
    r = await client.post(f"{API}/reports/{row['id']}/hide", headers=h, json={"reason": "例句不当"})
    assert r.status_code == 200
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as s:
        assert (await s.scalars(select(WordbookHiddenSentence.sentence_id))).all() == [card["examples"][0]["id"]]
        assert (await s.scalars(select(WordbookReport.status))).all() == ["hidden"]
        assert "wordbook.sentence.hide" in (await s.scalars(select(AdminAudit.action))).all()
    fresh = (await client.get("/api/wordbook/cards/junior/talk", headers=h_user)).json()
    assert card["examples"][0]["id"] not in [e["id"] for e in fresh["examples"]]
    assert (await client.post(f"{API}/reports/999/dismiss", headers=h, json={"reason": "x"})).status_code == 404


def test_doctor_findings():
    assert {"wordbook_data_missing", "wordbook_ai_unavailable"} <= set(CODES)
    from dataclasses import replace
    s = make_settings(wordbook_enabled=True, wordbook_ai_enabled=True, upstream_llm_base_url="")
    found = {f.code: f for f in diagnose(s, replace(OK, wordbook_missing=("toefl",)))}
    assert found["wordbook_data_missing"].level == "error" and "toefl" in found["wordbook_data_missing"].message_zh
    assert found["wordbook_ai_unavailable"].level == "warn"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `server/app/admin/wordbook_admin.py`:

```python
"""管理端:词书文件与学习统计、重新加载词典、例句举报处理(spec 2026-10-10-wordbook §9)。不返回用户内容。"""
import hashlib
import json
from datetime import timedelta
from typing import Literal

from sqlalchemy import func, select, update

from app.admin import audit
from app.models import (DailyUsage, VocabularyItem, WordbookEnrollment, WordbookEvent, WordbookExplanation,
                        WordbookHiddenSentence, WordbookReport, iso_utc)
from app.services.usage import utc_day
from app.wordbook import store
from app.wordbook.books import BOOKS, enabled_books
from app.wordbook.index import DataUnavailable, dictionary


class WordbookAdminError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, status: int, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status, self.message = status, message


def _manifest(data_dir) -> dict[str, dict]:
    try:
        return {b["file"]: b for b in json.loads((data_dir / "SOURCE.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["books"]}
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):
        return {}


def _sha_ok(app, path, expected: dict | None) -> bool | None:
    if expected is None or not path.is_file():
        return None
    stat = path.stat()
    cache = app.state.__dict__.setdefault("wordbook_sha_cache", {})
    slot = (str(path), stat.st_size, stat.st_mtime_ns)
    if slot not in cache:
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        with path.open("rb") as handle:
            for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1 << 20), b""):
                digest.update(chunk)
        cache[slot] = digest.hexdigest() == expected["sha256"]
    return cache[slot]


async def _count(session, stmt) -> int:
    return int(await session.scalar(stmt) or 0)


async def overview(session, app, now) -> dict:
    settings = app.state.settings
    ds = dictionary(app)
    loaded = ds.loaded()
    manifest = _manifest(settings.resolved_wordbook_data_dir)
    enabled = set(enabled_books(settings.wordbook_books))
    books = []
    for b in BOOKS:
        if b.id not in enabled:
            continue
        path = settings.resolved_wordbook_data_dir / b.file
        index = loaded.get(b.id)
        books.append({
            "id": b.id, "title_zh": b.title_zh, "enabled": True,
            "file": {"bytes": path.stat().st_size if path.is_file() else 0,
                     "sha256_ok": _sha_ok(app, path, manifest.get(b.file)), "entries": b.entries, "unique": b.unique},
            "index": {"loaded": index is not None, "keys": len(index) if index else 0,
                      "dropped": index.dropped if index else 0, "built_ms": index.built_ms if index else None,
                      "stale": ds.stale(b.id)},
            "learners": await _count(session, select(func.count()).select_from(WordbookEnrollment).where(
                WordbookEnrollment.book_id == b.id)),
            "learners_7d": await _count(session, select(func.count(func.distinct(WordbookEvent.user_id))).where(
                WordbookEvent.book_id == b.id, WordbookEvent.created_at >= now - timedelta(days=7))),
            "learned": await _count(session, select(func.count()).select_from(WordbookEvent).where(
                WordbookEvent.book_id == b.id, WordbookEvent.kind == "learn")),
            "mastered": await _count(session, select(func.count()).select_from(VocabularyItem).where(
                VocabularyItem.source == store.wordbook_source(b.id), VocabularyItem.box >= 4)),
            "reports_open": await _count(session, select(func.count()).select_from(WordbookReport).where(
                WordbookReport.book_id == b.id, WordbookReport.status == "open")),
        })
    today_calls = await _count(session, select(DailyUsage.count).where(
        DailyUsage.scope == "wordbook_ai_global", DailyUsage.subject == "all", DailyUsage.day == utc_day(now)))
    return {"enabled": settings.wordbook_enabled,
            "ai": {"enabled": settings.wordbook_ai_enabled, "available": settings.wordbook_ai_available,
                   "today_calls": today_calls, "global_daily": settings.wordbook_ai_global_daily,
                   "cached": await _count(session, select(func.count()).select_from(WordbookExplanation))},
            "books": books}


async def reload(session, app, actor, *, book_id: str | None, reason: str) -> None:
    dictionary(app).reload(book_id)
    await audit.record(session, actor, "wordbook.reload", target_type="wordbook", target_id=book_id or "all",
                       reason=reason)
    await session.commit()


async def reports(session, app) -> list[dict]:
    rows = (await session.execute(
        select(WordbookReport.book_id, WordbookReport.word, WordbookReport.sentence_id,
               func.min(WordbookReport.id), func.count(), func.min(WordbookReport.reason),
               func.min(WordbookReport.created_at))
        .where(WordbookReport.status == "open")
        .group_by(WordbookReport.book_id, WordbookReport.word, WordbookReport.sentence_id)
        .order_by(func.count().desc()).limit(200))).all()
    ds = dictionary(app)
    out = []
    for book_id, word, sid, first_id, count, reason, created in rows:
        try:
            card = await ds.card(book_id, word)
        except DataUnavailable:
            card = None
        sentence = next((e.en for e in card.examples if e.id == sid), None) if card else None
        out.append({"id": first_id, "book_id": book_id, "word": word, "sentence_id": sid, "sentence": sentence,
                    "reason": reason, "count": count, "created_at": iso_utc(created)})
    return out


async def resolve(session, app, actor, report_id: int, *, action: Literal["hide", "dismiss"], reason: str) -> None:
    row = await session.get(WordbookReport, report_id)
    if row is None:
        raise WordbookAdminError(404, "Report not found")
    status = "hidden" if action == "hide" else "dismissed"
    await session.execute(update(WordbookReport).where(
        WordbookReport.sentence_id == row.sentence_id, WordbookReport.status == "open").values(status=status))
    if action == "hide" and await session.get(WordbookHiddenSentence, row.sentence_id) is None:
        session.add(WordbookHiddenSentence(sentence_id=row.sentence_id, book_id=row.book_id,
                                           hidden_by=actor.name[:64], created_at=row.created_at))
    await audit.record(session, actor, "wordbook.sentence.hide" if action == "hide" else "wordbook.report.dismiss",
                       target_type="wordbook_sentence", target_id=row.sentence_id, reason=reason)
    await session.commit()
    if action == "hide":
        await store.load_hidden(session, dictionary(app))
```

`server/app/routers/admin_wordbook.py`:

```python
"""/api/admin/wordbook*:词书统计、重新加载、例句举报(spec 2026-10-10-wordbook §9)。业务在 app/admin/wordbook_admin.py。"""
from typing import Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Path, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.admin import wordbook_admin
from app.admin.perms import AdminContext, require
from app.core.deps import get_session
from app.models import utcnow

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/admin")


class ReasonIn(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)

    @field_validator("reason")
    @classmethod
    def _strip(cls, value: str) -> str:
        if not value.strip():
            raise ValueError("reason is required")
        return value.strip()


class ReloadIn(ReasonIn):
    book_id: Literal["junior", "senior", "cet4", "cet6", "kaoyan", "toefl", "sat"] | None = None


@router.get("/wordbook")
async def wordbook_overview(request: Request, _: AdminContext = Depends(require("viewer")),
                            session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session)):
    return await wordbook_admin.overview(session, request.app, utcnow())


@router.post("/wordbook/reload")
async def wordbook_reload(body: ReloadIn, request: Request, ctx: AdminContext = Depends(require("operator")),
                          session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session)):
    await wordbook_admin.reload(session, request.app, ctx.actor, book_id=body.book_id, reason=body.reason)
    return {"ok": True}


@router.get("/wordbook/reports")
async def wordbook_reports(request: Request, _: AdminContext = Depends(require("viewer")),
                           session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session)):
    return {"reports": await wordbook_admin.reports(session, request.app)}


@router.post("/wordbook/reports/{report_id}/{action}")
async def wordbook_resolve(body: ReasonIn, request: Request, report_id: int = Path(ge=1),
                           action: Literal["hide", "dismiss"] = Path(),
                           ctx: AdminContext = Depends(require("operator")), session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_session)):
    try:
        await wordbook_admin.resolve(session, request.app, ctx.actor, report_id, action=action, reason=body.reason)
    except wordbook_admin.WordbookAdminError as e:
        raise HTTPException(e.status, e.message) from None
    return {"ok": True}
```

- Produces:路由 `/wordbook`(导航「用户与内容」组,图标为书本);`WordbookPage`;用量页 `feature` 名 `wordbook` → 「背单词」;配额标签 `wordbook_ai_daily_per_user` → 「每人每日 AI 讲解次数」

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试** — `admin/src/features/wordbook/wordbook-page.test.tsx`(按 `admin/src/features/rooms` 页面测试的 fetch 桩写法):

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { MemoryRouter } from "react-router-dom";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { WordbookPage } from "./wordbook-page";

const overview = {
  enabled: true,
  ai: { enabled: false, available: false, today_calls: 0, global_daily: 200, cached: 3 },
  books: [{ id: "junior", title_zh: "初中", enabled: true, file: { bytes: 4068095, sha256_ok: true, entries: 3223, unique: 1987 },
    index: { loaded: true, keys: 1985, dropped: 2, built_ms: 160, stale: false }, learners: 4, learners_7d: 2, learned: 120, mastered: 9, reports_open: 1 }],
};
const reports = { reports: [{ id: 5, book_id: "junior", word: "talk", sentence_id: "a".repeat(16), sentence: "After a long talk, we decided.", reason: "wrong", count: 2, created_at: "2026-10-10T09:00:00+00:00" }] };

function stubFetch() {
  const calls: { url: string; init?: RequestInit }[] = [];
  vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn(async (url: string, init?: RequestInit) => {
    calls.push({ url, init });
    const body = url.endsWith("/reports") ? reports : url.includes("/reload") || url.includes("/hide") ? { ok: true } : overview;
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status: 200, headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
  }));
  return calls;
}

afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals());

describe("WordbookPage", () => {
  it("shows per-book file, index and learning numbers", async () => {
    stubFetch();
    render(<MemoryRouter><WordbookPage /></MemoryRouter>);
    const row = await screen.findByTestId("wordbook-book-junior");
    expect(within(row).getByText("初中")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(row).getByText("1,985")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(row).getByText("校验通过")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("AI 讲解未开启")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("hides a reported sentence with a reason", async () => {
    const calls = stubFetch();
    render(<MemoryRouter><WordbookPage /></MemoryRouter>);
    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "屏蔽此句" }));
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText("理由"), { target: { value: "例句不当" } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "确认" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(calls.some((c) => c.url.endsWith("/reports/5/hide") && c.init?.method === "POST")).toBe(true));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `wordbook-page.tsx`:用 `useAsync(fetchWordbookAdmin)` 与 `useAsync(fetchReports)`;布局沿用管理端桌面版式(R-7):顶部 KPI 行(AI 讲解状态「AI 讲解未开启 / 已开启 · 今日 n / 上限 m · 缓存 k 条」);书表 `DataTable` 列:书名、启用(链接到 `/settings/wordbook` 修改 `wordbook_books`)、文件(字节,`sha256_ok` → 「校验通过 / 校验失败 / —」用 `StatusDot` good/bad/neutral)、索引(已加载 · 键数 · 丢弃 · 构建 ms;`stale` → amber「文件已变化,需重新加载」)、选书人数、近 7 天、学会、掌握、待处理举报;每行 `data-testid="wordbook-book-<id>"`;数字用 `toLocaleString("en-US")`。右上「重新加载词典」按钮(operator,`RoleGate`)→ `ConfirmDialog`(理由)→ `reloadWordbook(reason)`。举报表:书、词、句子、原因、次数、时间;操作「屏蔽此句」/「忽略」→ `ConfirmDialog`(理由,`aria-label="理由"`,确认按钮「确认」)→ `hideReport`/`dismissReport` → 重新加载两张表。`strings.ts` 放全部中文文案;`index.ts` 导出 `WordbookPage`。
`admin/src/app.tsx` 在 `prompts/:key` 前加 `<Route path="wordbook" element={<WordbookPage />} />`;`components/layout.tsx` 的「用户与内容」组加 `{ to: "/wordbook", label: "词书", icon: BookIcon }`(`icons.tsx` 已有或新增一个 24px 书本 SVG 图标)。
`features/usage/strings.ts` 的功能名表加 `wordbook: "背单词"`;`features/users/strings.ts` 的配额标签表加 `wordbook_ai_daily_per_user: "每人每日 AI 讲解次数"`。

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ApiClient } from "~/api/client";
import i18n from "~/i18n";
import { createFetchMock, json } from "~/test/fetch-mock";
import { renderWithProviders } from "~/test/render";
import { WordbookCard } from "./wordbook-card";

const CONFIG = { enabled: true, ai_available: false, defaults: { daily_new: 20, review_cap: 100 },
  books: [{ id: "cet4", title_zh: "四级", title_en: "CET-4", level: 3, words: 4544, available: true }] };
const PLAN = { new_target: 20, new_done: 8, new_left: 12, review_due: 34, review_done: 0, review_left: 34, backlog_guard: false };

function client(routes: Parameters<typeof createFetchMock>[0]) {
  return new ApiClient({ getToken: () => "t", fetchImpl: createFetchMock(routes).fetch });
}

beforeEach(() => { localStorage.setItem("enjoy-web.settings", JSON.stringify({ language: "zh-CN" })); void i18n.changeLanguage("zh-CN"); });
afterEach(() => { localStorage.removeItem("enjoy-web.settings"); void i18n.changeLanguage("en"); });

describe("WordbookCard", () => {
  it("renders nothing when the wordbook is disabled", async () => {
    const api = client({ "GET /api/wordbook/config": () => json({ enabled: false }) });
    const { container } = renderWithProviders(<WordbookCard />, { api });
    await waitFor(() => expect(container.querySelector("[data-testid=wordbook-card]")).toBeNull());
  });

  it("asks to pick a book when there is no enrollment", async () => {
    const api = client({ "GET /api/wordbook/config": () => json(CONFIG),
      "GET /api/wordbook/today": () => json({ enrollment: null, book: null, plan: null, progress: null, tomorrow_due: 0 }) });
    renderWithProviders(<WordbookCard />, { api });
    expect(await screen.findByRole("link", { name: "选择词书" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/wordbook/books");
  });

  it("shows today's plan and starts with reviews", async () => {
    const api = client({ "GET /api/wordbook/config": () => json(CONFIG),
      "GET /api/wordbook/today": () => json({ enrollment: { book_id: "cet4", status: "active", order_mode: "shuffle", daily_new: 20, review_cap: 100, started_at: "2026-10-01T00:00:00+00:00" },
        book: { ...CONFIG.books[0] }, plan: PLAN, progress: { words: 4544, learned: 860, mastered: 312, remaining: 3684 }, tomorrow_due: 41 }) });
    renderWithProviders(<WordbookCard />, { api });
    expect(await screen.findByText("四级")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("8/20")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "开始复习 34" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/wordbook/review");
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "拼写练习" })).toHaveAttribute("href", "/wordbook/spell");
    expect(screen.getByText("已掌握 312 / 4,544")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

`wordbook-card.tsx`:

```tsx
import { BookOpenIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { cn } from "@renderer/lib/utils";
import { useAuth } from "~/auth/auth-context";
import { SurfaceCard } from "~/components/surface-card";
import { PlanRing } from "./plan-ring";
import { useWordbookConfig } from "./use-wordbook-config";
import { fetchToday, type Today } from "./wordbook-api";

const PRIMARY = "inline-flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center rounded-button bg-blue px-4 text-body font-semibold text-on-blue";
const GHOST = "inline-flex h-11 flex-1 items-center justify-center rounded-button border-[0.5px] border-hair bg-surface px-4 text-body font-semibold text-ink";

/** 练习页「背单词」大卡(spec §11.1);开关关闭或配置未到时不渲染。 */
export function WordbookCard({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const { api } = useAuth();
  const config = useWordbookConfig();
  const [today, setToday] = useState<Today | null>(null);
  const enabled = config?.enabled === true;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    fetchToday(api, controller.signal).then(setToday, (): void => undefined);
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [api, enabled]);
  if (!enabled || !today) return null;
  const nf = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US");
  if (!today.plan || !today.book || !today.progress) {
    return (
      <SurfaceCard data-testid="wordbook-card" className={cn("flex items-center gap-3 p-3.5", className)}>
        <span className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-[12px] bg-teal-tint text-teal"><BookOpenIcon aria-hidden /></span>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="text-body font-semibold">{t("web.wordbook.card.title")}</p>
          <p className="text-meta text-ink-2">{today.enrollment ? t("web.wordbook.card.bookDisabled") : t("web.wordbook.card.pitch")}</p>
        </div>
        <Link to="/wordbook/books" className={cn(PRIMARY, "flex-none")}>{t("web.wordbook.card.pick")}</Link>
      </SurfaceCard>
    );
  }
  const { plan, progress, book } = today;
  const primary = plan.reviewLeft > 0
    ? { to: "/wordbook/review", label: t("web.wordbook.card.review", { n: plan.reviewLeft }) }
    : plan.newLeft > 0
      ? { to: "/wordbook/learn", label: t("web.wordbook.card.learn", { n: plan.newLeft }) }
      : { to: "/wordbook", label: t("web.wordbook.card.done") };
  return (
    <SurfaceCard data-testid="wordbook-card" className={cn("p-3.5", className)}>
      <Link to="/wordbook" className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
        <PlanRing done={plan.newDone} target={plan.newTarget} />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-body font-semibold">
            {t("web.wordbook.card.title")}
            <span className="rounded-[7px] bg-teal-tint px-2 text-meta font-semibold text-teal">{i18n.language.startsWith("zh") ? book.titleZh : book.titleEn}</span>
          </p>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-meta text-ink-2">{t("web.wordbook.card.counts", { due: plan.reviewLeft, newLeft: plan.newLeft })}</p>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-meta text-ink-2">{t("web.wordbook.card.mastered", { m: nf.format(progress.mastered), total: nf.format(progress.words) })}</p>
        </div>
      </Link>
      <div className="mt-3 flex gap-2">
        <Link to={primary.to} className={PRIMARY}>{primary.label}</Link>
        <Link to="/wordbook/spell" className={GHOST}>{t("web.wordbook.card.spell")}</Link>
      </div>
    </SurfaceCard>
  );
}
```

`plan-ring.tsx`(WC1 先建,WC2 复用):

```tsx
/** 新词进度环(青色;spec §11.3)。`done/target` 文本在环内。 */
export function PlanRing({ done, target, size = 56 }: { done: number; target: number; size?: number }) {
  const pct = target > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round((done / target) * 100)) : 0;
  return (
    <span role="img" aria-label={`${done}/${target}`} className="relative inline-flex flex-none items-center justify-center rounded-full"
      style={{ width: size, height: size, background: `conic-gradient(rgb(var(--teal)) 0 ${pct}%, rgb(var(--teal-tint)) ${pct}% 100%)` }}>
      <span className="absolute inset-[6px] rounded-full bg-surface" />
      <span className="num relative text-[15px] font-semibold text-teal">{done}/{target}</span>
    </span>
  );
}
```

(颜色令牌按 `web/src/styles/theme.css` 实际变量名写;若 `--teal` 不是 `r g b` 三元组格式,改用 `var(--teal)`。)

### Task WC2: 背单词首页、选书页、字母差异组件

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/src/features/wordbook/home-page.tsx`、`web/src/features/wordbook/books-page.tsx`、`web/src/features/wordbook/i18n/*`
- Create: `web/src/features/wordbook/letter-diff.tsx`、`web/src/features/wordbook/{home-page,books-page,letter-diff}.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:`fetchToday`、`fetchBooks`、`saveEnrollment`、`PlanRing`
- Produces:`LetterDiff({ diff, expected }: { diff: DiffOp[]; expected: string }): JSX.Element`(`eq` 正常;`sub` 输入字母删除线 + 正确字母琥珀下划线;`ins` 多余字母删除线;`del` 漏掉的字母琥珀下划线;每个片段 `data-op`;整体 `aria-label` = 「正确拼写 {expected}」)

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

`letter-diff.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { LetterDiff } from "./letter-diff";

describe("LetterDiff", () => {
  it("marks missing, extra and wrong letters", () => {
    const { container } = render(<LetterDiff expected="talking" diff={[
      { op: "eq", a: "t", b: "t" }, { op: "eq", a: "a", b: "a" }, { op: "eq", a: "l", b: "l" }, { op: "eq", a: "k", b: "k" },
      { op: "del", a: "i", b: "" }, { op: "eq", a: "n", b: "n" }, { op: "eq", a: "g", b: "g" }]} />);
    const ops = [...container.querySelectorAll("[data-op]")].map((n) => `${n.getAttribute("data-op")}:${n.textContent}`);
    expect(ops).toEqual(["eq:t", "eq:a", "eq:l", "eq:k", "del:i", "eq:n", "eq:g"]);
    expect(container.firstElementChild?.getAttribute("aria-label")).toContain("talking");
  });

  it("renders a substitution as typed-then-expected", () => {
    const { container } = render(<LetterDiff expected="cat" diff={[{ op: "eq", a: "c", b: "c" }, { op: "sub", a: "a", b: "u" }, { op: "eq", a: "t", b: "t" }]} />);
    const sub = container.querySelector("[data-op=sub]")!;
    expect(sub.querySelector("s")?.textContent).toBe("u");
    expect(sub.querySelector("u")?.textContent).toBe("a");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 跑测试确认失败** — Run: `cd web && yarn vitest run src/features/wordbook` → FAIL

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现**

`letter-diff.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import type { DiffOp } from "./wordbook-api";

/** 逐字母对齐(服务端 grading.align 的结果);颜色只做辅助,删除线/下划线本身表达差异。 */
export function LetterDiff({ diff, expected }: { diff: DiffOp[]; expected: string }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  return (
    <span className="font-mono text-[17px] tracking-[0.02em]" aria-label={t("web.wordbook.diff.label", { word: expected })}>
      {diff.map((d, i) => {
        if (d.op === "eq") return <span key={i} data-op="eq">{d.a}</span>;
        if (d.op === "del") return <span key={i} data-op="del"><u className="text-amber decoration-2">{d.a}</u></span>;
        if (d.op === "ins") return <span key={i} data-op="ins"><s className="text-ink-2">{d.b}</s></span>;
        return <span key={i} data-op="sub"><s className="text-ink-2">{d.b}</s><u className="text-amber decoration-2">{d.a}</u></span>;
      })}
    </span>
  );
}
```

`home-page.tsx`:`PageHeader`(标题「背单词」,右侧 44px 设置图标 → `/wordbook/books`);书卡(书名徽标 teal、`role="progressbar"` 总进度条:已掌握 teal 实色、已学 teal-tint、剩余 surface-2,下方三列数字「已掌握 / 已学 / 剩余」);今日计划卡(`PlanRing` 新词、复习计数 `reviewDone/reviewLeft+reviewDone`、明日预测);动作区三张 `ListRow`(复习 → `/wordbook/review`,学新词 → `/wordbook/learn`,拼写 → `/wordbook/spell`;计数为 0 的行禁用并显示「已完成」);`backlogGuard` 显示琥珀提示条。无选书 → `<Navigate to="/wordbook/books" replace />`;书被停用 → 提示并给「换一本」。
`books-page.tsx`:`fetchBooks` → 卡片列表(书名、级别「难度 n/6」、词数、`learned/mastered`,当前书带蓝色「当前」徽标);点卡片打开 `BottomSheet`:三组 `SegmentedControl`(新词 `[10,20,30,50]`、复习 `[50,100,200]`、顺序 `shuffle|book`),默认值取已选书的设置或 `config.defaults`;「开始」→ `saveEnrollment` → `navigate("/wordbook")`。说明文字「级别只是按词书大致排序,数据里没有词频」(spec §13)。
i18n:`home.*`、`books.*`、`diff.label`「正确拼写 {{word}}」等中英两份。

### Task WC3: 学新词(词卡 → 小测 → 小结)与会话队列

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/features/wordbook/{session-machine.ts,session-machine.test.ts,word-card.tsx,choice-question.tsx,session-summary.tsx,learn-page.test.tsx}`
- Modify: `web/src/features/wordbook/learn-page.tsx`、`web/src/features/wordbook/i18n/*`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces(`session-machine.ts`):`MAX_REQUEUE = 2`;`SessionState { queue: string[]; done: string[]; requeued: Record<string, number>; current: string | null; firstTry: Record<string, boolean> }`;`startSession(keys: string[]): SessionState`;`answerCurrent(s: SessionState, correct: boolean): SessionState`(答错且该词重排次数 < 2 → 放到队尾;否则记为完成);`isFinished(s): boolean`;`progressOf(s): { done: number; total: number }`
- Produces:`WordCardView({ card, onListen, listening })`、`ChoiceQuestion({ question, onAnswer, result })`(选项按钮 ≥ 48px,`result` 到达后正确项绿色勾、所选错误项中性删除线)、`SessionSummary({ stats, onAgain, onHome })`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试** — `session-machine.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { answerCurrent, isFinished, progressOf, startSession } from "./session-machine";

describe("session machine", () => {
  it("requeues wrong answers at the end, at most twice", () => {
    let s = startSession(["a", "b"]);
    s = answerCurrent(s, false);
    expect(s.queue).toEqual(["b", "a"]);
    s = answerCurrent(s, true);
    s = answerCurrent(s, false); // a 第二次答错:再排一次(已达上限 2)
    expect(s.current).toBe("a");
    s = answerCurrent(s, false); // 上限后答错也算完成
    expect(isFinished(s)).toBe(true);
    expect(s.firstTry).toEqual({ a: false, b: true });
    expect(progressOf(s)).toEqual({ done: 2, total: 2 });
  });

  it("an empty session is finished", () => {
    expect(isFinished(startSession([]))).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

`learn-page.test.tsx`:`POST /api/wordbook/learn/batch` 返回 2 张卡 + 2 道 `meaning` 题;流程:看到词卡「talk」与音标、点「下一个」→ 第二张卡 → 点「开始小测」→ 第一题点正确选项 → `POST /api/wordbook/answers` body 含 `{"mode":"learn","qtype":"meaning","key":"talk","choice":<正确选项>}` → 显示「答对了」→「继续」→ 第二题选错 → 该题回到队尾再出现一次 → 答对 → 小结显示「学会 2 个新词」「一次答对 1」;点「我认识」时 body 含 `"known":true` 且该词不出现在小测中;`aria-live="polite"` 区域只在每题结果出现时更新一次。

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `session-machine.ts`:

```ts
export const MAX_REQUEUE = 2;

export interface SessionState {
  queue: string[];
  done: string[];
  requeued: Record<string, number>;
  current: string | null;
  firstTry: Record<string, boolean>;
}

export function startSession(keys: string[]): SessionState {
  return { queue: [...keys], done: [], requeued: {}, current: keys[0] ?? null, firstTry: {} };
}

export function answerCurrent(s: SessionState, correct: boolean): SessionState {
  if (s.current === null) return s;
  const key = s.current;
  const rest = s.queue.slice(1);
  const firstTry = key in s.firstTry ? s.firstTry : { ...s.firstTry, [key]: correct };
  const count = s.requeued[key] ?? 0;
  if (!correct && count < MAX_REQUEUE) {
    const queue = [...rest, key];
    return { ...s, queue, current: queue[0], requeued: { ...s.requeued, [key]: count + 1 }, firstTry };
  }
  return { ...s, queue: rest, done: [...s.done, key], current: rest[0] ?? null, firstTry };
}

export const isFinished = (s: SessionState) => s.current === null;
export const progressOf = (s: SessionState) => ({ done: s.done.length, total: s.done.length + new Set(s.queue).size });
```

`learn-page.tsx`:状态 `phase: "loading" | "cards" | "quiz" | "summary" | "empty"`;`learnBatch(api)` 取卡与题;`cards` 阶段一次一张 `WordCardView`(词头 34px、美/英音标各带 44px 朗读键 `useTtsPlayer(...).play(key, word, model, voice)`、释义列表、首条例句(目标词 `<mark>` 琥珀)可展开译文与朗读、短语折叠)、底部「我认识」(ghost)与「下一个」(primary);「我认识」立即 `submitAnswer({ mode: "learn", qtype: "meaning", known: true })` 并把该词从小测移除;卡看完 → `quiz` 阶段用 `startSession(剩余题的 key)` + `ChoiceQuestion`;答题调用 `submitAnswer({ mode: "learn", qtype: "meaning", choice })`,用返回的 `correct` 驱动 `answerCurrent`;答错时先显示该词的词卡摘要再继续(spec §6.3「回到队尾再看词卡再测」);结束 → `SessionSummary`(学会 n、一次答对 m、段位开启时「+n 段位点(估算)」,用 `fetchRankConfig` 判断),「再来一轮」重新 `learnBatch`,`cards` 为空 → `empty` 阶段「今天的新词学完了」。TTS 默认模型/声音从 `loadServerAiConfig(api)` 读取;未配置时朗读键禁用并有 `title` 说明。错误统一 `translateServerError(t, err, WORDBOOK_SERVER_ERRORS)` 显示在页面内。

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { allFilled, clozeReducer, initCloze, interpretInput, SENTINEL } from "./cloze-engine";

const run = (count: number, actions: Parameters<typeof clozeReducer>[1][]) => actions.reduce(clozeReducer, initCloze(count));

describe("cloze engine", () => {
  it("types into the active blank, space moves on, never past the last blank", () => {
    const s = run(2, [{ type: "set", value: "talk" }, { type: "space" }, { type: "set", value: "go" }, { type: "space" }]);
    expect(s).toEqual({ values: ["talk", "go"], active: 1, hinted: [false, false] });
    expect(allFilled(s)).toBe(true);
  });

  it("backspace on an empty blank goes back and keeps the previous text", () => {
    const s = run(3, [{ type: "set", value: "a" }, { type: "space" }, { type: "backspaceEmpty" }]);
    expect(s.active).toBe(0);
    expect(s.values[0]).toBe("a");
    expect(run(1, [{ type: "backspaceEmpty" }]).active).toBe(0);
  });

  it("strips spaces and keeps letters, apostrophes and hyphens only", () => {
    expect(run(1, [{ type: "set", value: " don't-x1 " }]).values[0]).toBe("don't-x");
  });

  it("hint writes the first letter and marks the blank", () => {
    const s = run(2, [{ type: "focus", index: 1 }, { type: "hint", index: 1, letter: "c" }]);
    expect(s.values[1]).toBe("c");
    expect(s.hinted).toEqual([false, true]);
    expect(run(1, [{ type: "focus", index: 5 }]).active).toBe(0);
  });

  it("interprets hidden input diffs (Gboard keyCode 229 safe)", () => {
    expect(interpretInput(SENTINEL + "tal", SENTINEL + "talk")).toEqual([{ type: "set", value: "talk" }]);
    expect(interpretInput(SENTINEL + "talk", SENTINEL + "talk ")).toEqual([{ type: "set", value: "talk" }, { type: "space" }]);
    expect(interpretInput(SENTINEL, "")).toEqual([{ type: "backspaceEmpty" }]);
    expect(interpretInput(SENTINEL + "a", SENTINEL)).toEqual([{ type: "set", value: "" }]);
    expect(interpretInput(SENTINEL + "abc", "xyz")).toEqual([{ type: "set", value: "xyz" }]);  // 全选后替换,哨兵丢了但有字
    expect(interpretInput(SENTINEL, SENTINEL + "used to")).toEqual([{ type: "set", value: "used" }, { type: "space" }, { type: "set", value: "to" }]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `cloze-engine.ts`:

```ts
/** 按句拼写的输入引擎(spec §11.5):纯 reducer + 隐藏输入框差异解释器。 */
export const SENTINEL = "​";

export interface ClozeState { values: string[]; active: number; hinted: boolean[] }
export type ClozeAction =
  | { type: "set"; value: string }
  | { type: "space" }
  | { type: "backspaceEmpty" }
  | { type: "focus"; index: number }
  | { type: "hint"; index: number; letter: string }
  | { type: "reset"; count: number };

const clean = (v: string) => v.replace(/[^A-Za-zÀ-ÿ'’-]/g, "").replace(/’/g, "'");

export function initCloze(count: number): ClozeState {
  return { values: Array(count).fill(""), active: 0, hinted: Array(count).fill(false) };
}

export function clozeReducer(s: ClozeState, a: ClozeAction): ClozeState {
  const last = s.values.length - 1;
  switch (a.type) {
    case "set": {
      const values = [...s.values];
      values[s.active] = clean(a.value);
      return { ...s, values };
    }
    case "space":
      return { ...s, active: Math.min(s.active + 1, Math.max(last, 0)) };
    case "backspaceEmpty":
      return { ...s, active: Math.max(s.active - 1, 0) };
    case "focus":
      return a.index >= 0 && a.index <= last ? { ...s, active: a.index } : s;
    case "hint": {
      if (a.index < 0 || a.index > last) return s;
      const values = [...s.values];
      const hinted = [...s.hinted];
      values[a.index] = a.letter;
      hinted[a.index] = true;
      return { values, hinted, active: a.index };
    }
    case "reset":
      return initCloze(a.count);
  }
}

export const allFilled = (s: ClozeState) => s.values.every((v) => v.length > 0);

export function interpretInput(prev: string, next: string): ClozeAction[] {
  if (!next.startsWith(SENTINEL)) {
    const text = next.replace(SENTINEL, "");
    return text ? splitWords(text) : prev === SENTINEL ? [{ type: "backspaceEmpty" }] : [{ type: "set", value: "" }];
  }
  return splitWords(next.slice(SENTINEL.length));
}

function splitWords(text: string): ClozeAction[] {
  const parts = text.split(" ");
  const out: ClozeAction[] = [{ type: "set", value: parts[0] }];
  for (const part of parts.slice(1)) {
    out.push({ type: "space" });
    if (part) out.push({ type: "set", value: part });
  }
  return out;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `use-cloze-input.ts`:

```ts
import { type Dispatch, useCallback, useRef } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { type ClozeAction, type ClozeState, interpretInput, SENTINEL } from "./cloze-engine";

/** 一个视觉隐藏的 <input> 管理全部空(spec §11.5)。值 = 哨兵 + 当前空内容;只在 input 事件里比较差异。 */
export function useClozeInput({ state, dispatch, onSubmit }: { state: ClozeState; dispatch: Dispatch<ClozeAction>; onSubmit: () => void }) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const composing = useRef(false);
  const value = SENTINEL + (state.values[state.active] ?? "");
  const apply = useCallback((next: string) => {
    for (const action of interpretInput(value, next)) dispatch(action);
  }, [dispatch, value]);
  const focusBlank = useCallback((i: number) => {
    dispatch({ type: "focus", index: i });
    inputRef.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true }); // 必须在点击手势里调用,iOS 才弹键盘
  }, [dispatch]);
  return {
    inputRef,
    focusBlank,
    inputProps: {
      value,
      onChange: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => { if (!composing.current) apply(e.target.value); },
      onCompositionStart: () => { composing.current = true; },
      onCompositionEnd: (e: React.CompositionEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => { composing.current = false; apply(e.currentTarget.value); },
      onKeyDown: (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => { if (e.key === "Enter") { e.preventDefault(); onSubmit(); } },
      autoComplete: "off", autoCorrect: "off", autoCapitalize: "none", spellCheck: false,
      enterKeyHint: "done" as const, inputMode: "text" as const,
      "aria-label": t("web.wordbookSpell.input", { n: state.active + 1, total: state.values.length }),
      className: "sr-only-focusable",
    },
  };
}
```

(`sr-only-focusable`:`position:fixed; left:0; bottom:0; width:1px; height:1px; opacity:0;`——不要 `display:none`/`visibility:hidden`,否则移动端不弹键盘;字号 16px 防止 iOS 聚焦缩放。加到 `web/src/index.css` 的 utilities 层,本任务独占这几行。)

`cloze-board.tsx`:按 `set.lines` 渲染编号句子;`Segment.t === "text"` 原样输出;`"blank"` 渲染 `<span role="textbox" aria-readonly="true" aria-label=… onPointerDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); onFocusBlank(seg.i); }}>`,内容为 `state.values[seg.i]`,样式:最小宽度 3.2em、底部 2px 下划线(当前空 = 蓝色 + `aria-current`,已填 = ink,未填 = hair)、44px 行高命中;`showLength` 时用 `len` 个 0.62em 的格子底线;多词词头的连续空之间保留一个空格;`showZh` 时每句下方显示译文(`text-ink-2`);`results` 存在时每个空后面加结果徽标(图标 + 文字),颜色按 spec §11.3。`prefs.ts` 用 `readJson/writeJson(WORDBOOK_PREFS_KEY)`,try/catch 包裹。

```tsx
import { fireEvent, screen, waitFor, within } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiClient } from "~/api/client";
import i18n from "~/i18n";
import { createFetchMock, json } from "~/test/fetch-mock";
import { renderWithProviders } from "~/test/render";
import { WordbookSpellPage } from "./spell-page";

vi.mock("~/features/chat", () => ({
  useTtsPlayer: () => ({ states: {}, play: vi.fn(), playQueue: vi.fn(), stop: vi.fn(), prime: vi.fn() }),
  ttsErrorMessage: () => "tts error",
}));

const SET = { set_id: "abc123abc123", lines: [
  { key: "talk", sentence_id: "1".repeat(16), zh: "经过一次长谈后,我们决定离婚。", segments: [{ t: "text", v: "After a long " }, { t: "blank", i: 0, len: 4 }, { t: "text", v: ", we decided on divorce." }] },
  { key: "used to", sentence_id: "2".repeat(16), zh: "我以前住在海边的一个小镇。", segments: [{ t: "text", v: "I " }, { t: "blank", i: 1, len: 4 }, { t: "text", v: " " }, { t: "blank", i: 2, len: 2 }, { t: "text", v: " live in a small town by the sea." }] },
] };
const CARD = (word: string) => ({ key: word, word, us: "", uk: "", senses: [{ pos: "v", text: "说话" }], phrases: [], examples: [] });
const RESULT = { credit: 75, counted: true, words: [
  { key: "talk", outcome: "typo", credit: 50, item: null, card: CARD("talk"), blanks: [{ i: 0, outcome: "typo", credit: 50, typed: "tlak", expected: "talk",
    diff: [{ op: "eq", a: "t", b: "t" }, { op: "sub", a: "a", b: "l" }, { op: "sub", a: "l", b: "a" }, { op: "eq", a: "k", b: "k" }] }] },
  { key: "used to", outcome: "correct", credit: 100, item: null, card: CARD("used to"), blanks: [
    { i: 1, outcome: "correct", credit: 100, typed: "used", expected: "used", diff: [] },
    { i: 2, outcome: "correct", credit: 100, typed: "to", expected: "to", diff: [] }] },
] };

beforeEach(() => { localStorage.setItem("enjoy-web.settings", JSON.stringify({ language: "zh-CN" })); void i18n.changeLanguage("zh-CN"); });
afterEach(() => { localStorage.removeItem("enjoy-web.settings"); void i18n.changeLanguage("en"); });

describe("WordbookSpellPage", () => {
  it("types with space to advance, submits with Enter and shows four-outcome results", async () => {
    const mock = createFetchMock({
      "POST /api/wordbook/spell/sets": () => json({ book_id: "junior", sets: [SET] }),
      "POST /api/wordbook/spell/submit": () => json(RESULT),
      "POST /api/wordbook/explain": () => json({ error: "AI explanation unavailable" }, 503),
    });
    renderWithProviders(<WordbookSpellPage />, { api: new ApiClient({ getToken: () => "t", fetchImpl: mock.fetch }) });
    const input = await screen.findByLabelText("第 1 空,共 3 空");
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "​tlak " } });
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "​used " } });
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "​to" } });
    fireEvent.keyDown(input, { key: "Enter" });
    await waitFor(() => expect(mock.calls.some((c) => c.url.pathname === "/api/wordbook/spell/submit")).toBe(true));
    const body = JSON.parse(String(mock.calls.find((c) => c.url.pathname.endsWith("/submit"))!.body));
    expect(body.lines).toEqual([
      { key: "talk", sentence_id: "1".repeat(16), answers: [{ typed: "tlak", hinted: false }] },
      { key: "used to", sentence_id: "2".repeat(16), answers: [{ typed: "used", hinted: false }, { typed: "to", hinted: false }] },
    ]);
    const talk = await screen.findByTestId("spell-result-talk");
    expect(within(talk).getByText("小笔误")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(within(talk).getByLabelText(/正确拼写 talk/)).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole("status").textContent).toContain("75");
    fireEvent.click(within(talk).getByRole("button", { name: "为什么?" }));
    expect(await screen.findByText("AI 讲解暂不可用")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("first-letter hint marks the blank as hinted", async () => {
    const mock = createFetchMock({ "POST /api/wordbook/spell/sets": () => json({ book_id: "junior", sets: [SET] }),
      "POST /api/wordbook/spell/submit": () => json(RESULT) });
    renderWithProviders(<WordbookSpellPage />, { api: new ApiClient({ getToken: () => "t", fetchImpl: mock.fetch }) });
    fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole("button", { name: "首字母" }));
    const input = screen.getByLabelText("第 1 空,共 3 空");
    expect((input as HTMLInputElement).value).toBe("​t");
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "​talk " } });
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "​used " } });
    fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: "​to" } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "提交" }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(mock.calls.some((c) => c.url.pathname.endsWith("/submit"))).toBe(true));
    const body = JSON.parse(String(mock.calls.find((c) => c.url.pathname.endsWith("/submit"))!.body));
    expect(body.lines[0].answers[0]).toEqual({ typed: "talk", hinted: true });
  });

  it("explains how to get words when there are no sets", async () => {
    const mock = createFetchMock({ "POST /api/wordbook/spell/sets": () => json({ book_id: "junior", sets: [] }) });
    renderWithProviders(<WordbookSpellPage />, { api: new ApiClient({ getToken: () => "t", fetchImpl: mock.fetch }) });
    expect(await screen.findByText("先学几个新词再来拼写")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `spell-page.tsx`:`PageHeader`(标题「按句拼写」,右侧设置图标 → 底部表单「显示字母数」开关,写 `prefs`);`spellSets(api, { count: 2, size: 5 })` → 当前组 `sets[i]`;`useReducer(clozeReducer, initCloze(blankCount))`;顶部组进度「第 1 组 / 共 2 组」;卡片里 `ClozeBoard`;工具行(32px 视觉 / 44px 命中):「首字母」(对当前空 `dispatch({type:"hint", index, letter})`,首字母取自该空所在行的 key 对应词——服务端不下发答案,首字母从 `line.key` 的对应词取:`line.key.split(" ")[blankOffset][0]`)、「听这句」(`tts.play` 当前句的完整文本 = 文本段 + key 对应词)、「看译文」切换 `showZh`;底部固定主按钮「提交」(未全部填写时仍可提交,空的空按错误判);隐藏输入 `<input ref={inputRef} {...inputProps} />`;提交 → `submitSpell` → `SpellResults`(每词一张卡 `data-testid="spell-result-<key>"`:结果徽标、你的输入/`LetterDiff`、正确拼写 + 朗读、词性与释义、例句、错误类型说明文本 `web.wordbookSpell.why.<outcome>`、「为什么?」按钮 → `ExplainSheet`、「举报例句」→ `reportSentence`)+ `role="status" aria-live="polite"` 一次性播报「本组得分 75」;「下一组」/「完成」→ 小结(复用 `../session-summary`)。`ExplainSheet`:`BottomSheet`,打开时 `explainWord(api, key)`,加载中骨架;503 → 「AI 讲解暂不可用」+ 仍显示数据讲解;成功 → 纯文本段落(`whitespace-pre-wrap`,不渲染 HTML)。
i18n(`web.wordbookSpell.*`):`title`、`input`「第 {{n}} 空,共 {{total}} 空」、`hint`「首字母」、`listen`「听这句」、`showZh`「看译文」、`submit`「提交」、`score`「本组得分 {{n}}」、`outcome.correct|typo|inflection|wrong`「正确|小笔误|词形不对|错误」、`why.typo`「拼写接近,注意标出的字母」、`why.inflection`「词选对了,但词形不对:这里应为 {{expected}}」、`why.wrong`「再记一下正确拼写」、`whyButton`「为什么?」、`aiUnavailable`「AI 讲解暂不可用」、`report`「举报例句」、`empty`「先学几个新词再来拼写」、`showLength`「显示字母数」、`fromDictionary`「本组为词典例句」;英文同键。

### Task P2-1: full 级精简派生文件(同近义 / 同根 / 英英释义 / 真题数)

**Files:**
- Modify: `server/scripts/wordbook_data.py`(加 `derive` 子命令)、`server/dictionaries/kylebing-english-vocabulary/SOURCE.json`(加 `extras` 数组:每本 `{"id","file":"extras/<id>.extra.jsonl","upstream_path":"full_line_jsonl/full/正序/<书名>.jsonl","upstream_sha256","bytes","sha256"}`)、`server/app/wordbook/service.py`(`card_out` 加 `extras`)、`server/app/wordbook/quiz.py`(干扰项排除同近义词)、`web/src/features/wordbook/{wordbook-api.ts,word-card.tsx}`
- Create: `server/dictionaries/kylebing-english-vocabulary/extras/<7 个 id>.extra.jsonl`(由脚本生成)、`server/app/wordbook/extras.py`、`server/tests/test_wordbook_extras.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:`derive(full_dir: Path, out_dir: Path) -> dict[str, int]`(每本写出的字节数);派生行 `{"w": key, "syn": [str ≤ 8], "rel": [[pos, word, gloss ≤ 20 字] ≤ 10], "def": str ≤ 160, "exam": int}`;`Extras(syn: tuple[str, ...], rel: tuple[tuple[str, str, str], ...], definition: str, exam: int)`;`ExtrasStore(data_dir)`,`async get(book_id, key) -> Extras | None`(同 `DictionaryStore` 的懒加载 + 行偏移,文件缺失返回 None);`CardOut.extras: {syn, rel, def, exam} | null`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

```python
import json

from app.wordbook.extras import ExtrasStore
from tests.wordbook_helpers import make_data_dir

FULL_ROW = {"wordRank": 1, "headWord": "talk", "content": {"word": {"wordHead": "talk", "content": {
    "syno": {"synos": [{"pos": "v", "tran": "说", "hwds": [{"w": "speak"}, {"w": "chat"}, {"w": "speak"}]}]},
    "relWord": {"rels": [{"pos": "n", "words": [{"hwd": "talker", "tran": " 健谈的人；说话者；空谈家；讲话的人"}]}]},
    "trans": [{"tranCn": "说话", "pos": "v", "tranOther": "When you talk, you say things to someone."}],
    "exam": [{"question": "q"}]}}}, "bookId": "CET4_1"}


def test_derive_trims_and_dedupes(tmp_path):
    import importlib.util
    from pathlib import Path
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("wd", Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "scripts" / "wordbook_data.py")
    wd = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(wd)
    full = tmp_path / "full"
    full.mkdir()
    (full / "junior.jsonl").write_text(json.dumps(FULL_ROW, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n" + json.dumps(FULL_ROW, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    sizes = wd.derive(full, tmp_path / "out")
    row = json.loads((tmp_path / "out" / "junior.extra.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()[0])
    assert row == {"w": "talk", "syn": ["speak", "chat"], "rel": [["n", "talker", "健谈的人；说话者；空谈家；讲话的人"[:20]]],
                   "def": "When you talk, you say things to someone.", "exam": 2}
    assert sizes["junior"] == (tmp_path / "out" / "junior.extra.jsonl").stat().st_size


async def test_extras_store_reads_lazily(tmp_path):
    data = make_data_dir(tmp_path)
    (data / "extras").mkdir()
    (data / "extras" / "junior.extra.jsonl").write_text(
        '{"w":"talk","syn":["speak"],"rel":[],"def":"d","exam":0}\n', encoding="utf-8")
    store = ExtrasStore(data)
    assert (await store.get("junior", "talk")).syn == ("speak",)
    assert await store.get("junior", "nope") is None and await store.get("toefl", "talk") is None
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 跑测试确认失败** — Run: `cd server && uv run pytest -q tests/test_wordbook_extras.py` → FAIL

- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `derive`:逐行读 full 级 JSONL,键 = `normalize_word(headWord)`(丢弃规则同 `entry.headword_key`,脚本里复制同样的 `DENYLIST` 与缩写集合并在测试里断言与 `books.py` 一致),同键累加:`syn` 去重保序截 8、`rel` 按词去重截 10(释义去空白截 20 字)、`def` 取第一条非空 `tranOther` 截 160、`exam` 累加 `len(exam)`;按首次出现顺序写出,`ensure_ascii=False`、LF。子命令:`uv run python scripts/wordbook_data.py derive --full DIR`,`DIR` 由 `download --level full --dest <临时目录>`(同一函数,换 `upstream_path` 前缀)下载;full 级原始文件**不入库**。`extras.py` 的 `ExtrasStore` 复用 `index.py` 的模式(键 → 偏移,`asyncio.to_thread`,LRU 1024)。`card_out(card, extras)` 带 `extras`;`quiz.pick_distractors` 增加可选参数 `exclude: frozenset[str] = frozenset()`(同近义词键),`service` 传入。网页 `Card.extras` 类型与词卡「同近义 / 同根 / 英英释义 / 真题 n 次」折叠区。
- [ ] **Step 4: 生成并校验** — Run: `uv run python scripts/wordbook_data.py download --level full --dest <scratch>`、`... derive --full <scratch>`;输出七个文件,合计约 10.6 MB(spec §2.9 实测 10,641,725 字节,最大 toefl ≈ 3.0 MB);更新 `SOURCE.json` 的 `extras` 校验和;`test_wordbook_data_files.py` 加 extras 校验用例。
- [ ] **Step 5: 跑测试并提交** — Run: `cd server && uv run pytest -q tests/test_wordbook_*.py tests/test_wordbook_data_files.py`;`yarn web:typecheck && cd web && yarn vitest run src/features/wordbook` → PASS

```bash

git commit -m "feat(wordbook): derived synonyms, related words and definitions from the full-level data
```
