### Task P2-2: AI 生成短文(带回落)

**Files:**
- Create: `server/alembic/versions/0013_wordbook_passages.py`、`server/app/wordbook/passage.py`、`server/tests/test_wordbook_passage.py`
- Modify: `server/app/models.py`(`WordbookPassage`)、`server/app/core/{config,settings_registry,quotas}.py`、`server/app/services/prompts.py`、`server/app/admin/prompts_admin.py`、`server/app/wordbook/{cloze,service}.py`、`server/app/routers/wordbook.py`、`server/tests/test_migrations.py`、`web/src/features/wordbook/spell/{spell-page.tsx,cloze-board.tsx,i18n/*}`、`web/src/features/wordbook/wordbook-api.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:表 `wordbook_passages(id PK, cache_key String(64) unique, book_id, level String(4), words JSON, english Text, zh Text, targets JSON, status String(8) ok|bad, reports Integer, prompt_version Integer?, created_at)`;设置 `wordbook_passages_global_daily: int = 300`、`wordbook_passages_daily_per_user: int = 10`(配额键);提示词 `wordbook.passage`(占位符 `{level}`、`{words}`,边界 `<words>`…`</words>`);`passage.validate(payload: dict, words: list[str]) -> list[str]`(错误码 `json`、`length`、`missing:<word>`、`duplicate:<word>`、`zh`);`passage.to_cloze(row) -> ClozeSet`(`set_id` = `p:<id>`,一个 `ClozeLine`,每个目标词在其位置挖空);`passage.passage_set(session, http, settings, book_id, words, user_id, now) -> ClozeSet | None`(None = 回落);`ClozeSet` 增加 `source: Literal["dataset","ai"] = "dataset"`;`/spell/submit` 接受 `set_id` 以 `p:` 开头的提交,按缓存的 `targets` 判分;`POST /reports` 的 `kind="passage"`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

```python
import json

import httpx
import respx

from app.wordbook import passage

GOOD = {"english": "We had a long talk about the plan. They decided to abandon it and accept a new idea.",
        "zh": "我们就计划长谈了一次。他们决定放弃它,接受一个新想法。",
        "targets": [{"word": "talk", "form": "talk"}, {"word": "abandon", "form": "abandon"}, {"word": "accept", "form": "accept"}],
        "difficulty": "A2"}


def test_validate_accepts_inflected_targets_and_rejects_missing():
    assert passage.validate(GOOD, ["talk", "abandon", "accept"]) == []
    inflected = {**GOOD, "english": GOOD["english"].replace("long talk", "long talking session"),
                 "targets": [{"word": "talk", "form": "talking"}] + GOOD["targets"][1:]}
    assert passage.validate(inflected, ["talk", "abandon", "accept"]) == []
    missing = {**GOOD, "english": "We had a long chat. They decided to abandon it and accept a new idea."}
    assert "missing:talk" in passage.validate(missing, ["talk", "abandon", "accept"])
    assert passage.validate({"english": "x"}, ["talk"])[0] in ("json", "zh", "length")


@respx.mock
async def test_bad_output_retries_once_then_falls_back(app):
    route = respx.post("https://llm.test/v1/chat/completions").mock(return_value=httpx.Response(
        200, json={"choices": [{"message": {"content": "not json"}}]}))
    app.state.settings = app.state.settings.model_copy(update={"wordbook_enabled": True, "wordbook_ai_enabled": True})
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as s:
        from app.models import User
        s.add(User(id=7, username="lily", password_hash="x"))
        await s.commit()
        result = await passage.passage_set(s, app.state.http, app.state.settings, "junior", ["talk", "abandon", "accept"], 7, None)
    assert result is None and route.call_count == 2


@respx.mock
async def test_402_falls_back_without_retry_and_caches_success(app):
    route = respx.post("https://llm.test/v1/chat/completions").mock(return_value=httpx.Response(402))
    app.state.settings = app.state.settings.model_copy(update={"wordbook_enabled": True, "wordbook_ai_enabled": True})
    async with app.state.sessionmaker() as s:
        from app.models import User
        s.add(User(id=7, username="lily", password_hash="x"))
        await s.commit()
        assert await passage.passage_set(s, app.state.http, app.state.settings, "junior", ["talk", "abandon", "accept"], 7, None) is None
        assert route.call_count == 1
        route.mock(return_value=httpx.Response(200, json={"choices": [{"message": {"content": json.dumps(GOOD)}}]}))
        first = await passage.passage_set(s, app.state.http, app.state.settings, "junior", ["accept", "talk", "abandon"], 7, None)
        again = await passage.passage_set(s, app.state.http, app.state.settings, "junior", ["talk", "abandon", "accept"], 7, None)
    assert first.source == "ai" and again.set_id == first.set_id and route.call_count == 2
    blanks = [seg for seg in first.lines[0].segments if seg.t == "blank"]
    assert len(blanks) == 3
```

(`now=None` 时函数内部取 `utcnow()`。)

- [ ] **Step 2: 跑测试确认失败** — Run: `cd server && uv run pytest -q tests/test_wordbook_passage.py tests/test_migrations.py` → FAIL
- [ ] **Step 3: 实现** — `passage.py`:`DEFAULT_PASSAGE_TEMPLATE`(要求只输出 JSON,字段如 spec §12.1,难度按书的 `cefr`,60–120 词,目标词各恰好出现一次);`cache_key = sha256("|".join(sorted(words)) + "|" + level + "|" + str(prompt_version))`;查缓存(`status="ok"`)→ 命中直接 `to_cloze`;否则检查 `wordbook_ai_available` 与两级额度(`usage.consume` 范围 `wordbook_passage_user` / `wordbook_passage_global`),调用 `llm.complete`(`max_tokens=600`、`temperature=0.7`);`model_json` 解析(复用 `json.loads`,去掉 ```` ``` ```` 包裹);`validate` 失败 → 再调一次;仍失败或 `LlmUnavailable` → 退还额度、`return None`(402 不重试);成功 → 插入行(并发唯一键冲突 → 读已有行)。`validate` 用 `textnorm.find_form` 定位每个目标,`form` 必须 `same_lemma(form, word)`,同一词出现多次 → `duplicate`。`to_cloze`:按目标在原文中的位置切分成 `Segment`,`expected` = 各 `form`,`lemmas` = 各 `word`。`service.spell_sets`:当 `wordbook_ai_available` 时先对第一组的目标词调用 `passage_set`,返回 None 就用数据集句组(spec R-W8);`set_out` 带 `source`。`spell_submit`:`set_id` 以 `p:` 开头 → 读 `WordbookPassage` 行复原(行不存在或 `status="bad"` → 422 `Sentence changed`)。举报 `kind="passage"` 时 `sentence_id` = `p:<id>` 的 16 位哈希;累计 3 次 → `status="bad"`。网页:`ClozeSet.source`;`source === "dataset"` 且 AI 已开启时组标题下显示「本组为词典例句」;AI 组显示整段英文与「看译文」整段中文;「举报这段」按钮。迁移 `0013`(`down_revision="0012"`,开工时跑 `alembic heads` 确认)+ 往返测试。

```python
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone

from app.wordbook.stats import forecast, heatmap


def test_heatmap_fills_missing_days():
    assert heatmap([("2026-10-09", 12)], date(2026, 10, 8), 3) == [
        {"day": "2026-10-08", "answers": 0}, {"day": "2026-10-09", "answers": 12}, {"day": "2026-10-10", "answers": 0}]


def test_forecast_buckets_by_local_day():
    dues = [datetime(2026, 10, 10, 17, tzinfo=timezone.utc), datetime(2026, 10, 11, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)]
    assert forecast(dues, 480, date(2026, 10, 11), 2) == [{"day": "2026-10-11", "due": 2}, {"day": "2026-10-12", "due": 0}]
```

### Task P3-2: 摸底测试(按词书级别估算)

**Files:**
- Create: `server/app/wordbook/placement.py`、`server/app/wordbook/pseudowords.py`(20 个内置伪词,如 `flinder`、`bastropic`)、`server/tests/test_wordbook_placement.py`、`web/src/features/wordbook/{placement-page.tsx,placement-page.test.tsx}`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:`GET /api/wordbook/placement` → `{items: [{key, word, kind: "yes_no"}], checks: [QuestionOut]}`(每级从「只在该级及以上的书里出现」的词里抽 8 个 + 5 个伪词;抽查题 = 每级 2 道 `meaning`);`POST /api/wordbook/placement {answers: [{key, known}], checks: [{key, choice}]}` → `{suggested_book, estimate: {low, high}, note}`;`estimate_level(known_by_level: dict[int, float], false_alarm: float) -> int`(纯函数:校正后认识率 = `(p − fa) / (1 − fa)`,`fa` 为伪词选「认识」的比例;从 1 级起取连续校正后 ≥ 0.6 的最高级别,没有则 0);`suggest_book(level) -> str`(`BOOKS` 中第一本 `level > 给定级别` 的书,超出取 `sat`)

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试**

```python
from app.wordbook.placement import estimate_level, suggest_book


def test_false_alarms_lower_the_estimate():
    assert estimate_level({1: 0.95, 2: 0.9, 3: 0.7, 4: 0.4}, false_alarm=0.0) == 3
    assert estimate_level({1: 0.95, 2: 0.9, 3: 0.7, 4: 0.4}, false_alarm=0.4) == 2


def test_suggested_book_is_one_level_above():
    assert suggest_book(2) == "cet4" and suggest_book(6) == "sat" and suggest_book(0) == "junior"
```

## 验收标准(第一阶段)

1. `WORDBOOK_ENABLED=false`:`/api/wordbook/config` → `{"enabled": false}`,其余 `/api/wordbook/*` 404;练习页与「我的」不显示入口;「致谢与许可」页仍可访问。
2. 打开后:练习页出现「背单词」大卡;「我的 → 学习」有「背单词」行;底部导航仍为 3 或 4 项。
3. 选「四级」(默认每日 20 新词、复习上限 100、乱序)→ 首页显示今日计划、已掌握/已学/剩余;同一用户已有的词(查词加入)计为已学且不重复建行。
4. 学新词:5 词一轮,词卡有音标、释义、例句、朗读;小测选释义;答错回队尾;建行 `source='wordbook:cet4'`、盒 0、明天到期;「我认识」进盒 2、4 天后。
5. 复习:到期词按五种题型出题,干扰项来自同一本书且不与正确释义重叠;第一次作答写盒子,同轮再次作答只记练习;未到期答错回盒 0。
6. 按句拼写:3–6 句一组,只挖目标词,多词词头为连续空,默认不显示长度(可开),空格跳空、空的空上退格回跳、回车提交,单一隐藏输入且关闭自动纠错/大写/拼写检查;四种结果(正确/小笔误/词形不对/错误)带逐字母差异与讲解;首字母提示得分减半;组均分 ≥ 60 计一次 `spelling_set`。
7. AI 讲解:子开关关或上游 402 时返回 503 并显示「AI 讲解暂不可用」,数据讲解照常;成功后全站缓存,第二次不调用上游;额度按每人与全站计。
8. 段位:学新词与完成拼写组产生 `word_learned`/`spelling_set` 点数且计入每日上限;复习照常产生 `vocab_review`;四个新成就可解锁;含 `words_learned` 阶段练习的规则在背单词关闭时不能发布。
9. 管理端:设置页「背单词」分组可启停每本书与改默认值(审计);词书页显示文件校验、索引、学习统计、举报;重新加载与屏蔽例句入审计;用量页出现「背单词」功能;诊断能报出数据缺失。
10. 数据:七个文件 sha256 与清单一致;镜像内有 `/app/dictionaries/kylebing-english-vocabulary/`;全部书加载后进程额外内存 < 12 MiB(spec §2.10)。
11. 全量测试、类型检查、构建、架构测试绿色;日志中无用户拼写输入与 AI 正文(caplog 断言);浅色与深色视觉对照完成;真机未验证项已在 `e2e-results.md` 标注。

## 规格覆盖自查(spec → task)

|---|---|
| §2 数据调研、§3 文件布局 / gitattributes / Docker / NOTICE / 脚本 | F1(数据与脚本)、D3 Step 5(真实数据冒烟)、WC1(致谢页)、Z2 Step 3(镜像) |
| §2.11 清洗与合并 | D1、D2 |
| §4 运行时索引、LRU、重新加载、屏蔽句 | D3、S1(`load_hidden`)、A1(reload / hide) |
| §5 数据模型与迁移 0012、`scope`、配额只计非词书行 | F3、S1 |
| §6.1 盒子映射、R-W5 | S3、S4 |
| §6.2 每日计划、积压保护、乱序/书序、明日预测 | S1、S2、WC2 |
